CHEMIN : C:\Users\hp\Downloads\ISLAMCRAFT V2.0\client\main.js

import { Game } from "./src/core/Game.js";

const loadingScreen =
    document.getElementById("loading-screen");

const loadingMessage =
    document.getElementById("loading-message");

const loadingProgress =
    document.getElementById("loading-progress");

function setLoading(
    message,
    progress = null
) {

    if (loadingMessage) {

        loadingMessage.textContent =
            message;

    }

    if (
        loadingProgress &&
        progress !== null
    ) {

        const value =
            Math.max(
                0,
                Math.min(
                    100,
                    Number(progress)
                )
            );

        loadingProgress.style.width =
            `${value}%`;

        loadingProgress.setAttribute(
            "aria-valuenow",
            String(value)
        );

    }

}

function hideLoading() {

    if (!loadingScreen) {

        return;

    }

    loadingScreen.classList.add(
        "loading-screen-hidden"
    );

    setTimeout(
        () => {

            loadingScreen.hidden =
                true;

        },
        350
    );

}

function showError(error) {

    console.error(
        "Erreur pendant le démarrage d'ISLAMCRAFT :",
        error
    );

    if (loadingMessage) {

        loadingMessage.textContent =
            "Impossible de charger ISLAMCRAFT.";

    }

    if (loadingScreen) {

        loadingScreen.classList.remove(
            "loading-screen-hidden"
        );

    }

    const existing =
        document.getElementById(
            "startup-error"
        );

    if (existing) {

        existing.remove();

    }

    const errorElement =
        document.createElement(
            "p"
        );

    errorElement.id =
        "startup-error";

    errorElement.className =
        "startup-error";

    errorElement.textContent =
        error?.message ??
        "Une erreur inconnue est survenue.";

    loadingScreen
        ?.querySelector(
            ".loading-content"
        )
        ?.appendChild(
            errorElement
        );

}

async function bootstrap() {

    try {

        setLoading(
            "Initialisation du moteur...",
            10
        );

        const game =
            new Game();

        setLoading(
            "Chargement de la scène...",
            30
        );

        await game.initialize();

        setLoading(
            "Démarrage du jeu...",
            80
        );

        game.start();

        window.ISLAMCRAFT =
            game;

        setLoading(
            "ISLAMCRAFT prêt !",
            100
        );

        hideLoading();

        console.log(
            "ISLAMCRAFT client prêt."
        );

    } catch (error) {

        showError(
            error
        );

    }

}

window.addEventListener(
    "DOMContentLoaded",
    bootstrap,
    {
        once: true
    }
);

CHEMIN : C:\Users\hp\Downloads\ISLAMCRAFT V2.0\client\src\assets\AnimationLoader.js 
========================================== 
export class AnimationLoader {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.animations = new Map();

        this.loading = new Map();

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("AnimationLoader initialisé.");

    }

    async load(key, loader) {

        if (this.animations.has(key)) {

            return this.animations.get(key);

        }

        if (this.loading.has(key)) {

            return this.loading.get(key);

        }

        if (typeof loader !== "function") {

            throw new TypeError(

                `Loader invalide pour l'animation "${key}".`

            );

        }

        const promise = Promise.resolve()

            .then(() => loader())

            .then(animation => {

                this.animations.set(

                    key,

                    animation

                );

                this.loading.delete(key);

                return animation;

            })

            .catch(error => {

                this.loading.delete(key);

                throw error;

            });

        this.loading.set(key, promise);

        return promise;

    }

    has(key) {

        return this.animations.has(key);

    }

    get(key) {

        return this.animations.get(key) ?? null;

    }

    remove(key) {

        this.animations.delete(key);

    }

    clear() {

        this.animations.clear();

        this.loading.clear();

    }

    getSize() {

        return this.animations.size;

    }

}

export class AssetManager {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.assets = new Map();

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("AssetManager initialisé.");

    }

    register(name, asset) {

        if (!name) {

            return;

        }

        this.assets.set(name, asset);

    }

    has(name) {

        return this.assets.has(name);

    }

    get(name) {

        return this.assets.get(name) ?? null;

    }

    remove(name) {

        this.assets.delete(name);

    }

    clear() {

        this.assets.clear();

    }

    getSize() {

        return this.assets.size;

    }

    getAll() {

        return [...this.assets.entries()];

    }

}

export class TextureLoader {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.textures = new Map();

        this.loading = new Map();

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("TextureLoader initialisé.");

    }

    async load(key, loader) {

        if (this.textures.has(key)) {

            return this.textures.get(key);

        if (this.loading.has(key)) {

            return this.loading.get(key);

        }

        if (typeof loader !== "function") {

            throw new TypeError(

                `Loader invalide pour la texture "${key}".`

            );

                this.textures.set(key, texture);

        return this.textures.has(key);

CHEMIN : C:\Users\hp\Downloads\ISLAMCRAFT V2.0\client\src\audio\AudioManager.js 
========================================== 
export class AudioManager {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.context = null;

        this.masterVolume = 1;

        this.musicVolume = 1;

        this.sfxVolume = 1;

        this.muted = false;

        this.initialized = false;

    }

    async initialize() {

        if (

            typeof window !== "undefined" &&

            window.AudioContext

        ) {

            this.context = new AudioContext();

        }

        this.initialized = true;

        console.log("AudioManager initialisé.");

    }

    async resume() {

        if (

            this.context &&

            this.context.state === "suspended"

        ) {

            await this.context.resume();

        }

    }

    setMasterVolume(volume) {

        this.masterVolume = Math.max(

            0,

            Math.min(

                1,

                Number(volume) || 0

            )

        );

    }

    setMusicVolume(volume) {

        this.musicVolume = Math.max(

            0,

            Math.min(

                1,

                Number(volume) || 0

            )

        );

    }

    setSfxVolume(volume) {

        this.sfxVolume = Math.max(

            0,

            Math.min(

                1,

                Number(volume) || 0

            )

        );

    }

    setMuted(muted) {

        this.muted = Boolean(muted);

    }

    toggleMute() {

        this.muted = !this.muted;

        return this.muted;

    }

    getMasterVolume() {

        return this.masterVolume;

    }

    getMusicVolume() {

        return this.musicVolume;

    }

    getSfxVolume() {

        return this.sfxVolume;

    }

    isMuted() {

        return this.muted;

    }

    getEffectiveVolume(volume) {

        if (this.muted) {

            return 0;

        }

        return volume * this.masterVolume;

    }

    async destroy() {

        if (this.context) {

            await this.context.close();

            this.context = null;

        }

        this.initialized = false;

    }

}

export class ChestSounds {

        this.enabled = true;

        console.log("ChestSounds initialisé.");

    }

    playOpen() {

        if (!this.enabled) {

            return;

        }

        this.game.audioManager?.playSFX?.(

            "chest_open"

        );

    }

    playClose() {

        if (!this.enabled) {

            return;

        }

        this.game.audioManager?.playSFX?.(

            "chest_close"

        );

    }

    playReward() {

        if (!this.enabled) {

            return;

        }

        this.game.audioManager?.playSFX?.(

            "chest_reward"

        );

    }

    setEnabled(enabled) {

        this.enabled = Boolean(enabled);

    }

    isEnabled() {

        return this.enabled;

    }

}

CHEMIN : C:\Users\hp\Downloads\ISLAMCRAFT V2.0\client\src\audio\MusicManager.js 
========================================== 
export class MusicManager {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.currentTrack = null;

        this.playing = false;

        this.volume = 1;

        this.enabled = true;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("MusicManager initialisé.");

    }

    play(track) {

        if (!this.enabled || !track) {

            return;

        }

        this.currentTrack = track;

        this.playing = true;

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "audio:music-play",

            track

        );

    }

    stop() {

        if (!this.playing) {

            return;

        }

        this.playing = false;

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "audio:music-stop",

            this.currentTrack

        );

        this.currentTrack = null;

    }

    pause() {

        if (!this.playing) {

            return;

        }

        this.playing = false;

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "audio:music-pause",

            this.currentTrack

        );

    }

    resume() {

        if (!this.currentTrack) {

            return;

        }

        this.playing = true;

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "audio:music-resume",

            this.currentTrack

        );

    }

    setVolume(volume) {

        this.volume = Math.max(

            0,

            Math.min(

                1,

                Number(volume) || 0

            )

        );

    }

    setEnabled(enabled) {

        this.enabled = Boolean(enabled);

    }

    getCurrentTrack() {

        return this.currentTrack;

    }

    getVolume() {

        return this.volume;

    }

    isPlaying() {

        return this.playing;

    }

    isEnabled() {

        return this.enabled;

    }

}

                sound,

            }

CHEMIN : C:\Users\hp\Downloads\ISLAMCRAFT V2.0\client\src\camera\CameraCollision.js 
========================================== 
export class CameraCollision {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.enabled = true;

        this.minimumDistance = 0.5;

        this.collisionDistance = 0;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("CameraCollision initialisé.");

    }

    setEnabled(enabled) {

        this.enabled = Boolean(enabled);

    }

    isEnabled() {

        return this.enabled;

    }

    setMinimumDistance(distance) {

        this.minimumDistance = Math.max(

            0,

            Number(distance) || 0

        );

    }

    getMinimumDistance() {

        return this.minimumDistance;

    }

    resolve(position, target) {

        if (!this.enabled || !position || !target) {

            return position;

        }

        const dx = position.x - target.x;

        const dy = position.y - target.y;

        const dz = position.z - target.z;

        const distance = Math.sqrt(

            dx * dx +

            dy * dy +

            dz * dz

        );

        this.collisionDistance = distance;

        if (distance <= this.minimumDistance) {

            return {

                x: target.x,

                y: target.y,

                z: target.z

            };

        }

        return {

            x: position.x,

            y: position.y,

            z: position.z

        };

    }

    getCollisionDistance() {

        return this.collisionDistance;

    }

} 
.

export class CameraController {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.camera = null;

        this.target = null;

        this.orbit = null;

        this.enabled = true;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.camera =
            this.game.scene?.getCamera?.() ?? null;

        this.target =
            this.game.localPlayer ?? null;

        this.orbit =
            this.game.cameraOrbit ?? null;

        console.log(
            "CameraController initialisé."
        );

    }

    setCamera(camera) {

        this.camera = camera;

    }

    setTarget(target) {

        this.target = target;

    }

    setOrbit(orbit) {

        this.orbit = orbit;

    }

    rotate(deltaYaw, deltaPitch) {

        if (!this.enabled) {
            return;
        }

        if (!this.orbit) {
            return;
        }

        this.orbit.rotate(
            deltaYaw,
            deltaPitch
        );

    }

    zoom(delta) {

        if (!this.enabled) {
            return;
        }

        if (!this.orbit) {
            return;
        }

        this.orbit.zoom(delta);

    }

    getCamera() {

        return this.camera;

    }

    getTarget() {

        return this.target;

    }

    setEnabled(enabled) {

        this.enabled = Boolean(enabled);

    }

    isEnabled() {

        return this.enabled;

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        if (!this.enabled) {
            return;
        }

        if (!this.camera || !this.target) {
            return;
        }

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(
            "camera:target-updated",
            this.target.position
        );

    }

}

export class CameraFollow {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.camera = null;

        this.target = null;

        this.offset = {

            x: 0,

            y: 5,

            z: 8

        };

        this.followSpeed = 8;

        this.enabled = true;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.camera =
            this.game.scene?.getCamera?.() ?? null;

        this.target = this.game.localPlayer ?? null;

        console.log("CameraFollow initialisé.");

    }

    setCamera(camera) {

        this.camera = camera;

    }

    setTarget(target) {

        this.target = target;

    }

    setOffset(x, y, z) {

        this.offset.x = Number(x) || 0;

        this.offset.y = Number(y) || 0;

        this.offset.z = Number(z) || 0;

    }

    setFollowSpeed(speed) {

        this.followSpeed = Math.max(

            0,

            Number(speed) || 0

        );

    }

    setEnabled(enabled) {

        this.enabled = Boolean(enabled);

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        if (

            !this.enabled ||

            !this.camera ||

            !this.target ||

            !this.target.position

        ) {

            return;

        }

        const targetPosition = this.target.position;

        const factor = Math.min(

            1,

            this.followSpeed * deltaTime

        );

        const desiredX =

            targetPosition.x + this.offset.x;

        const desiredY =

            targetPosition.y + this.offset.y;

        const desiredZ =

            targetPosition.z + this.offset.z;

        this.camera.position.x +=

            (desiredX - this.camera.position.x) *

            factor;

        this.camera.position.y +=

            (desiredY - this.camera.position.y) *

            factor;

        this.camera.position.z +=

            (desiredZ - this.camera.position.z) *

            factor;

    }

    isEnabled() {

        return this.enabled;

    }

}

export class CameraOrbit {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.camera = null;

        this.target = null;

        this.distance = 6;

        this.minDistance = 2;

        this.maxDistance = 20;

        this.yaw = 0;

        this.pitch = 0.35;

        this.minPitch = -1.2;

        this.maxPitch = 1.2;

        this.enabled = true;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.camera =
            this.game.scene?.getCamera?.() ?? null;;

        this.target = this.game.localPlayer ?? null;

        console.log("CameraOrbit initialisé.");

    }

    setCamera(camera) {

        this.camera = camera;

    }

    setTarget(target) {

        this.target = target;

    }

    setDistance(distance) {

        this.distance = Math.max(

            this.minDistance,

            Math.min(

                this.maxDistance,

                Number(distance) || this.distance

            )

        );

    }

    setYaw(yaw) {

        this.yaw = Number(yaw) || 0;

    }

    setPitch(pitch) {

        this.pitch = Math.max(

            this.minPitch,

            Math.min(

                this.maxPitch,

                Number(pitch) || 0

            )

        );

    }

    rotate(deltaYaw, deltaPitch) {

        this.yaw += Number(deltaYaw) || 0;

        this.setPitch(

            this.pitch + (Number(deltaPitch) || 0)

        );

    }

    zoom(delta) {

        this.setDistance(

            this.distance + (Number(delta) || 0)

        );

    }

    update() {

        if (

            !this.enabled ||

            !this.camera ||

            !this.target ||

            !this.target.position

        ) {

            return;

        }

        const target = this.target.position;

const cosPitch = Math.cos(this.pitch);
const sinPitch = Math.sin(this.pitch);

/*
 * La caméra reste toujours derrière le joueur.
 * La rotation du joueur détermine donc le yaw
 * de la caméra.
 */
const playerYaw =
    this.target.rotation?.y ?? 0;

const cameraYaw =
    playerYaw - Math.PI;

const cosYaw =
    Math.cos(cameraYaw);

const sinYaw =
    Math.sin(cameraYaw);

        this.camera.position.x =

            target.x +

            this.distance *

            cosPitch *

            sinYaw;

        this.camera.position.y =

            target.y +

            this.distance *

            sinPitch;

        this.camera.position.z =

            target.z +

            this.distance *

            cosPitch *

            cosYaw;
        
        this.camera.lookAt(
    target.x,
    target.y + 0.9,
    target.z
);

    }

    setEnabled(enabled) {

        this.enabled = Boolean(enabled);

    }

    isEnabled() {

        return this.enabled;

    }

} 
.

export class CameraSmoothing {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.enabled = true;

        this.positionSpeed = 10;

        this.rotationSpeed = 10;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("CameraSmoothing initialisé.");

    }

    setEnabled(enabled) {

        this.enabled = Boolean(enabled);

    }

    setPositionSpeed(speed) {

        this.positionSpeed = Math.max(

            0,

            Number(speed) || 0

        );

    }

    setRotationSpeed(speed) {

        this.rotationSpeed = Math.max(

            0,

            Number(speed) || 0

        );

    }

    smoothPosition(current, target, deltaTime) {

        if (!this.enabled || !current || !target) {

            return current;

        }

        const factor = Math.min(

            1,

            this.positionSpeed * deltaTime

        );

        current.x +=

            (target.x - current.x) * factor;

        current.y +=

            (target.y - current.y) * factor;

        current.z +=

            (target.z - current.z) * factor;

        return current;

    }

    smoothRotation(current, target, deltaTime) {

        if (!this.enabled || !current || !target) {

            return current;

        }

        const factor = Math.min(

            1,

            this.rotationSpeed * deltaTime

        );

        current.x +=

            (target.x - current.x) * factor;

        current.y +=

            (target.y - current.y) * factor;

        current.z +=

            (target.z - current.z) * factor;

        return current;

    }

    isEnabled() {

        return this.enabled;

    }

} 
.

CHEMIN : C:\Users\hp\Downloads\ISLAMCRAFT V2.0\client\src\core\CleanupManager.js 
========================================== 
export class CleanupManager {

    constructor() {

        this.tasks = new Set();

    }

    register(task) {

        if (typeof task !== "function") {

            throw new Error("CleanupManager : la tâche doit être une fonction.");

        }

        this.tasks.add(task);

    }

    unregister(task) {

        this.tasks.delete(task);

    }

    cleanup() {

        for (const task of this.tasks) {

            try {

                task();

            } catch (error) {

                console.error("Erreur pendant le nettoyage :", error);

            }

        }

        this.tasks.clear();

    }

    destroy() {

        this.cleanup();

    }

}

    }

    async loadTreasureModel() {

    this.treasureLoader =
        new GLTFLoader();


    const modelURL =
        new URL(
            "../assets/low_poly_treasure_chest.glb",
            import.meta.url
        ).href;


    const gltf =
        await this.treasureLoader.loadAsync(
            modelURL
        );


    if (!gltf?.scene) {

        throw new Error(
            "Le modèle GLB du trésor ne contient aucune scène."
        );

    }


    this.treasureModel =
        gltf.scene;


    /*
     * ==================================================
     * DIMENSION AUTOMATIQUE DU TRÉSOR
     * ==================================================
     *
     * On ne dépend plus de la taille originale
     * du modèle Sketchfab.
     *
     * Le coffre sera automatiquement redimensionné
     * pour faire environ 0.9 bloc de haut.
     */

    /*
 * ==================================================
 * NORMALISATION AUTOMATIQUE DU TRÉSOR
 * ==================================================
 *
 * Le modèle Sketchfab peut avoir une échelle
 * interne énorme.
 *
 * On mesure sa vraie bounding box puis on
 * normalise le modèle pour qu'il fasse
 * exactement 0.60 bloc de haut.
 */

this.treasureModel.scale.set(
    1,
    1,
    1
);

this.treasureModel.updateMatrixWorld(
    true
);


const originalBox =
    new THREE.Box3()
        .setFromObject(
            this.treasureModel
        );


const originalSize =
    new THREE.Vector3();


originalBox.getSize(
    originalSize
);


console.log(
    "[TREASURE] Taille originale du modèle :",
    originalSize.toArray()
);


const targetHeight =
    0.8;


if (
    originalSize.y > 0
) {

    const scaleFactor =
        targetHeight /
        originalSize.y;


    this.treasureModel.scale.setScalar(
        scaleFactor
    );

}


this.treasureModel.updateMatrixWorld(
    true
);


/*
 * Vérification de la taille APRÈS
 * normalisation.
 */

const normalizedBox =
    new THREE.Box3()
        .setFromObject(
            this.treasureModel
        );


const normalizedSize =
    new THREE.Vector3();


normalizedBox.getSize(
    normalizedSize
);


console.log(
    "[TREASURE] Taille finale du modèle :",
    normalizedSize.toArray()
);


    this.treasureModel.traverse(
        object => {

            if (!object.isMesh) {

                return;

            }


            object.castShadow =
                true;

            object.receiveShadow =
                true;


            object.userData.isTreasure =
                true;

        }
    );


    /*
     * Le modèle chargé est uniquement
     * un modèle de référence.
     */

    this.treasureModel.visible =
        false;


    console.log(
        "Modèle de trésor GLB chargé.",
        {
            tailleOriginale:
                originalSize.toArray(),

            hauteurCible:
                targetHeight,

            echelleFinale:
                this.treasureModel.scale.toArray()
        }
    );

}

spawnTreasures() {

    if (
        this.treasuresSpawned ||
        !this.treasureModel
    ) {

        return;

    }


    const chunkManager =
        this.world
            ?.getChunkManager?.();


    if (!chunkManager) {

        return;

    }


    const chunks =
        chunkManager.getLoadedChunks?.();


    if (!chunks || chunks.size === 0) {

        return;

    }


    const chunkList =
        [...chunks.values()]
            .filter(
                chunk =>
                    chunk &&
                    Array.isArray(
                        chunk.terrain
                    )
            );


    if (chunkList.length === 0) {

        return;

    }


    /*
     * On trie les chunks pour que
     * le placement soit déterministe.
     */

    chunkList.sort(
        (a, b) =>
            a.chunkX - b.chunkX ||
            a.chunkZ - b.chunkZ
    );


    const positions = [];

const occupiedTreasurePositions =
    new Set();


/*
 * ==================================================
 * GÉNÉRATION DES CANDIDATS
 * ==================================================
 *
 * On génère beaucoup plus de positions
 * que nécessaire, puis on sélectionne celles
 * qui sont suffisamment éloignées.
 */

const candidates = [];


for (
    let chunkIndex = 0;
    chunkIndex < chunkList.length;
    chunkIndex++
) {

    const chunk =
        chunkList[chunkIndex];


    const size =
        chunk.size;


    /*
     * Plusieurs candidats par chunk.
     *
     * Cela évite que les trésors utilisent
     * systématiquement la même colonne.
     */

    const candidatesPerChunk =
        100;


    for (
        let i = 0;
        i < candidatesPerChunk;
        i++
    ) {

        /*
         * Génération déterministe.
         */

        const seed =
            Math.sin(
                (
                    chunk.chunkX * 127.1 +
                    chunk.chunkZ * 311.7 +
                    chunkIndex * 97.3 +
                    i * 74.7
                )
            ) *
            43758.5453;


        const normalized =
            seed -
            Math.floor(seed);


        const seed2 =
            Math.sin(
                (
                    chunk.chunkX * 269.5 +
                    chunk.chunkZ * 183.3 +
                    chunkIndex * 53.7 +
                    i * 91.7
                )
            ) *
            43758.5453;


        const normalized2 =
            seed2 -
            Math.floor(seed2);


        const localX =
            Math.min(
                size - 1,
                Math.max(
                    0,
                    Math.floor(
                        normalized * size
                    )
                )
            );


        const localZ =
            Math.min(
                size - 1,
                Math.max(
                    0,
                    Math.floor(
                        normalized2 * size
                    )
                )
            );


        const highestY =
            chunk.getHighestSolidY?.(
                localX,
                localZ
            );


        if (
            !Number.isInteger(
                highestY
            ) ||
            highestY < 0
        ) {

            continue;

        }


        const worldX =
            chunk.chunkX *
            size +
            localX +
            0.5;


        const worldZ =
            chunk.chunkZ *
            size +
            localZ +
            0.5;


        const surfaceY =
            highestY + 1;


        const positionKey =
            `${Math.floor(worldX)}:${Math.floor(surfaceY)}:${Math.floor(worldZ)}`;


        if (
            occupiedTreasurePositions.has(
                positionKey
            )
        ) {

            continue;

        }


        occupiedTreasurePositions.add(
            positionKey
        );


        candidates.push({

            x: worldX,

            y: surfaceY,

            z: worldZ

        });

    }

}


/*
 * ==================================================
 * MÉLANGE DÉTERMINISTE DES CANDIDATS
 * ==================================================
 */

candidates.sort(
    (a, b) => {

        const hashA =
            Math.sin(
                a.x * 12.9898 +
                a.z * 78.233
            );

        const hashB =
            Math.sin(
                b.x * 12.9898 +
                b.z * 78.233
            );

        return hashA - hashB;

    }
);


/*
 * ==================================================
 * SÉLECTION DES POSITIONS
 * ==================================================
 *
 * Distance minimale de 8 blocs entre
 * deux trésors.
 */

const minimumTreasureDistance =
    5;


const minimumTreasureDistanceSquared =
    minimumTreasureDistance *
    minimumTreasureDistance;


for (
    const candidate of candidates
) {

    if (
        positions.length >=
        this.treasureCount
    ) {

        break;

    }


    let tooClose =
        false;


    for (
        const existing of positions
    ) {

        const dx =
            candidate.x -
            existing.x;


        const dz =
            candidate.z -
            existing.z;


        const distanceSquared =
            dx * dx +
            dz * dz;


        if (
            distanceSquared <
            minimumTreasureDistanceSquared
        ) {

            tooClose = true;

            break;

        }

    }


    if (tooClose) {

        continue;

    }


    positions.push(
        candidate
    );

}


console.log(
    `[TREASURE] ${positions.length}/${this.treasureCount} positions sélectionnées parmi ${candidates.length} candidates.`
);

    /*
     * Il faut avoir réellement réussi
     * à trouver les positions.
     */

    if (
        positions.length === 0
    ) {

        return;

    }


    for (
        let i = 0;
        i < positions.length;
        i++
    ) {

        this.createTreasure(
            i,
            positions[i]
        );

    }


    this.treasuresSpawned =
        true;


    console.log(
        `💎 ${this.treasures.size} trésors générés.`
    );

}
createTreasure(id, position) {

    if (!this.treasureModel) {

        return null;

    }


    /*
     * Chaque trésor reçoit sa propre
     * copie du modèle.
     */

    const mesh =
    this.treasureModel.clone(
        true
    );


mesh.visible = true;


/*
 * ==================================================
 * NORMALISATION FINALE DE CHAQUE COFFRE
 * ==================================================
 *
 * On ne fait pas confiance uniquement à
 * l'échelle du modèle original.
 *
 * Chaque copie est mesurée puis redimensionnée
 * individuellement pour garantir une hauteur
 * réelle de 0.60 bloc.
 */

mesh.scale.set(
    1,
    1,
    1
);


mesh.updateMatrixWorld(
    true
);


const treasureBoxBeforeScale =
    new THREE.Box3()
        .setFromObject(
            mesh
        );


const treasureSizeBeforeScale =
    new THREE.Vector3();


treasureBoxBeforeScale.getSize(
    treasureSizeBeforeScale
);


const targetTreasureHeight =
    0.60;


let treasureScale =
    1;


if (
    treasureSizeBeforeScale.y > 0
) {

    treasureScale =
        targetTreasureHeight /
        treasureSizeBeforeScale.y;


    mesh.scale.setScalar(
        treasureScale
    );

}


mesh.updateMatrixWorld(
    true
);


/*
 * Vérification de la taille réelle.
 */

const treasureBox =
    new THREE.Box3()
        .setFromObject(
            mesh
        );


const treasureSize =
    new THREE.Vector3();


treasureBox.getSize(
    treasureSize
);


console.log(
    "[TREASURE] COFFRE",
    {
        id,

        taille: {
            hauteur:
                treasureSize.y,

            largeur:
                treasureSize.x,

            profondeur:
                treasureSize.z
        },

        position: {
            x: position.x,

            y: position.y,

            z: position.z
        }
    }
);


    const box =
        new THREE.Box3()
            .setFromObject(
                mesh
            );


    /*
     * Faire reposer le dessous du coffre
     * exactement sur le sol.
     */

    const bottomOffset =
        box.min.y;


    mesh.position.set(
        position.x,
        position.y - bottomOffset,
        position.z
    );


    mesh.userData.isTreasure =
        true;


    mesh.userData.treasureId =
        id;


    /*
     * Création de l'objet collectible
     * du système existant.
     */

    const treasure =
        new Collectible(
            this,
            {

                id:
                    `treasure_${id}`,

                type:
                    "treasure",

                position:
    mesh.position.clone(),

scale:
    new THREE.Vector3(
        treasureScale,
        treasureScale,
        treasureScale
    ),

reward:
                    {
                        type: "treasure",
                        id:
                            `treasure_${id}`
                    },

                value: 1,

                interactionDistance: 3

            }
        );


    treasure.setMesh(
        mesh
    );


    /*
     * Ajouter le trésor à la scène.
     */

    this.scene.add(
        mesh
    );


    /*
     * Le EntityManager s'occupe
     * maintenant de son update().
     */

    this.entityManager.register(
        treasure
    );


    this.treasures.set(
        treasure.id,
        treasure
    );


    return treasure;

}

collectTreasure(treasure) {
    if (!treasure) {
        return false;
    }

    if (treasure.collected) {
        return false;
    }

    const player =
        this.localPlayer;

    if (!player) {
        return false;
    }

    const playerPosition =
        player.position;

    if (!playerPosition) {
        return false;
    }

    if (
        !treasure.canPlayerInteract?.(
            playerPosition
        )
    ) {
        return false;
    }

    const collected =
        treasure.collect(
            player
        );

    if (!collected) {
        return false;
    }

    this.treasuresCollected =
        Math.min(
            this.treasuresCollected + 1,
            this.treasureCount
        );

    /*
     * Score du trésor.
     */
    this.scoreController?.addScore?.(
        100
    );

    /*
     * XP d'exploration / collecte.
     */
    this.progressionController?.addXP?.(
        25
    );

    /*
     * Synchronisation HUD.
     */
    this.updateHUD();

    /*
     * Événement gameplay.
     */
    this.eventBus?.emit?.(
        "treasure:collected",
        {
            treasureId:
                treasure.id,

            collected:
                this.treasuresCollected,

            total:
                this.treasureCount
        }
    );

    /*
     * Condition de fin :
     * tous les trésors sont collectés.
     */
    if (
        this.treasuresCollected >=
        this.treasureCount
    ) {
        this.eventBus?.emit?.(
            "treasure:collection-complete",
            {
                collected:
                    this.treasuresCollected,

                total:
                    this.treasureCount
            }
        );
    }

    return true;
}
updateHUD() {
    if (!this.uiManager) {
        return;
    }

    const score =
        this.scoreController?.getScore?.() ??
        0;

    const bestScore =
        this.scoreController?.getBestScore?.() ??
        0;

    this.uiManager.updateScore(
        score,
        bestScore
    );

    const currentXP =
        this.progressionController?.getCurrentXP?.() ??
        this.xpController?.getCurrentXP?.() ??
        0;

    const requiredXP =
        this.progressionController?.getRequiredXP?.() ??
        this.levelController?.getRequiredXP?.() ??
        100;

    this.uiManager.updateXP(
        currentXP,
        requiredXP
    );

    const level =
        this.progressionController?.getLevel?.() ??
        this.levelController?.getLevel?.() ??
        1;

    this.uiManager.updateLevel(
        level
    );

    const playerName =
        this.localPlayer?.name ??
        "Joueur";

    this.uiManager.updatePlayerName(
        playerName
    );
}
    start() {

        if (!this.initialized) {

            throw new Error(
                "Le jeu doit être initialisé avant d'être lancé."
            );

        }

        console.log(
            "Démarrage de la Game Loop..."
        );

        this.loop.start();

    }


    stop() {

        if (this.loop) {

            this.loop.stop();

        }

    }


    update(deltaTime) {

        /*
         * INPUT
         */

        if (this.inputManager) {

            this.inputManager.update(
                deltaTime
            );

        }


        /*
         * JOUEUR
         */

        if (this.playerController) {
    this.playerController.update(
        deltaTime
    );
    if (
    this.collisionController
) {

    this.collisionController.update(
        deltaTime
    );

}
if (
    this.interactionController
) {

    this.interactionController.update(
        deltaTime
    );

}
}


/*
 * MONDE
 */

if (this.world) {
    this.world.update(
        deltaTime
    );
}
/*
 * TRÉSORS
 */

if (
    !this.treasuresSpawned
) {

    this.spawnTreasures();

}


/*
 * ENTITÉS
 */

        if (this.entityManager) {

            this.entityManager.update(
                deltaTime
            );

        }


        /*
         * CAMÉRA
         */

        if (this.cameraController) {

            this.cameraController.update(
                deltaTime
            );

        }

        if (this.cameraOrbit) {

            this.cameraOrbit.update(
                deltaTime
            );

        }

        if (this.cameraFollow) {

            this.cameraFollow.update(
                deltaTime
            );

        }

        /*
 * HUD
 */

if (this.uiManager) {
    this.uiManager.update(
        deltaTime
    );
}

        /*
         * SCÈNE
         */

        if (this.scene) {

            this.scene.update(
                deltaTime
            );

        }

    }


    render(interpolation) {

        if (this.renderer) {

            this.renderer.render(
                interpolation
            );

        }

    }

}

export class GameLoop {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.running = false;

        this.lastFrameTime = 0;

        this.deltaTime = 0;

        this.fixedTimeStep = 1 / 60;

        this.accumulator = 0;

        this.maxFrameTime = 0.25;

        this.frameId = null;

        this.frameCount = 0;

        this.fps = 0;

        this.fpsTimer = 0;

        this.loop = this.loop.bind(this);

    }

    start() {

        if (this.running) return;

        this.running = true;

        this.lastFrameTime = performance.now() / 1000;

        this.frameId = requestAnimationFrame(this.loop);

    }

    stop() {

        this.running = false;

        if (this.frameId !== null) {

            cancelAnimationFrame(this.frameId);

            this.frameId = null;

        }

    }

    loop() {

        if (!this.running) return;

        const currentTime = performance.now() / 1000;

        let frameTime = currentTime - this.lastFrameTime;

        this.lastFrameTime = currentTime;

        if (frameTime > this.maxFrameTime) {

            frameTime = this.maxFrameTime;

        }

        this.deltaTime = frameTime;

        this.accumulator += frameTime;

        while (this.accumulator >= this.fixedTimeStep) {

            this.game.update(this.fixedTimeStep);

            this.accumulator -= this.fixedTimeStep;

        }

        const interpolation = this.accumulator / this.fixedTimeStep;

        this.game.render(interpolation);

        this.updateFPS(frameTime);

        this.frameId = requestAnimationFrame(this.loop);

    }

    updateFPS(deltaTime) {

        this.frameCount++;

        this.fpsTimer += deltaTime;

        if (this.fpsTimer >= 1) {

            this.fps = this.frameCount;

            this.frameCount = 0;

            this.fpsTimer = 0;

        }

    }

    getFPS() {

        return this.fps;

    }

    getDeltaTime() {

        return this.deltaTime;

    }

}

export class GameState {

    constructor() {

        this.started = false;
        this.paused = false;

        this.currentState = "BOOT";

        this.previousState = null;

        this.playerReady = false;

        this.worldReady = false;

        this.networkReady = false;

        this.assetsReady = false;

        this.loadingProgress = 0;

    }

    setState(newState) {

        if (this.currentState === newState) return;

        this.previousState = this.currentState;

        this.currentState = newState;

        console.log(
            `[GameState] ${this.previousState} -> ${this.currentState}`
        );

    }

    getState() {

        return this.currentState;

    }

    is(stateName) {

        return this.currentState === stateName;

    }

    markStarted() {

        this.started = true;

    }

    markPaused(value = true) {

        this.paused = value;

    }

    isRunning() {

        return this.started && !this.paused;

    }

    setLoadingProgress(percent) {

        this.loadingProgress = Math.max(
            0,
            Math.min(100, percent)
        );

    }

    getLoadingProgress() {

        return this.loadingProgress;

    }

    setWorldReady(value = true) {

        this.worldReady = value;

    }

    setAssetsReady(value = true) {

        this.assetsReady = value;

    }

    setNetworkReady(value = true) {

        this.networkReady = value;

    }

    setPlayerReady(value = true) {

        this.playerReady = value;

    }

    isFullyReady() {

        return (
            this.worldReady &&
            this.assetsReady &&
            this.playerReady &&
            this.networkReady
        );

    }

}

export class TimeManager {

    constructor() {

        this.startTime = performance.now();

        this.currentTime = this.startTime;

        this.previousTime = this.startTime;

        this.deltaTime = 0;

        this.elapsedTime = 0;

        this.timeScale = 1;

        this.paused = false;

    }

    update() {

        this.currentTime = performance.now();

        if (this.paused) {

            this.deltaTime = 0;

            this.previousTime = this.currentTime;

            return;

        }

        this.deltaTime =
            ((this.currentTime - this.previousTime) / 1000) *
            this.timeScale;

        this.previousTime = this.currentTime;

        this.elapsedTime += this.deltaTime;

    }

    pause() {

        this.paused = true;

    }

    resume() {

        this.paused = false;

        this.previousTime = performance.now();

    }

    reset() {

        this.startTime = performance.now();

        this.currentTime = this.startTime;

        this.previousTime = this.startTime;

        this.deltaTime = 0;

        this.elapsedTime = 0;

    }

    setTimeScale(scale) {

        this.timeScale = Math.max(0, scale);

    }

    getDeltaTime() {

        return this.deltaTime;

    }

    getElapsedTime() {

        return this.elapsedTime;

    }

    getElapsedMilliseconds() {

        return this.elapsedTime * 1000;

    }

    isPaused() {

        return this.paused;

    }

}

import * as THREE from "three";

export class EnvironmentRenderer {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.scene = null;

        this.sky = null;

        this.ground = null;

        this.grid = null;

        this.enabled = true;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.scene = this.game.scene.getScene();

        this.createGround();

        this.createGrid();

        this.createSky();

        console.log("EnvironmentRenderer initialisé.");

    }

    createGround() {

        const geometry = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(1000, 1000);

        const material = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({

            color: 0x6aa84f

        });

        this.ground = new THREE.Mesh(

            geometry,

            material

        );

        this.ground.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;

        this.ground.receiveShadow = true;

        this.scene.add(this.ground);

    }

    createGrid() {

        this.grid = new THREE.GridHelper(

            1000,

            100,

            0x666666,

            0x444444

        );

        this.grid.visible = this.game.config.debug.showChunks;

        this.scene.add(this.grid);

    }

    createSky() {

        this.sky = new THREE.Color(

            this.game.config.backgroundColor

        );

        this.scene.background = this.sky;

    }

    update() {

        // Plus tard :
        // - cycle jour / nuit
        // - brouillard dynamique
        // - météo
        // - nuages
        // - ambiance

    }

    setGroundColor(color) {

        this.ground.material.color.set(color);

    }

    setBackground(color) {

        this.scene.background = new THREE.Color(color);

    }

}

import * as THREE from "three";

export class FrustumCulling {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.camera = null;

        this.frustum = new THREE.Frustum();

        this.projectionMatrix = new THREE.Matrix4();

        this.dynamicObjects = new Set();

        this.enabled = true;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.camera = this.game.scene.getCamera();

        console.log("FrustumCulling initialisé.");

    }

    register(object) {

        if (!object) return;

        this.dynamicObjects.add(object);

    }

    unregister(object) {

        this.dynamicObjects.delete(object);

    }

    update() {

        if (!this.enabled) return;

        this.projectionMatrix.multiplyMatrices(

            this.camera.projectionMatrix,

            this.camera.matrixWorldInverse

        );

        this.frustum.setFromProjectionMatrix(

            this.projectionMatrix

        );

        for (const object of this.dynamicObjects) {

            if (!object) continue;

            if (!object.geometry) continue;

            if (!object.geometry.boundingSphere) {

                object.geometry.computeBoundingSphere();

            }

            const sphere = object.geometry.boundingSphere.clone();

            sphere.applyMatrix4(object.matrixWorld);

            object.visible = this.frustum.intersectsSphere(sphere);

        }

    }

    clear() {

        this.dynamicObjects.clear();

    }

    setEnabled(value) {

        this.enabled = value;

    }

}

import * as THREE from "three";

export class InstancingManager {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.scene = null;

        this.instancedMeshes = new Map();

        this.tempMatrix = new THREE.Matrix4();

        this.enabled = true;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.scene = this.game.scene.getScene();

        console.log("InstancingManager initialisé.");

    }

    createInstancedMesh(id, geometry, material, maxInstances) {

        if (this.instancedMeshes.has(id)) {

            return this.instancedMeshes.get(id);

        }

        const mesh = new THREE.InstancedMesh(

            geometry,

            material,

            maxInstances

        );

        mesh.castShadow = true;

        mesh.receiveShadow = true;

        mesh.count = 0;

        this.scene.add(mesh);

        this.instancedMeshes.set(id, mesh);

        return mesh;

    }

    addInstance(id, position, rotation, scale = 1) {

        const mesh = this.instancedMeshes.get(id);

        if (!mesh) return -1;

        const index = mesh.count;

        this.tempMatrix.compose(

            position,

            rotation,

            new THREE.Vector3(scale, scale, scale)

        );

        mesh.setMatrixAt(index, this.tempMatrix);

        mesh.count++;

        mesh.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;

        return index;

    }

    clear(id) {

        const mesh = this.instancedMeshes.get(id);

        if (!mesh) return;

        mesh.count = 0;

        mesh.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;

    }

    remove(id) {

        const mesh = this.instancedMeshes.get(id);

        if (!mesh) return;

        this.scene.remove(mesh);

        mesh.dispose();

        this.instancedMeshes.delete(id);

    }

    clearAll() {

        for (const id of this.instancedMeshes.keys()) {

            this.remove(id);

        }

    }

}

import * as THREE from "three";

export class Lighting {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.scene = null;

        this.ambientLight = null;

        this.directionalLight = null;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.scene = this.game.scene.getScene();

        this.createAmbientLight();

        this.createDirectionalLight();

        console.log("Lighting initialisé.");

    }

    createAmbientLight() {

        this.ambientLight = new THREE.AmbientLight(

            0xffffff,

            1.2

        );

        this.scene.add(this.ambientLight);

    }

    createDirectionalLight() {

        this.directionalLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(

            0xffffff,

            2.0

        );

        this.directionalLight.position.set(

            100,

            150,

            75

        );

        this.directionalLight.castShadow = true;

        this.directionalLight.shadow.mapSize.width = 4096;
        this.directionalLight.shadow.mapSize.height = 4096;

        this.directionalLight.shadow.camera.near = 1;
        this.directionalLight.shadow.camera.far = 500;

        this.directionalLight.shadow.camera.left = -200;
        this.directionalLight.shadow.camera.right = 200;
        this.directionalLight.shadow.camera.top = 200;
        this.directionalLight.shadow.camera.bottom = -200;

        this.scene.add(this.directionalLight);

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        // Réservé au futur cycle jour/nuit.
    }

    getSun() {

        return this.directionalLight;

    }

    getAmbient() {

        return this.ambientLight;

    }

}

import * as THREE from "three";

export class LODManager {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.camera = null;

        this.enabled = true;

        this.objects = new Set();

        this.defaultDistances = {

            HIGH: 35,
            MEDIUM: 80,
            LOW: 180,
            HIDDEN: 300

        };

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.camera = this.game.scene.getCamera();

        console.log("LODManager initialisé.");

    }

    register(object, options = {}) {

        if (!object) return;

        object.userData.lod = {

            high: options.high ?? this.defaultDistances.HIGH,

            medium: options.medium ?? this.defaultDistances.MEDIUM,

            low: options.low ?? this.defaultDistances.LOW,

            hidden: options.hidden ?? this.defaultDistances.HIDDEN,

            current: "HIGH"

        };

        this.objects.add(object);

    }

    unregister(object) {

        this.objects.delete(object);

    }

    update() {

        if (!this.enabled) return;

        if (!this.camera) return;

        const cameraPosition = this.camera.position;

        for (const object of this.objects) {

            if (!object) continue;

            const distance = cameraPosition.distanceTo(

                object.position

            );

            const lod = object.userData.lod;

            if (!lod) continue;

            if (distance > lod.hidden) {

                object.visible = false;

                lod.current = "HIDDEN";

            }

            else {

                object.visible = true;

                if (distance <= lod.high) {

                    lod.current = "HIGH";

                }

                else if (distance <= lod.medium) {

                    lod.current = "MEDIUM";

                }

                else {

                    lod.current = "LOW";

                }

            }

        }

    }

    clear() {

        this.objects.clear();

    }

    setEnabled(enabled) {

        this.enabled = enabled;

    }

}

import * as THREE from "three";

export class Renderer {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.renderer = null;

        this.clock = new THREE.Clock();

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({

            antialias: this.game.config.antialias,

            alpha: this.game.config.alpha

        });

        this.renderer.setPixelRatio(

            this.game.config.pixelRatio

        );

        this.renderer.setSize(

            window.innerWidth,

            window.innerHeight

        );

        this.renderer.setClearColor(

            this.game.config.backgroundColor

        );

        this.renderer.shadowMap.enabled =

            this.game.config.shadows;

        this.renderer.shadowMap.type =

            THREE.PCFSoftShadowMap;

        document.body.style.margin = "0";

        document.body.style.overflow = "hidden";

        document.body.appendChild(

            this.renderer.domElement

        );

        window.addEventListener(

            "resize",

            this.onResize.bind(this)

        );

        console.log("Renderer initialisé.");

    }

    render() {

        if (

            !this.game.scene ||

            !this.game.scene.scene ||

            !this.game.scene.camera

        ) {

            return;

        }

        this.renderer.render(

            this.game.scene.scene,

            this.game.scene.camera

        );

    }

    onResize() {

        if (

            !this.game.scene ||

            !this.game.scene.camera

        ) {

            return;

        }

        this.game.scene.camera.aspect =

            window.innerWidth /

            window.innerHeight;

        this.game.scene.camera.updateProjectionMatrix();

        this.renderer.setSize(

            window.innerWidth,

            window.innerHeight

        );

    }

    getRenderer() {

        return this.renderer;

    }

}

import * as THREE from "three";

export class SceneManager {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.scene = null;

        this.camera = null;

        this.clock = new THREE.Clock();

        this.updatables = [];

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.createScene();

        this.createCamera();

        console.log("SceneManager initialisé.");

    }

    createScene() {

        this.scene = new THREE.Scene();

        this.scene.background = new THREE.Color(
            this.game.config.backgroundColor
        );

        this.scene.fog = new THREE.Fog(
            this.game.config.backgroundColor,
            100,
            this.game.config.camera.far
        );

    }

    createCamera() {

        this.camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(

            this.game.config.camera.fov,

            window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight,

            this.game.config.camera.near,

            this.game.config.camera.far

        );

        this.camera.position.set(0, 8, 12);

        this.camera.lookAt(0, 0, 0);

    }

    add(object) {

        if (!object) return;

        this.scene.add(object);

    }

    remove(object) {

        if (!object) return;

        this.scene.remove(object);

    }

    registerUpdatable(system) {

        if (!system) return;

        if (typeof system.update !== "function") return;

        this.updatables.push(system);

    }

    unregisterUpdatable(system) {

        this.updatables = this.updatables.filter(
            item => item !== system
        );

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        for (const system of this.updatables) {

            system.update(deltaTime);

        }

    }

    getScene() {

        return this.scene;

    }

    getCamera() {

        return this.camera;

    }

}

import { LocalPlayer } from "./player/LocalPlayer.js";
import { RemotePlayer } from "./player/RemotePlayer.js";
import { Chest } from "./chest/Chest.js";
import { WorldObject } from "./world/WorldObject.js";
import { InteractiveObject } from "./world/InteractiveObject.js";
import { Collectible } from "./world/Collectible.js";

export class EntityFactory {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("EntityFactory initialisé.");

    }

    create(type, data = {}) {

        switch (type) {

            case "localPlayer":

                return new LocalPlayer(
                    this.game,
                    data
                );

            case "remotePlayer":

                return new RemotePlayer(
                    this.game,
                    data
                );

            case "chest":

                return new Chest(
                    this.game,
                    data
                );

            case "worldObject":

                return new WorldObject(
                    this.game,
                    data
                );

            case "interactiveObject":

                return new InteractiveObject(
                    this.game,
                    data
                );

            case "collectible":

                return new Collectible(
                    this.game,
                    data
                );

            default:

                throw new Error(

                    `Type d'entité inconnu : ${type}`

                );

        }

    }

}

export class EntityManager {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.entities = new Map();

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("EntityManager initialisé.");

    }

    register(entity) {

        if (!entity) {

            return;

        }

        this.entities.set(

            entity.id,

            entity

        );

    }

    unregister(id) {

        this.entities.delete(id);

    }

    get(id) {

        return this.entities.get(id);

    }

    has(id) {

        return this.entities.has(id);

    }

    getAll() {

        return [

            ...this.entities.values()

        ];

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        for (const entity of this.entities.values()) {

            if (typeof entity.update === "function") {

                entity.update(deltaTime);

            }

        }

    }

    clear() {

        this.entities.clear();

    }

}

export class EntityRegistry {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.types = new Map();

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("EntityRegistry initialisé.");

    }

    register(type, constructor) {

        if (this.types.has(type)) {

            console.warn(

                `Le type "${type}" est déjà enregistré.`

            );

            return;

        }

        this.types.set(

            type,

            constructor

        );

    }

    unregister(type) {

        this.types.delete(type);

    }

    has(type) {

        return this.types.has(type);

    }

    get(type) {

        return this.types.get(type);

    }

    getAll() {

        return [...this.types.keys()];

    }

    clear() {

        this.types.clear();

    }

}

import * as THREE from "three";

export class Chest {

    constructor(game, data = {}) {

        this.game = game;

        this.id = data.id ?? crypto.randomUUID();

        this.position = data.position?.clone() ?? new THREE.Vector3();

        this.rotation = new THREE.Euler();

        this.mesh = null;

        this.isOpen = false;

        this.isActive = true;

        this.canInteract = true;

        this.loot = data.loot ?? null;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("Chest initialisé.");

    }

    setMesh(mesh) {

        this.mesh = mesh;

        if (this.mesh) {

            this.mesh.position.copy(this.position);

            this.mesh.rotation.copy(this.rotation);

        }

    }

    setPosition(position) {

        this.position.copy(position);

        if (this.mesh) {

            this.mesh.position.copy(position);

        }

    }

    open() {

        if (!this.canInteract || this.isOpen) {

            return false;

        }

        this.isOpen = true;

        this.canInteract = false;

        if (this.game.chestAnimator) {

            this.game.chestAnimator.playOpen(this);

        }

        if (this.game.audioManager?.play) {

            this.game.audioManager.play("chest_open");

        }

        return true;

    }

    close() {

        this.isOpen = false;

        this.canInteract = true;

    }

    destroy() {

        this.isActive = false;

        if (this.mesh && this.game.scene) {

            this.game.scene.remove(this.mesh);

        }

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        if (!this.isActive) {

            return;

        }

        if (this.mesh) {

            this.mesh.position.copy(this.position);

            this.mesh.rotation.copy(this.rotation);

        }

    }

}

export class ChestAnimator {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.animations = new Map();

        this.openAngle = -Math.PI / 2;

        this.animationSpeed = 4;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("ChestAnimator initialisé.");

    }

    playOpen(chest) {

        if (!chest || !chest.mesh) {

            return;

        }

        this.animations.set(

            chest.id,

            {

                chest,

                target: this.openAngle,

                finished: false

            }

        );

    }

    playClose(chest) {

        if (!chest || !chest.mesh) {

            return;

        }

        this.animations.set(

            chest.id,

            {

                chest,

                target: 0,

                finished: false

            }

        );

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        for (const animation of this.animations.values()) {

            const lid =

                animation.chest.mesh.userData.lid;

            if (!lid) {

                continue;

            }

            const current = lid.rotation.x;

            const target = animation.target;

            const next = current +

                (target - current) *

                Math.min(

                    1,

                    deltaTime * this.animationSpeed

                );

            lid.rotation.x = next;

            if (Math.abs(target - next) < 0.01) {

                lid.rotation.x = target;

                animation.finished = true;

            }

        }

        for (const [id, animation] of this.animations) {

            if (animation.finished) {

               this.animations.delete(id);

            }

        }

    }

}

import * as THREE from "three";

export class ChestInteraction {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.interactionDistance = 3;

        this.nearbyChests = new Map();

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("ChestInteraction initialisé.");

    }

    register(chest) {

        if (!chest) {

            return;

        }

        this.nearbyChests.set(

            chest.id,

            chest

        );

    }

    unregister(id) {

        this.nearbyChests.delete(id);

    }

    check(playerPosition) {

        const available = [];

        for (const chest of this.nearbyChests.values()) {

            if (!chest.isActive || chest.isOpen) {

                continue;

            }

            const distance =

                playerPosition.distanceTo(

                    chest.position

                );

            if (distance <= this.interactionDistance) {

                available.push(chest);

            }

        }

        return available;

    }

    interact(playerPosition) {

        const chests = this.check(

            playerPosition

        );

        if (chests.length === 0) {

            return false;

        }

        const chest = chests[0];

        const opened = chest.open();

        if (!opened) {

            return false;

        }

        if (this.game.lootController) {

            this.game.lootController.generateLoot(

                chest

            );

        }

        if (this.game.networkManager?.sendChestOpen) {

            this.game.networkManager.sendChestOpen(

                chest.id

            );

        }

        return true;

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        // Futures améliorations :
        // - touche interaction
        // - mobile bouton
        // - highlight coffre proche
        // - animation prompt

    }

}

import * as THREE from "three";

export class ChestRenderer {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.materials = {};

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.materials.base = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({

            color: 0x8b5a2b

        });

        this.materials.lid = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({

            color: 0x6d3f1d

        });

        this.materials.lock = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({

            color: 0xffd700

        });

        console.log("ChestRenderer initialisé.");

    }

    createChest() {

        const group = new THREE.Group();

        const base = this.createBase();
        const lid = this.createLid();
        const lock = this.createLock();

        group.add(base);
        group.add(lid);
        group.add(lock);

        group.userData.base = base;
        group.userData.lid = lid;
        group.userData.lock = lock;

        group.castShadow = true;
        group.receiveShadow = true;

        return group;

    }

    createBase() {

        const geometry = new THREE.BoxGeometry(

            1.4,
            0.8,
            1

        );

        const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(

            geometry,

            this.materials.base

        );

        mesh.position.y = 0.4;

        mesh.castShadow = true;

        mesh.receiveShadow = true;

        return mesh;

    }

    createLid() {

        const geometry = new THREE.BoxGeometry(

            1.4,
            0.25,
            1

        );

        const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(

            geometry,

            this.materials.lid

        );

        mesh.position.y = 0.95;

        mesh.castShadow = true;

        mesh.receiveShadow = true;

        return mesh;

    }

    createLock() {

        const geometry = new THREE.BoxGeometry(

            0.15,
            0.2,
            0.05

        );

        const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(

            geometry,

            this.materials.lock

        );

        mesh.position.set(

            0,
            0.75,
            0.53

        );

        mesh.castShadow = true;

        return mesh;

    }

}

import * as THREE from "three";
import { PlayerModel } from "./PlayerModel.js";

export class LocalPlayer {

    constructor(game, data = {}) {

        this.game = game;

        this.id =
            data.id ??
            crypto.randomUUID();

        this.username =
            data.username ??
            "Player";

        this.skin =
            data.skin ??
            "default";

        this.position =
            new THREE.Vector3();

        this.rotation =
            new THREE.Euler();

        this.velocity =
            new THREE.Vector3();

        this.mesh = null;

        this.model = null;

        this.enabled = true;

    }


    async initialize() {

        /*
         * Position initiale
         */

        this.position.set(
            0,
            0,
            0
        );


        /*
         * Création du modèle 3D
         */

        this.model =
            new PlayerModel(this.game);

        await this.model.initialize();


        /*
         * Récupération du groupe 3D
         */

        this.setMesh(
            this.model.getMesh()
        );
        this.mesh.scale.set(
    0.85,
    0.85,
    0.85
);


        /*
         * Placement dans la scène
         */

        if (
            this.mesh &&
            this.game.scene
        ) {

            this.game.scene.add(
                this.mesh
            );

        }


        /*
         * Position initiale
         */

        this.mesh?.position.copy(
            this.position
        );

        this.mesh?.rotation.copy(
            this.rotation
        );


        console.log(
            "LocalPlayer initialisé avec modèle 3D."
        );

    }


    setMesh(mesh) {

        this.mesh = mesh;

    }


    setPosition(x, y, z) {

        this.position.set(
            x,
            y,
            z
        );

        if (this.mesh) {

            this.mesh.position.copy(
                this.position
            );

        }

    }


    setRotation(x, y, z) {

        this.rotation.set(
            x,
            y,
            z
        );

        if (this.mesh) {

            this.mesh.rotation.copy(
                this.rotation
            );

        }

    }


    translate(vector) {

        this.position.add(
            vector
        );

        if (this.mesh) {

            this.mesh.position.copy(
                this.position
            );

        }

    }


    update(deltaTime) {

        if (!this.enabled) {
            return;
        }

        if (this.mesh) {

            this.mesh.position.copy(
                this.position
            );

            this.mesh.rotation.copy(
                this.rotation
            );

        }

    }


    destroy() {

        if (
            this.mesh &&
            this.game.scene
        ) {

            this.game.scene.remove(
                this.mesh
            );

        }

        if (this.model?.group) {

            this.model.group.traverse(
                object => {

                    if (object.geometry) {

                        object.geometry.dispose();

                    }

                    if (object.material) {

                        if (
                            Array.isArray(
                                object.material
                            )
                        ) {

                            object.material.forEach(
                                material =>
                                    material.dispose()
                            );

                        }
                        else {

                            object.material.dispose();

                        }

                    }

                }
            );

        }

        this.mesh = null;
        this.model = null;

    }

}

export class PlayerAnimator {

    constructor(game, playerModel) {

        this.game = game;

        this.playerModel = playerModel;

        this.walkTime = 0;

        this.walkSpeed = 8;

        this.walkAmplitude = 0.7;

        this.currentState = "idle";

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("PlayerAnimator initialisé.");

    }

    setState(state) {

        this.currentState = state;

    }

    update(deltaTime, velocity) {

        if (!this.playerModel) {

            return;

        }

        const speed = velocity.length();

        if (speed > 0.05) {

            this.currentState = "walk";

        } else {

            this.currentState = "idle";

        }

        switch (this.currentState) {

            case "walk":

                this.animateWalk(deltaTime);

                break;

            default:

                this.animateIdle(deltaTime);

                break;

        }

    }

    animateIdle(deltaTime) {

        this.walkTime += deltaTime;

        const offset =

            Math.sin(this.walkTime * 2) * 0.02;

        this.playerModel.group.position.y = offset;

        this.playerModel.leftArm.rotation.x = 0;

        this.playerModel.rightArm.rotation.x = 0;

        this.playerModel.leftLeg.rotation.x = 0;

        this.playerModel.rightLeg.rotation.x = 0;

    }

    animateWalk(deltaTime) {

        this.walkTime += deltaTime * this.walkSpeed;

        const armAngle =

            Math.sin(this.walkTime) * this.walkAmplitude;

        const legAngle =

            Math.sin(this.walkTime) * this.walkAmplitude;

        this.playerModel.leftArm.rotation.x = armAngle;

        this.playerModel.rightArm.rotation.x = -armAngle;

        this.playerModel.leftLeg.rotation.x = -legAngle;

        this.playerModel.rightLeg.rotation.x = legAngle;

    }

}

CHEMIN : C:\Users\hp\Downloads\ISLAMCRAFT V2.0\client\src\entities\player\PlayerController.js 
========================================== 
import * as THREE from "three";

export class PlayerController {

    constructor(game, player) {

        this.game = game;

        this.player = player;

        this.enabled = true;

        this.moveDirection = new THREE.Vector3();

        this.cameraDirection = new THREE.Vector3();

        this.cameraRight = new THREE.Vector3();

        this.velocity = new THREE.Vector3();

        this.speed = 6;

        this.rotationSpeed = 5;

    }


    async initialize() {

        console.log(
            "PlayerController initialisé."
        );

    }


    update(deltaTime) {

        if (!this.enabled) {

            return;

        }


        const input =
            this.game.inputManager?.getMovement?.();


        if (!input) {

            return;

        }


        /*
         * Direction du déplacement.
         */

        this.moveDirection.set(
            0,
            0,
            0
        );


        const camera =
            this.game.scene?.getCamera?.();


        if (camera) {

            camera.getWorldDirection(
                this.cameraDirection
            );


            this.cameraDirection.y =
                0;


            this.cameraDirection.normalize();


            this.cameraRight.crossVectors(
                this.cameraDirection,
                new THREE.Vector3(
                    0,
                    1,
                    0
                )
            );


            this.cameraRight.normalize();

        }


        this.moveDirection.addScaledVector(
            this.cameraDirection,
            input.forward
        );


        this.moveDirection.addScaledVector(
            this.cameraRight,
            input.right
        );


        if (
            this.moveDirection.lengthSq() > 0
        ) {

            this.moveDirection.normalize();

        }


        /*
         * Vitesse de déplacement.
         */

        this.velocity.copy(
            this.moveDirection
        ).multiplyScalar(
            this.speed * deltaTime
        );


        /*
         * COLLISIONS
         *
         * Un seul appel au CollisionController.
         */

        const collisionController =
            this.game.collisionController;


        if (
            collisionController
        ) {

            const allowedMovement =
                collisionController.resolveMovement(
                    this.player.position,
                    this.velocity
                );


            this.velocity.copy(
                allowedMovement
            );

        }


        /*
         * Application du déplacement.
         */

        this.player.translate(
            this.velocity
        );


        /*
         * Rotation du personnage.
         */

        if (
            this.moveDirection.lengthSq() >
            0.001
        ) {

            const angle =
                Math.atan2(
                    this.moveDirection.x,
                    this.moveDirection.z
                );


            let angleDifference =
                angle -
                this.player.rotation.y;


            while (
                angleDifference >
                Math.PI
            ) {

                angleDifference -=
                    Math.PI * 2;

            }


            while (
                angleDifference <
                -Math.PI
            ) {

                angleDifference +=
                    Math.PI * 2;

            }


            this.player.rotation.y +=
                angleDifference *
                Math.min(
                    1,
                    deltaTime *
                    this.rotationSpeed
                );

        }


        /*
         * Animation.
         */

        if (
            this.game.playerAnimator
        ) {

            this.game.playerAnimator.update(
                deltaTime,
                this.velocity
            );

        }


        /*
         * Synchronisation réseau.
         */

        if (
            this.game.networkManager
                ?.sendPlayerMovement
        ) {

            this.game.networkManager.sendPlayerMovement({

                position:
                    this.player.position,

                rotation:
                    this.player.rotation

            });

        }

    }

}

import * as THREE from "three";

export class PlayerInterpolation {

    constructor(game, remotePlayer) {

        this.game = game;

        this.remotePlayer = remotePlayer;

        this.positionBuffer = [];

        this.rotationBuffer = [];

        this.interpolationDelay = 100;

        this.maxBufferSize = 20;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("PlayerInterpolation initialisé.");

    }

    pushSnapshot(snapshot) {

        if (!snapshot) {

            return;

        }

        this.positionBuffer.push({

            time: snapshot.time,

            position: new THREE.Vector3(

                snapshot.position.x,

                snapshot.position.y,

                snapshot.position.z

            )

        });

        this.rotationBuffer.push({

            time: snapshot.time,

            rotation: new THREE.Euler(

                snapshot.rotation.x,

                snapshot.rotation.y,

                snapshot.rotation.z

            )

        });

        while (this.positionBuffer.length > this.maxBufferSize) {

            this.positionBuffer.shift();

        }

        while (this.rotationBuffer.length > this.maxBufferSize) {

            this.rotationBuffer.shift();

        }

    }

    update(serverTime) {

        if (this.positionBuffer.length < 2) {

            return;

        }

        const renderTime = serverTime - this.interpolationDelay;

        let previous = null;
        let next = null;

        for (let i = 0; i < this.positionBuffer.length - 1; i++) {

            const a = this.positionBuffer[i];
            const b = this.positionBuffer[i + 1];

            if (renderTime >= a.time && renderTime <= b.time) {

                previous = a;
                next = b;
                break;

            }

        }

        if (!previous || !next) {

            return;

        }

        const alpha =

            (renderTime - previous.time) /

            (next.time - previous.time);

        this.remotePlayer.position.lerpVectors(

            previous.position,

            next.position,

            alpha

        );

        this.remotePlayer.mesh.position.copy(

            this.remotePlayer.position

        );

    }

    clear() {

        this.positionBuffer.length = 0;

        this.rotationBuffer.length = 0;

    }

}
