export class QuestionSelector {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.random = Math.random;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("QuestionSelector initialisé.");

    }

    select(source = null) {

        const database =

            this.game.questionDatabase?.getQuestions(

                source

            ) ?? [];

        if (database.length === 0) {

            return [];

        }

        const difficulty =

            this.game.difficultyController?.getDifficulty()

            ?? "easy";

        const filtered = database.filter(

            question =>

                !question.difficulty ||

                question.difficulty === difficulty

        );

        const shuffled =

            [...filtered].sort(

                () => this.random() - 0.5

            );

        const count =

            this.game.config?.quiz?.questionsPerQuiz

            ?? 5;

        return shuffled.slice(0, count);

    }

    selectByCategory(category) {

        return this.select(category);

    }

    selectDailyQuestions() {

        return this.select("daily");

    }

    selectChallengeQuestions() {

        return this.select("challenge");

    }

}

export class QuizController {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.currentSession = null;

        this.enabled = true;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("QuizController initialisé.");

    }

    startQuiz(source) {

        if (!this.enabled) {

            return false;

        }

        if (this.currentSession) {

            return false;

        }

        const session = this.game.quizSession;

        if (!session) {

            return false;

        }

        session.reset();

        session.setSource(source);

        session.start();

        this.currentSession = session;

        this.game.uiManager?.openQuiz(session);

        return true;

    }

    submitAnswer(answer) {

        if (!this.currentSession) {

            return false;

        }

        const result =

            this.currentSession.submitAnswer(

                answer

            );

        if (result.correct) {

            this.game.quizRewards?.grant(

                result

            );

        }

        if (result.finished) {

            this.finishQuiz();

        }

        return result;

    }

    finishQuiz() {

        if (!this.currentSession) {

            return;

        }

        this.currentSession.finish();

        this.game.uiManager?.closeQuiz();

        this.currentSession = null;

    }

    cancelQuiz() {

        if (!this.currentSession) {

            return;

        }

        this.currentSession.cancel();

        this.game.uiManager?.closeQuiz();

        this.currentSession = null;

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        if (!this.currentSession) {

            return;

        }

        this.currentSession.update(

            deltaTime

        );

    }

}

export class QuizRewards {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.rewardMultiplier = 1;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("QuizRewards initialisé.");

    }

    grant(result) {

        if (!result) {

            return;

        }

        const xp = this.calculateXP(result);

        const coins = this.calculateCoins(result);

        this.game.playerProfile?.addExperience(

            xp

        );

        this.game.playerInventory?.addCoins(

            coins

        );

        this.game.achievementManager?.checkQuizAchievements(

            result

        );

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "quiz:reward",

            {

                xp,

                coins,

                score: result.score

            }

        );

        return {

            xp,

            coins

        };

    }

    calculateXP(result) {

        const baseXP =

            result.score * 10;

        return Math.round(

            baseXP * this.rewardMultiplier

        );

    }

    calculateCoins(result) {

        const baseCoins =

            result.score * 5;

        return Math.round(

            baseCoins * this.rewardMultiplier

        );

    }

    setMultiplier(multiplier) {

        this.rewardMultiplier =

            Math.max(

                0,

                multiplier

            );

    }

    reset() {

        this.rewardMultiplier = 1;

    }

}

export class QuizSession {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.active = false;

        this.source = null;

        this.questions = [];

        this.currentIndex = 0;

        this.score = 0;

        this.correctAnswers = 0;

        this.wrongAnswers = 0;

        this.startTime = 0;

        this.elapsedTime = 0;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("QuizSession initialisée.");

    }

    setSource(source) {

        this.source = source;

    }

    reset() {

        this.active = false;

        this.questions = [];

        this.currentIndex = 0;

        this.score = 0;

        this.correctAnswers = 0;

        this.wrongAnswers = 0;

        this.elapsedTime = 0;

    }

    start() {

        this.questions =

            this.game.questionSelector?.select(

                this.source

            ) ?? [];

        this.active = true;

        this.startTime = performance.now();

    }

    getCurrentQuestion() {

        return this.questions[

            this.currentIndex

        ] ?? null;

    }

    submitAnswer(answer) {

        const question =

            this.getCurrentQuestion();

        if (!question) {

            return {

                correct: false,

                finished: true

            };

        }

        const correct =

            this.game.answerController?.check(

                question,

                answer

            ) ?? false;

        if (correct) {

            this.correctAnswers++;

            this.score +=

                question.points ?? 1;

        } else {

            this.wrongAnswers++;

        }

        this.currentIndex++;

        return {

            correct,

            finished:

                this.currentIndex >=

                this.questions.length,

            score: this.score,

            currentQuestion:

                this.getCurrentQuestion()

        };

    }

    finish() {

        this.active = false;

    }

    cancel() {

        this.active = false;

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        if (!this.active) {

            return;

        }

        this.elapsedTime += deltaTime;

    }

}

export class LeaderboardController {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.entries = [];

        this.maxEntries = 100;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("LeaderboardController initialisé.");

    }

    addEntry(playerId, playerName, score) {

        const existing = this.entries.find(

            entry => entry.playerId === playerId

        );

        if (existing) {

            existing.score = Math.max(

                existing.score,

                score

            );

        } else {

            this.entries.push({

                playerId,

                playerName,

                score

            });

        }

        this.sort();

        this.trim();

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "leaderboard:updated",

            this.entries

        );

    }

    sort() {

        this.entries.sort(

            (a, b) => b.score - a.score

        );

    }

    trim() {

        if (this.entries.length > this.maxEntries) {

            this.entries.length = this.maxEntries;

        }

    }

    getEntries() {

        return [...this.entries];

    }

    getPlayerRank(playerId) {

        return this.entries.findIndex(

            entry => entry.playerId === playerId

        ) + 1;

    }

    clear() {

        this.entries.length = 0;

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        // Réservé :
        // Synchronisation réseau
        // Classement mondial
        // Classement hebdomadaire
        // Classement par mode de jeu

    }

}

export class ScoreController {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.score = 0;

        this.bestScore = 0;

        this.multiplier = 1;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("ScoreController initialisé.");

    }

    addScore(points) {

        if (points <= 0) {

            return;

        }

        const finalScore = Math.floor(

            points * this.multiplier

        );

        this.score += finalScore;

        if (this.score > this.bestScore) {

            this.bestScore = this.score;

        }

        this.game.leaderboardController?.addEntry(

            this.game.localPlayer?.id,

            this.game.localPlayer?.name,

            this.score

        );

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "score:changed",

            {

                score: this.score,

                bestScore: this.bestScore,

                added: finalScore

            }

        );

    }

    removeScore(points) {

        if (points <= 0) {

            return;

        }

        this.score = Math.max(

            0,

            this.score - points

        );

    }

    setMultiplier(multiplier) {

        this.multiplier = Math.max(

            1,

            multiplier

        );

    }

    resetMultiplier() {

        this.multiplier = 1;

    }

    getScore() {

        return this.score;

    }

    getBestScore() {

        return this.bestScore;

    }

    reset() {

        this.score = 0;

        this.multiplier = 1;

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        // Réservé :
        // Bonus temporaires
        // Combo
        // Multiplicateurs
        // Synchronisation réseau

    }

} 
.

export class ScoreDisplay {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.visible = true;

        this.lastScore = -1;

        this.lastBestScore = -1;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("ScoreDisplay initialisé.");

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        if (!this.visible) {

            return;

        }

        const scoreController = this.game.scoreController;

        if (!scoreController) {

            return;

        }

        const score = scoreController.getScore();

        const bestScore = scoreController.getBestScore();

        if (

            score !== this.lastScore ||

            bestScore !== this.lastBestScore

        ) {

            this.lastScore = score;

            this.lastBestScore = bestScore;

            this.refresh();

        }

    }

    refresh() {

        this.game.uiManager?.updateScore?.(

            this.lastScore,

            this.lastBestScore

        );

    }

    show() {

        this.visible = true;

        this.refresh();

    }

    hide() {

        this.visible = false;

    }

    isVisible() {

        return this.visible;

    }

}

export class ClientStateMachine {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.states = new Map();

        this.currentState = null;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("ClientStateMachine initialisé.");

    }

    register(name, state) {

        this.states.set(

            name,

            state

        );

    }

    changeState(name, data = null) {

        const nextState =

            this.states.get(name);

        if (!nextState) {

            console.warn(

                `État inconnu : ${name}`

            );

            return false;

        }

        if (this.currentState?.exit) {

            this.currentState.exit();

        }

        this.currentState = nextState;

        if (this.currentState.enter) {

            this.currentState.enter(data);

        }

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "state:changed",

            {

                state: name,

                data

            }

        );

        return true;

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        this.currentState?.update?.(

            deltaTime

        );

    }

    render() {

        this.currentState?.render?.();

    }

    getCurrentState() {

        return this.currentState;

    }

}

export class LobbyState {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.readyPlayers = 0;

        this.requiredPlayers = 1;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("LobbyState initialisé.");

    }

    enter(data = null) {

        this.readyPlayers = 0;

        this.game.uiManager?.showLobby?.();

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "lobby:entered",

            data

        );

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        if (this.readyPlayers >= this.requiredPlayers) {

            this.game.clientStateMachine?.changeState(

                "playing"

            );

        }

    }

    exit() {

        this.game.uiManager?.hideLobby?.();

    }

    setReadyPlayers(count) {

        this.readyPlayers = Math.max(

            0,

            count

        );

    }

    setRequiredPlayers(count) {

        this.requiredPlayers = Math.max(

            1,

            count

        );

    }

}

export class PlayingState {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.elapsedTime = 0;

        this.paused = false;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("PlayingState initialisé.");

    }

    enter(data = null) {

        this.elapsedTime = 0;

        this.paused = false;

        this.game.uiManager?.showGameplay?.();

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "gameplay:started",

            data

        );

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        if (this.paused) {

            return;

        }

        this.elapsedTime += deltaTime;

        this.game.world?.update?.(

            deltaTime

        );

        this.game.playerManager?.update?.(

            deltaTime

        );

        this.game.entityManager?.update?.(

            deltaTime

        );

        if (

            this.game.matchController?.isFinished?.()

        ) {

            this.game.clientStateMachine?.changeState(

                "victory"

            );

        }

    }

    exit() {

        this.game.uiManager?.hideGameplay?.();

    }

    pause() {

        this.paused = true;

    }

    resume() {

        this.paused = false;

    }

    isPaused() {

        return this.paused;

    }

    getElapsedTime() {

        return this.elapsedTime;

    }

}

export class RestartState {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.restartDelay = 3;

        this.elapsedTime = 0;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("RestartState initialisé.");

    }

    enter(data = null) {

        this.elapsedTime = 0;

        this.game.uiManager?.showRestartScreen?.();

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "restart:entered",

            data

        );

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        this.elapsedTime += deltaTime;

        if (

            this.elapsedTime >= this.restartDelay

        ) {

            this.resetGame();

            this.game.clientStateMachine?.changeState(

                "lobby"

            );

        }

    }

    exit() {

        this.game.uiManager?.hideRestartScreen?.();

    }

    resetGame() {

        this.game.scoreController?.reset?.();

        this.game.progressionController?.reset?.();

        this.game.playerManager?.reset?.();

        this.game.entityManager?.reset?.();

        this.game.world?.reset?.();

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "game:reset"

        );

    }

    setRestartDelay(seconds) {

        this.restartDelay = Math.max(

            0,

            seconds

        );

    }

}

export class VictoryState {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.results = null;

        this.nextStateDelay = 5;

        this.elapsedTime = 0;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("VictoryState initialisé.");

    }

    enter(results = null) {

        this.results = results;

        this.elapsedTime = 0;

        this.game.uiManager?.showVictoryScreen?.(

            this.results

        );

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "victory:entered",

            this.results

        );

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        this.elapsedTime += deltaTime;

        if (

            this.elapsedTime >= this.nextStateDelay

        ) {

            this.game.clientStateMachine?.changeState(

                "restart"

            );

        }

    }

    exit() {

        this.game.uiManager?.hideVictoryScreen?.();

    }

    setResults(results) {

        this.results = results;

    }

    getResults() {

        return this.results;

    }

    setDelay(seconds) {

        this.nextStateDelay = Math.max(

            0,

            seconds

        );

    }

}

export class CameraTouchInput {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.enabled = true;


        /*
         * Doigt utilisé pour la rotation.
         */

        this.pointerId = null;

        this.startX = 0;

        this.startY = 0;


        /*
         * Doigts utilisés pour le pinch.
         */

        this.pinchTouches = new Map();

        this.previousPinchDistance = null;


        /*
         * Sensibilité rotation.
         */

        this.sensitivity = 0.25;


        /*
         * Sensibilité zoom.
         */

        this.zoomSensitivity = 0.02;

    }


    async initialize() {

        window.addEventListener(
            "touchstart",
            this.onTouchStart.bind(this),
            {
                passive: false
            }
        );


        window.addEventListener(
            "touchmove",
            this.onTouchMove.bind(this),
            {
                passive: false
            }
        );


        window.addEventListener(
            "touchend",
            this.onTouchEnd.bind(this),
            {
                passive: false
            }
        );


        window.addEventListener(
            "touchcancel",
            this.onTouchEnd.bind(this),
            {
                passive: false
            }
        );


        console.log(
            "CameraTouchInput initialisé."
        );

    }


    onTouchStart(event) {

        if (!this.enabled) {

            return;

        }

        const target =
    event.target;

if (
    target?.closest?.(
        "#islamcraft-touch-joystick"
    )
) {

    return;

}


        for (
            const touch of event.changedTouches
        ) {

            this.pinchTouches.set(
                touch.identifier,
                {
                    x: touch.clientX,
                    y: touch.clientY
                }
            );

        }


        /*
         * Deux doigts :
         * début du pinch.
         */

        if (
            this.pinchTouches.size >= 2
        ) {

            this.previousPinchDistance =
                this.getPinchDistance();


            /*
             * On arrête la rotation
             * pendant le pinch.
             */

            this.pointerId = null;

            return;

        }


        /*
         * Un doigt :
         * rotation de caméra.
         */

        const touch =
            event.changedTouches[0];


        if (!touch) {

            return;

        }


        this.pointerId =
            touch.identifier;


        this.startX =
            touch.clientX;

        this.startY =
            touch.clientY;

    }


    onTouchMove(event) {

        if (!this.enabled) {

            return;

        }


        /*
         * Mise à jour des positions.
         */

        for (
            const touch of event.changedTouches
        ) {

            if (
                this.pinchTouches.has(
                    touch.identifier
                )
            ) {

                this.pinchTouches.set(
                    touch.identifier,
                    {
                        x: touch.clientX,
                        y: touch.clientY
                    }
                );

            }

        }


        /*
         * =========================
         * PINCH
         * =========================
         */

        if (
            this.pinchTouches.size >= 2
        ) {

            const currentDistance =
                this.getPinchDistance();


            if (
                this.previousPinchDistance !== null
            ) {

                const delta =
                    currentDistance -
                    this.previousPinchDistance;


                /*
                 * IMPORTANT :
                 *
                 * doigts qui s'écartent
                 * → caméra plus proche
                 *
                 * doigts qui se rapprochent
                 * → caméra plus loin
                 */

                this.game.cameraController?.zoom?.(
                    -delta *
                    this.zoomSensitivity
                );

            }


            this.previousPinchDistance =
                currentDistance;


            return;

        }


        /*
         * =========================
         * ROTATION
         * =========================
         */

        if (
            this.pointerId === null
        ) {

            return;

        }


        for (
            const touch of event.changedTouches
        ) {

            if (
                touch.identifier !==
                this.pointerId
            ) {

                continue;

            }


            const deltaX =
                (
                    touch.clientX -
                    this.startX
                ) *
                this.sensitivity;


            const deltaY =
                (
                    touch.clientY -
                    this.startY
                ) *
                this.sensitivity;


            this.game.cameraController?.rotate?.(
                deltaX,
                deltaY
            );


            this.startX =
                touch.clientX;

            this.startY =
                touch.clientY;

        }

    }


    onTouchEnd(event) {

        for (
            const touch of event.changedTouches
        ) {

            this.pinchTouches.delete(
                touch.identifier
            );


            if (
                touch.identifier ===
                this.pointerId
            ) {

                this.pointerId =
                    null;

            }

        }


        /*
         * Fin du pinch.
         */

        if (
            this.pinchTouches.size < 2
        ) {

            this.previousPinchDistance =
                null;

        }

    }


    getPinchDistance() {

        const touches =
            [...this.pinchTouches.values()];


        if (
            touches.length < 2
        ) {

            return 0;

        }


        const first =
            touches[0];

        const second =
            touches[1];


        return Math.hypot(
            second.x - first.x,
            second.y - first.y
        );

    }


    setEnabled(enabled) {

        this.enabled =
            Boolean(enabled);

    }


    isEnabled() {

        return this.enabled;

    }

} 
.

export class InputManager {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.enabled = true;

        this.keyboardInput = null;

        this.touchInput = null;

        this.cameraTouchInput = null;

        this.touchJoystick = null;

        this.inputState = null;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.keyboardInput ??= this.game.keyboardInput;

        this.touchInput ??= this.game.touchInput;

        this.cameraTouchInput ??= this.game.cameraTouchInput;

        this.touchJoystick ??= this.game.touchJoystick;

        this.inputState ??= this.game.inputState;

        console.log("InputManager initialisé.");

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        if (!this.enabled) {

            return;

        }

        this.keyboardInput?.update?.(deltaTime);

        this.touchInput?.update?.(deltaTime);

        this.cameraTouchInput?.update?.(deltaTime);

        this.touchJoystick?.update?.(deltaTime);

    }

    enable() {

        this.enabled = true;

    }

    disable() {

        this.enabled = false;

    }

    isEnabled() {

        return this.enabled;

    }

    getState() {
        return this.inputState;
    }

    getMovement() {

    if (!this.inputState) {

        return null;

    }


    /*
     * ==================================================
     * JOYSTICK
     * ==================================================
     */

    const joystickX =
        Number(
            this.inputState.joystickX
        ) || 0;


    const joystickY =
        Number(
            this.inputState.joystickY
        ) || 0;


    /*
     * Le joystick est prioritaire
     * lorsqu'il est utilisé.
     */

    const joystickMagnitude =
        Math.hypot(
            joystickX,
            joystickY
        );


    if (
        joystickMagnitude > 0.05
    ) {

        return {

            forward:
                -joystickY,

            right:
                joystickX

        };

    }


    /*
     * ==================================================
     * CLAVIER
     * ==================================================
     *
     * On conserve entièrement
     * le fonctionnement PC.
     */

    return {

        forward:
            (
                this.inputState.moveForward
                    ? 1
                    : 0
            ) -
            (
                this.inputState.moveBackward
                    ? 1
                    : 0
            ),

        right:
    (
        this.inputState.moveRight
            ? 1
            : 0
    ) -
    (
        this.inputState.moveLeft
            ? 1
            : 0
    )

    };

}

    reset() {
        this.inputState?.reset?.();
    }

}

export class InputState {

    constructor() {

        this.reset();

    }

    reset() {

        this.moveForward = false;
        this.moveBackward = false;
        this.moveLeft = false;
        this.moveRight = false;

        this.jump = false;
        this.sprint = false;
        this.crouch = false;

        this.interact = false;
        this.attack = false;

        this.lookX = 0;
        this.lookY = 0;

        this.joystickX = 0;
        this.joystickY = 0;

    }

    clearFrameInput() {

        this.jump = false;
        this.interact = false;
        this.attack = false;

        this.lookX = 0;
        this.lookY = 0;

    }

    setMovement(forward, backward, left, right) {

        this.moveForward = forward;
        this.moveBackward = backward;
        this.moveLeft = left;
        this.moveRight = right;

    }

    setJoystick(x, y) {

        this.joystickX = x;
        this.joystickY = y;

    }

    setLook(x, y) {

        this.lookX = x;
        this.lookY = y;

    }

}

export class KeyboardInput {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.enabled = true;

        this.state = null;

        this.keys = new Set();

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.state = this.game.inputState;

        window.addEventListener(

            "keydown",

            this.onKeyDown.bind(this)

        );

        window.addEventListener(

            "keyup",

            this.onKeyUp.bind(this)

        );


        console.log("KeyboardInput initialisé.");

    }

    onKeyDown(event) {

        if (!this.enabled) {

            return;

        }

        this.keys.add(event.code);

        this.updateMovement();

    }

    onKeyUp(event) {

        this.keys.delete(event.code);

        this.updateMovement();

    }

    

    updateMovement() {

        this.state?.setMovement(
    this.keys.has("KeyW") ||
    this.keys.has("ArrowUp"),

    this.keys.has("KeyS") ||
    this.keys.has("ArrowDown"),

    this.keys.has("KeyA") ||
    this.keys.has("ArrowLeft"),

    this.keys.has("KeyD") ||
    this.keys.has("ArrowRight")
);

        this.state.jump = this.keys.has("Space");

        this.state.sprint =

            this.keys.has("ShiftLeft") ||

            this.keys.has("ShiftRight");

        this.state.crouch =

            this.keys.has("ControlLeft") ||

            this.keys.has("ControlRight");

        this.state.interact =

            this.keys.has("KeyE");

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        // Réservé pour les traitements continus

    }

    setEnabled(enabled) {

        this.enabled = enabled;

    }

}

export class TouchInput {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.enabled = true;

        this.inputState = null;

        this.activeTouches = new Map();

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.inputState = this.game.inputState;

        window.addEventListener(

            "touchstart",

            this.onTouchStart.bind(this),

            { passive: false }

        );

        window.addEventListener(

            "touchmove",

            this.onTouchMove.bind(this),

            { passive: false }

        );

        window.addEventListener(

            "touchend",

            this.onTouchEnd.bind(this),

            { passive: false }

        );

        window.addEventListener(

            "touchcancel",

            this.onTouchEnd.bind(this),

            { passive: false }

        );

        console.log("TouchInput initialisé.");

    }

    onTouchStart(event) {

        if (!this.enabled) {

            return;

        }

        for (const touch of event.changedTouches) {

            this.activeTouches.set(

                touch.identifier,

                {

                    x: touch.clientX,

                    y: touch.clientY

                }

            );

        }

    }

    onTouchMove(event) {

        if (!this.enabled) {

            return;

        }

        for (const touch of event.changedTouches) {

            if (!this.activeTouches.has(touch.identifier)) {

                continue;

            }

            this.activeTouches.set(

                touch.identifier,

                {

                    x: touch.clientX,

                    y: touch.clientY

                }

            );

        }

    }

    onTouchEnd(event) {

        for (const touch of event.changedTouches) {

            this.activeTouches.delete(

                touch.identifier

            );

        }

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        // Les gestes tactiles seront interprétés
        // par TouchJoystick et CameraTouchInput.

    }

    setEnabled(enabled) {

        this.enabled = enabled;

    }

    isEnabled() {

        return this.enabled;

    }

}

export class TouchJoystick {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.enabled = true;

        this.inputState = null;

        this.pointerId = null;

        this.centerX = 0;
        this.centerY = 0;

        this.radius = 70;

        this.currentX = 0;
        this.currentY = 0;

        this.base = null;
        this.knob = null;

    }


    async initialize() {

        this.inputState =
            this.game.inputState;


        this.createUI();

        this.installInput();


        console.log(
            "TouchJoystick initialisé."
        );

    }


    createUI() {

        if (
            document.getElementById(
                "islamcraft-touch-joystick"
            )
        ) {

            return;

        }


        /*
         * CONTENEUR DU JOYSTICK
         */

        const base =
            document.createElement(
                "div"
            );


        base.id =
            "islamcraft-touch-joystick";


        base.style.position =
            "fixed";

        base.style.left =
            "28px";

        base.style.bottom =
            "28px";

        base.style.width =
            "150px";

        base.style.height =
            "150px";

        base.style.borderRadius =
            "50%";

        base.style.background =
            "rgba(255,255,255,0.18)";

        base.style.border =
            "3px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.45)";

        base.style.boxSizing =
            "border-box";

        base.style.zIndex =
            "900";

        base.style.touchAction =
            "none";

        base.style.userSelect =
            "none";

        base.style.webkitUserSelect =
            "none";


        /*
         * EMPÊCHER LES TOUCHES DU JOYSTICK
         * DE PASSER AUX AUTRES SYSTÈMES.
         */

        base.addEventListener(
            "pointerdown",
            event => {

                event.preventDefault();

                event.stopPropagation();

            }
        );


        /*
         * BOUTON CENTRAL
         */

        const knob =
            document.createElement(
                "div"
            );


        knob.style.position =
            "absolute";

        knob.style.left =
            "50%";

        knob.style.top =
            "50%";

        knob.style.width =
            "68px";

        knob.style.height =
            "68px";

        knob.style.transform =
            "translate(-50%, -50%)";

        knob.style.borderRadius =
            "50%";

        knob.style.background =
            "rgba(255,255,255,0.42)";

        knob.style.border =
            "3px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.75)";

        knob.style.boxSizing =
            "border-box";

        knob.style.pointerEvents =
            "none";


        base.appendChild(
            knob
        );


        document.body.appendChild(
            base
        );


        this.base =
            base;

        this.knob =
            knob;

    }


    installInput() {

        if (!this.base) {

            return;

        }


        this.base.addEventListener(
            "pointerdown",
            event => {

                if (!this.enabled) {

                    return;

                }


                if (
                    this.pointerId !== null
                ) {

                    return;

                }


                event.preventDefault();
                event.stopPropagation();


                this.pointerId =
                    event.pointerId;


                const rect =
                    this.base.getBoundingClientRect();


                this.centerX =
                    rect.left +
                    rect.width / 2;


                this.centerY =
                    rect.top +
                    rect.height / 2;


                this.currentX =
                    event.clientX;

                this.currentY =
                    event.clientY;


                try {

                    this.base.setPointerCapture(
                        event.pointerId
                    );

                } catch {

                    // Certains appareils
                    // peuvent ne pas supporter
                    // correctement setPointerCapture.

                }


                this.updateFromPointer(
                    event.clientX,
                    event.clientY
                );

            }
        );


        this.base.addEventListener(
            "pointermove",
            event => {

                if (
                    !this.enabled ||
                    event.pointerId !==
                        this.pointerId
                ) {

                    return;

                }


                event.preventDefault();
                event.stopPropagation();


                this.updateFromPointer(
                    event.clientX,
                    event.clientY
                );

            }
        );


        const endPointer =
            event => {

                if (
                    event.pointerId !==
                    this.pointerId
                ) {

                    return;

                }


                event.preventDefault();
                event.stopPropagation();


                this.end(
                    event.pointerId
                );

            };


        this.base.addEventListener(
            "pointerup",
            endPointer
        );


        this.base.addEventListener(
            "pointercancel",
            endPointer
        );


        this.base.addEventListener(
            "lostpointercapture",
            event => {

                if (
                    event.pointerId ===
                    this.pointerId
                ) {

                    this.end(
                        event.pointerId
                    );

                }

            }
        );

    }


    updateFromPointer(
        x,
        y
    ) {

        const dx =
            x -
            this.centerX;


        const dy =
            y -
            this.centerY;


        const distance =
            Math.hypot(
                dx,
                dy
            );


        let normalizedX =
            0;

        let normalizedY =
            0;


        if (
            distance > 0
        ) {

            const limitedDistance =
                Math.min(
                    distance,
                    this.radius
                );


            const factor =
                limitedDistance /
                distance;


            normalizedX =
                (
                    dx *
                    factor
                ) /
                this.radius;


            normalizedY =
                (
                    dy *
                    factor
                ) /
                this.radius;

        }


        this.currentX =
            x;

        this.currentY =
            y;


        /*
         * Mise à jour de l'état global.
         */

        this.inputState?.setJoystick(
            normalizedX,
            normalizedY
        );


        /*
         * Déplacer visuellement
         * le bouton central.
         */

        if (this.knob) {

            const knobX =
                normalizedX *
                this.radius;


            const knobY =
                normalizedY *
                this.radius;


            this.knob.style.transform =
                `translate(calc(-50% + ${knobX}px), calc(-50% + ${knobY}px))`;

        }

    }


    end(pointerId) {

        if (
            pointerId !==
            this.pointerId
        ) {

            return;

        }


        this.pointerId =
            null;


        this.inputState?.setJoystick(
            0,
            0
        );


        /*
         * Replacer le bouton
         * au centre.
         */

        if (this.knob) {

            this.knob.style.transform =
                "translate(-50%, -50%)";

        }

    }


    update(deltaTime) {

        /*
         * Rien à faire ici pour le moment.
         *
         * Le joystick fonctionne
         * directement avec les événements
         * pointer.
         */

    }


    setEnabled(enabled) {

        this.enabled =
            Boolean(enabled);


        if (!this.enabled) {

            this.end(
                this.pointerId
            );

        }


        if (this.base) {

            this.base.style.display =
                this.enabled
                    ? "block"
                    : "none";

        }

    }


    isEnabled() {

        return this.enabled;

    }

} 
.

CHEMIN : C:\Users\hp\Downloads\ISLAMCRAFT V2.0\client\src\network\ClientPrediction.js 
========================================== 
export class ClientPrediction {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.enabled = true;

        this.pendingInputs = [];

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("ClientPrediction initialisé.");

    }

    addInput(input) {

        if (!this.enabled) {

            return;

        }

        this.pendingInputs.push(input);

    }

    predict(player, deltaTime) {

        if (!this.enabled || !player) {

            return;

        }

        for (const input of this.pendingInputs) {

            player.applyInput?.(

                input,

                deltaTime

            );

        }

    }

    acknowledge(sequence) {

        this.pendingInputs = this.pendingInputs.filter(

            input => input.sequence > sequence

        );

    }

    clear() {

        this.pendingInputs.length = 0;

    }

        // La prédiction est exécutée
        // lors de la simulation locale.

    }

    setEnabled(enabled) {

        this.enabled = enabled;

    }

    isEnabled() {

        return this.enabled;

    }

export class ClientReconciliation {

        this.enabled = true;

        this.lastAcknowledgedSequence = 0;

            this.lastAcknowledgedSequence

        );

    }

    reset() {

        this.lastAcknowledgedSequence = 0;

    }

        // Les corrections sont appliquées
        // à la réception des snapshots serveur.

    }

    setEnabled(enabled) {

        this.enabled = enabled;

    }

        return this.enabled;

export class ConnectionManager {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.connected = false;

        this.connecting = false;

        this.serverUrl = "";

        this.retryCount = 0;

        this.maxRetries = 5;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("ConnectionManager initialisé.");

    }

    async connect(serverUrl) {

        if (this.connected || this.connecting) {

            return;

        }

        this.serverUrl = serverUrl;

        this.connecting = true;

        try {

            await this.game.socketClient?.connect?.(

                serverUrl

            );

            this.connected = true;

            this.retryCount = 0;

            this.game.eventBus?.emit(

                "network:connected"

            );

        } catch (error) {

            this.retryCount++;

            this.game.eventBus?.emit(

                "network:connection-failed",

                error

            );

        } finally {

            this.connecting = false;

        }

    }

    disconnect() {

        this.game.socketClient?.disconnect?.();

        this.connected = false;

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "network:disconnected"

        );

    }

    reconnect() {

        if (

            this.retryCount >= this.maxRetries ||

            !this.serverUrl

        ) {

            return;

        }

        this.connect(

            this.serverUrl

        );

    }

    isConnected() {

        return this.connected;

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        // Réservé :
        // heartbeat
        // reconnexion automatique
        // timeout

    }

}

CHEMIN : C:\Users\hp\Downloads\ISLAMCRAFT V2.0\client\src\network\InputBuffer.js

export class InputBuffer {

    constructor() {

        this.inputs = [];

        this.nextSequence = 1;

        this.maxSize = 256;

    }

    add(input) {

        const bufferedInput = {

            ...input,

            sequence: this.nextSequence++

        };

        this.inputs.push(bufferedInput);

        if (this.inputs.length > this.maxSize) {

            this.inputs.shift();

        }

        return bufferedInput;

    }

    removeUntil(sequence) {

        this.inputs = this.inputs.filter(

            input => input.sequence > sequence

        );

    }

    getPendingInputs() {

        return [...this.inputs];

    }

    getLastSequence() {

        return this.nextSequence - 1;

    }

    clear() {

        this.inputs.length = 0;

        this.nextSequence = 1;

    }

    size() {

        return this.inputs.length;

    }

    isEmpty() {

        return this.inputs.length === 0;

    }

}

export class NetworkClock {

    constructor() {

        this.serverTime = 0;

        this.localTime = 0;

        this.timeOffset = 0;

        this.latency = 0;

        this.lastSyncTime = 0;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("NetworkClock initialisé.");

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        this.localTime += deltaTime;

    }

    synchronize(serverTimestamp, roundTripTime = 0) {

        this.latency = roundTripTime * 0.5;

        this.serverTime = serverTimestamp;

        this.timeOffset =

            (serverTimestamp + this.latency)

            - this.localTime;

        this.lastSyncTime = this.localTime;

    }

    getServerTime() {

        return this.localTime + this.timeOffset;

    }

    getLocalTime() {

        return this.localTime;

    }

    getLatency() {

        return this.latency;

    }

    getTimeOffset() {

        return this.timeOffset;

    }

    reset() {

        this.serverTime = 0;

        this.localTime = 0;

        this.timeOffset = 0;

        this.latency = 0;

        this.lastSyncTime = 0;

    }

}

export class NetworkManager {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.connected = false;

        this.socketClient = null;

        this.connectionManager = null;

        this.networkClock = null;

        this.networkMetrics = null;

        this.snapshotBuffer = null;

        this.clientPrediction = null;

        this.clientReconciliation = null;

        this.remoteInterpolation = null;

        this.remoteExtrapolation = null;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.socketClient = this.game.socketClient;

        this.connectionManager = this.game.connectionManager;

        this.networkClock = this.game.networkClock;

        this.networkMetrics = this.game.networkMetrics;

        this.snapshotBuffer = this.game.snapshotBuffer;

        this.clientPrediction = this.game.clientPrediction;

        this.clientReconciliation = this.game.clientReconciliation;

        this.remoteInterpolation = this.game.remoteInterpolation;

        this.remoteExtrapolation = this.game.remoteExtrapolation;

        console.log("NetworkManager initialisé.");

    }

    async connect(serverUrl) {

        await this.connectionManager?.connect?.(

            serverUrl

        );

        this.connected =

            this.connectionManager?.isConnected?.() ?? false;

    }

    disconnect() {

        this.connectionManager?.disconnect?.();

        this.connected = false;

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        if (!this.connected) {

            return;

        }

        this.networkClock?.update?.(

            deltaTime

        );

        this.networkMetrics?.update?.(

            deltaTime

        );

        this.socketClient?.update?.(

            deltaTime

        );

        this.snapshotBuffer?.update?.(

            deltaTime

        );

        this.clientPrediction?.update?.(

            deltaTime

        );

        this.clientReconciliation?.update?.(

            deltaTime

        );

        this.remoteInterpolation?.update?.(

            deltaTime

        );

        this.remoteExtrapolation?.update?.(

            deltaTime

        );

    }

    isConnected() {

        return this.connected;

    }

}

export class NetworkMetrics {

    constructor() {

        this.ping = 0;

        this.jitter = 0;

        this.packetLoss = 0;

        this.bytesSent = 0;

        this.bytesReceived = 0;

        this.lastPingTime = 0;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("NetworkMetrics initialisé.");

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        this.lastPingTime += deltaTime;

    }

    setPing(ping) {

        this.ping = Math.max(

            0,

            ping

        );

    }

    setJitter(jitter) {

        this.jitter = Math.max(

            0,

            jitter

        );

    }

    setPacketLoss(packetLoss) {

        this.packetLoss = Math.max(

            0,

            Math.min(

                packetLoss,

                100

            )

        );

    }

    addBytesSent(bytes) {

        this.bytesSent += Math.max(

            0,

            bytes

        );

    }

    addBytesReceived(bytes) {

        this.bytesReceived += Math.max(

            0,

            bytes

        );

    }

    getStats() {

        return {

            ping: this.ping,

            jitter: this.jitter,

            packetLoss: this.packetLoss,

            bytesSent: this.bytesSent,

            bytesReceived: this.bytesReceived

        };

    }

    reset() {

        this.ping = 0;

        this.jitter = 0;

        this.packetLoss = 0;

        this.bytesSent = 0;

        this.bytesReceived = 0;

        this.lastPingTime = 0;

    }

}

export class RemoteExtrapolation {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.enabled = true;

        this.maxExtrapolationTime = 0.25;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("RemoteExtrapolation initialisé.");

    }

    extrapolate(entity, deltaTime) {

        if (

            !this.enabled ||

            !entity

        ) {

            return;

        }

        const velocity = entity.velocity;

        if (!velocity) {

            return;

        }

        const time = Math.min(

            deltaTime,

            this.maxExtrapolationTime

        );

        entity.position.x += velocity.x * time;

        entity.position.y += velocity.y * time;

        entity.position.z += velocity.z * time;

    }

    extrapolateAll(entities, deltaTime) {

        if (!Array.isArray(entities)) {

            return;

        }

        for (const entity of entities) {

            this.extrapolate(

                entity,

                deltaTime

            );

        }

    }

    setEnabled(enabled) {

        this.enabled = enabled;

    }

    isEnabled() {

        return this.enabled;

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        // Les extrapolations sont appliquées
        // après la dernière mise à jour réseau.

    }

}

export class RemoteInterpolation {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.enabled = true;

        this.interpolationDelay = 100;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("RemoteInterpolation initialisé.");

    }

    interpolate(entity, alpha) {

        if (

            !this.enabled ||

            !entity ||

            !entity.previousSnapshot ||

            !entity.nextSnapshot

        ) {

            return;

        }

        const previous = entity.previousSnapshot;

        const next = entity.nextSnapshot;

        entity.position.x = previous.position.x +

            (next.position.x - previous.position.x) * alpha;

        entity.position.y = previous.position.y +

            (next.position.y - previous.position.y) * alpha;

        entity.position.z = previous.position.z +

            (next.position.z - previous.position.z) * alpha;

        entity.rotation.x = previous.rotation.x +

            (next.rotation.x - previous.rotation.x) * alpha;

        entity.rotation.y = previous.rotation.y +

            (next.rotation.y - previous.rotation.y) * alpha;

        entity.rotation.z = previous.rotation.z +

            (next.rotation.z - previous.rotation.z) * alpha;

    }

    interpolateAll(entities, alpha) {

        if (!Array.isArray(entities)) {

            return;

        }

        for (const entity of entities) {

            this.interpolate(

                entity,

                alpha

            );

        }

    }

    setInterpolationDelay(milliseconds) {

        this.interpolationDelay = Math.max(

            0,

            milliseconds

        );

    }

    getInterpolationDelay() {

        return this.interpolationDelay;

    }

    setEnabled(enabled) {

        this.enabled = enabled;

    }

    isEnabled() {

        return this.enabled;

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        // L'interpolation est déclenchée
        // par NetworkManager à chaque frame.

    }

}

export class SnapshotBuffer {

    constructor() {

        this.snapshots = [];

        this.maxSnapshots = 64;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("SnapshotBuffer initialisé.");

    }

    add(snapshot) {

        if (!snapshot) {

            return;

        }

        this.snapshots.push(snapshot);

        if (this.snapshots.length > this.maxSnapshots) {

            this.snapshots.shift();

        }

    }

    getLatest() {

        if (this.snapshots.length === 0) {

            return null;

        }

        return this.snapshots[this.snapshots.length - 1];

    }

    getPrevious() {

        if (this.snapshots.length < 2) {

            return null;

        }

        return this.snapshots[this.snapshots.length - 2];

    }

    getAll() {

        return [...this.snapshots];

    }

    clear() {

        this.snapshots.length = 0;

    }

    size() {

        return this.snapshots.length;

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        // Les snapshots sont consommés par
        // RemoteInterpolation et
        // RemoteExtrapolation.

    }

}

export class SocketClient {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.socket = null;

        this.connected = false;

        this.serverUrl = "";

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("SocketClient initialisé.");

    }

    async connect(serverUrl) {

        if (this.connected) {

            return;

        }

        this.serverUrl = serverUrl;

        if (!serverUrl) {

            throw new Error(

                "URL du serveur manquante."

            );

        }

        if (typeof WebSocket === "undefined") {

            throw new Error(

                "WebSocket n'est pas disponible."

            );

        }

        await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {

            const socket = new WebSocket(serverUrl);

            this.socket = socket;

            socket.addEventListener(

                "open",

                () => {

                    this.connected = true;

                    this.game.eventBus?.emit(

                        "socket:connected"

                    );

                    resolve();

                },

                { once: true }

            );

            socket.addEventListener(

                "message",

                event => {

                    this.handleMessage(

                        event.data

                    );

                }

            );

            socket.addEventListener(

                "close",

                () => {

                    this.connected = false;

                    this.game.eventBus?.emit(

                        "socket:disconnected"

                    );

                }

            );

            socket.addEventListener(

                "error",

                error => {

                    this.connected = false;

                    this.game.eventBus?.emit(

                        "socket:error",

                        error

                    );

                    reject(error);

                },

                { once: true }

            );

        });

    }

    disconnect() {

        if (!this.socket) {

            return;

        }

        this.socket.close();

        this.socket = null;

        this.connected = false;

    }

    send(type, data = null) {

        if (

            !this.connected ||

            !this.socket

        ) {

            return false;

        }

        const message = JSON.stringify({

            type,

            data

        });

        this.socket.send(message);

        return true;

    }

    handleMessage(rawMessage) {

        let message;

        try {

            message = JSON.parse(rawMessage);

        } catch {

            return;

        }

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "network:message",

            message

        );

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        // La communication est gérée
        // par les événements WebSocket.

    }

    isConnected() {

        return this.connected;

    }

} 
.

export class ResponsiveLayout {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.width = 0;

        this.height = 0;

        this.orientation = "landscape";

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.update();

        window.addEventListener(

            "resize",

            () => this.update()

        );

        console.log("ResponsiveLayout initialisé.");

    }

    update() {

        this.width = window.innerWidth;

        this.height = window.innerHeight;

        this.orientation =

            this.width >= this.height

                ? "landscape"

                : "portrait";

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "ui:layout-changed",

            {

                width: this.width,

                height: this.height,

                orientation: this.orientation

            }

        );

    }

    getWidth() {

        return this.width;

    }

    getHeight() {

        return this.height;

    }

    getOrientation() {

        return this.orientation;

    }

    isPortrait() {

        return this.orientation === "portrait";

    }

    isLandscape() {

        return this.orientation === "landscape";

    }

}

export class UIManager {
    constructor(game) {
        this.game = game;

        this.components = new Map();

        this.initialized = false;
    }

    async initialize() {
        this.register(
            "hud",
            this.game.hud
        );

        this.register(
            "lobby",
            this.game.lobbyUI
        );

        this.register(
            "minimap",
            this.game.minimap
        );

        this.register(
            "victory",
            this.game.victoryScreen
        );

        this.register(
            "defeat",
            this.game.defeatScreen
        );

        this.register(
            "loading",
            this.game.loadingScreen
        );

        this.register(
            "restart",
            this.game.restartScreen
        );

        this.initialized = true;

        console.log(
            "UIManager initialisé."
        );
    }

    register(name, component) {
        if (!name || !component) {
            return;
        }

        this.components.set(
            name,
            component
        );
    }

    unregister(name) {
        this.components.delete(name);
    }

    get(name) {
        return (
            this.components.get(name) ??
            null
        );
    }

    show(name, data = null) {
        const component =
            this.get(name);

        component?.show?.(data);
    }

    hide(name) {
        const component =
            this.get(name);

        component?.hide?.();
    }

    update(deltaTime) {
        for (
            const component
            of this.components.values()
        ) {
            component?.update?.(
                deltaTime
            );
        }
    }

    showGameplay() {
        this.show("hud");

        this.game.minimap?.show?.();

        this.game.interactionPrompt?.show?.();
    }

    hideGameplay() {
        this.hide("hud");

        this.game.minimap?.hide?.();

        this.game.interactionPrompt?.hide?.();

        this.game.lootPresentation?.clear?.();
    }

            hud.get?.("score");

            scoreHUD &&
            typeof scoreHUD.setScore ===
                "function"
        ) {
            scoreHUD.setScore(
                score
            );
        }

        this.game.eventBus?.emit?.(
            "hud:score-updated",
            {
                score,
                bestScore
            }
        );
    }

        currentXP,

            this.game.hud;

        if (!hud) {
            return;
        }

            hud.get?.("xp");

        if (
            xpBar &&
            typeof xpBar.setXP ===
                "function"
        ) {
            xpBar.setXP(
                currentXP,
                requiredXP
            );
        }
    }

    updateLevel(level) {
        const hud =
            this.game.hud;

        if (!hud) {
            return;
        }

        const levelDisplay =
            hud.get?.("level");

        if (
            levelDisplay &&
            typeof levelDisplay.setLevel ===
                "function"
        ) {
            levelDisplay.setLevel(
                level
            );
        }
    }

    updatePlayerName(name) {
        const hud =
            this.game.hud;

        if (!hud) {
            return;
        }

        const playerNameHUD =
            hud.get?.("playerName");

        if (
            playerNameHUD &&
            typeof playerNameHUD.setPlayerName ===
                "function"
        ) {
            playerNameHUD.setPlayerName(
                name
            );
        }
    }

    showLootReward(reward) {
        const rewardPopup =
            this.game.rewardPopup;

        rewardPopup?.show?.(
            reward
        );
    }

    hideLootReward() {
        const rewardPopup =
            this.game.rewardPopup;

        rewardPopup?.hide?.();
    }

    showVictoryScreen(data = null) {
        this.show(
            "victory",
            data
        );
    }

    hideVictoryScreen() {
        this.hide(
            "victory"
        );
    }

    showDefeatScreen(data = null) {
        this.show(
            "defeat",
            data
        );

        this.hide(
            "defeat"
        );

    showRestartScreen(data = null) {
        this.show(
            "restart",
            data
        );

    hideRestartScreen() {
        this.hide(
            "restart"
        );

        this.show(

    hideLobby() {

export class LootAnimation {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.active = false;

        this.elapsedTime = 0;

        this.duration = 1000;

        this.reward = null;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("LootAnimation initialisée.");

    }

    play(reward = null) {

        this.reward = reward;

        this.elapsedTime = 0;

        this.active = true;

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "loot:animation-started",

            reward

        );

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        if (!this.active) {

            return;

        }

        this.elapsedTime += deltaTime;

        if (this.elapsedTime >= this.duration) {

            this.finish();

        }

    }

    finish() {

        if (!this.active) {

            return;

        }

        this.active = false;

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "loot:animation-finished",

            this.reward

        );

    }

    stop() {

        this.active = false;

        this.elapsedTime = 0;

        this.reward = null;

    }

    isPlaying() {

        return this.active;

    }

    getProgress() {

        if (this.duration <= 0) {

            return 1;

        }

        return Math.min(

            this.elapsedTime / this.duration,

            1

        );

    }

} 
.

export class LootPanel {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.visible = false;

        this.rewards = [];

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("LootPanel initialisé.");

    }

    show(rewards = []) {

        this.rewards = Array.isArray(rewards)

            ? [...rewards]

            : [];

        this.visible = true;

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "loot:panel-opened",

            this.rewards

        );

    }

    hide() {

        this.visible = false;

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "loot:panel-closed"

        );

    }

    setRewards(rewards) {

        this.rewards = Array.isArray(rewards)

            ? [...rewards]

            : [];

    }

    getRewards() {

        return [...this.rewards];

    }

    isVisible() {

        return this.visible;

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        if (!this.visible) {

            return;

        }

        // Mise à jour de l'affichage du contenu du coffre.

    }

}

export class RewardPopup {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.visible = false;

        this.reward = null;

        this.elapsedTime = 0;

        this.duration = 3000;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("RewardPopup initialisé.");

    }

    show(reward) {

        this.reward = reward;

        this.elapsedTime = 0;

        this.visible = true;

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "reward-popup:shown",

            reward

        );

    }

    hide() {

        this.visible = false;

        this.reward = null;

        this.elapsedTime = 0;

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        if (!this.visible) {

            return;

        }

        this.elapsedTime += deltaTime;

        if (this.elapsedTime >= this.duration) {

            this.hide();

        }

    }

    setDuration(duration) {

        this.duration = Math.max(

            0,

            duration

        );

    }

    getReward() {

        return this.reward;

    }

    isVisible() {

        return this.visible;

    }

}

CHEMIN : C:\Users\hp\Downloads\ISLAMCRAFT V2.0\client\src\ui\hud\HUD.js 
========================================== 
export class HUD {
    constructor(game) {
        this.game = game;

        this.visible = true;
        this.components = new Map();

        this.container = null;
        this.playerNameElement = null;
        this.scoreElement = null;
        this.levelElement = null;
        this.xpTextElement = null;
        this.xpFillElement = null;

        this.boundHandlers = [];
    }

    async initialize() {
        this.createDOM();
        this.bindEvents();
        this.refreshFromComponents();

        console.log("HUD initialisé.");
    }

    createDOM() {
        if (this.container) {
            return;
        }

        const container = document.createElement("div");

        container.id = "islamcraft-hud";

        Object.assign(container.style, {
            position: "fixed",
            top: "0",
            left: "0",
            width: "100%",
            height: "100%",
            pointerEvents: "none",
            zIndex: "1000",
            fontFamily:
                "Arial, Helvetica, sans-serif",
            boxSizing: "border-box"
        });

        const panel = document.createElement("div");

        Object.assign(panel.style, {
            position: "absolute",
            top: "16px",
            left: "16px",
            minWidth: "230px",
            padding: "12px 14px",
            borderRadius: "14px",
            background:
                "rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.58)",
            border:
                "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.18)",
            color: "#ffffff",
            boxSizing: "border-box",
            backdropFilter: "blur(6px)",
            WebkitBackdropFilter: "blur(6px)",
            textShadow:
                "0 1px 3px rgba(0,0,0,0.8)"
        });

        const playerName = document.createElement("div");

        Object.assign(playerName.style, {
            fontSize: "18px",
            fontWeight: "700",
            marginBottom: "8px",
            whiteSpace: "nowrap",
            overflow: "hidden",
            textOverflow: "ellipsis",
            maxWidth: "300px"
        });

        playerName.textContent = "Joueur";

        const score = document.createElement("div");

        Object.assign(score.style, {
            fontSize: "16px",
            fontWeight: "600",
            marginBottom: "4px"
        });

        score.textContent = "Score : 0";

        const level = document.createElement("div");

        Object.assign(level.style, {
            fontSize: "15px",
            fontWeight: "600",
            marginBottom: "8px"
        });

        level.textContent = "Niveau : 1";

        const xpText = document.createElement("div");

        Object.assign(xpText.style, {
            fontSize: "13px",
            fontWeight: "600",
            marginBottom: "5px"
        });

        xpText.textContent = "XP : 0 / 100";

        const xpBackground =
            document.createElement("div");

        Object.assign(xpBackground.style, {
            width: "100%",
            height: "10px",
            borderRadius: "999px",
            background:
                "rgba(255,255,255,0.18)",
            overflow: "hidden"
        });

        const xpFill =
            document.createElement("div");

        Object.assign(xpFill.style, {
            width: "0%",
            height: "100%",
            borderRadius: "999px",
            background:
                "linear-gradient(90deg, #d4af37, #f4d675)",
            transition:
                "width 180ms ease-out"
        });

        xpBackground.appendChild(xpFill);

        panel.appendChild(playerName);
        panel.appendChild(score);
        panel.appendChild(level);
        panel.appendChild(xpText);
        panel.appendChild(xpBackground);

        container.appendChild(panel);

        document.body.appendChild(container);

        this.container = container;
        this.playerNameElement = playerName;
        this.scoreElement = score;
        this.levelElement = level;
        this.xpTextElement = xpText;
        this.xpFillElement = xpFill;

        this.applyVisibility();
    }

    bindEvents() {
        const eventBus = this.game?.eventBus;

        if (!eventBus?.on) {
            return;
        }

        const events = [
            [
                "hud:player-name-changed",
                value => {
                    this.updatePlayerName(value);
                }
            ],
            [
                "hud:score-changed",
                value => {
                    this.updateScore(value);
                }
            ],
            [
                "hud:level-changed",
                value => {
                    this.updateLevel(value);
                }
            ],
            [
                "hud:xp-changed",
                value => {
                    this.updateXP(value);
                }
            ]
        ];

        for (const [eventName, handler] of events) {
            eventBus.on(eventName, handler);
            this.boundHandlers.push([
                eventName,
                handler
            ]);
        }
    }

    unregisterEvents() {
        const eventBus = this.game?.eventBus;

        if (!eventBus) {
            return;
        }

        for (const [eventName, handler] of this.boundHandlers) {
            if (typeof eventBus.off === "function") {
                eventBus.off(
                    eventName,
                    handler
                );
            }

            if (typeof eventBus.removeListener === "function") {
                eventBus.removeListener(
                    eventName,
                    handler
                );
            }
        }

        this.boundHandlers.length = 0;
    }

    refreshFromComponents() {
        const playerName =
            this.get("playerName") ??
            this.get("player-name");

        const score =
            this.get("score");

        const level =
            this.get("level");

        const xp =
            this.get("xp");

        if (playerName?.getPlayerName) {
            this.updatePlayerName(
                playerName.getPlayerName()
            );
        }

        if (score?.getScore) {
            this.updateScore(
                score.getScore()
            );
        }

        if (level?.getLevel) {
            this.updateLevel(
                level.getLevel()
            );
        }

        if (xp?.getXP) {
            this.updateXP({
                currentXP: xp.getXP(),
                requiredXP:
                    xp.getRequiredXP?.() ?? 100
            });
        }
    }

    updatePlayerName(name) {
        if (!this.playerNameElement) {
            return;
        }

        const value =
            typeof name === "string" &&
            name.trim().length > 0
                ? name.trim()
                : "Joueur";

        this.playerNameElement.textContent =
            value;
    }

    updateScore(score) {
        if (!this.scoreElement) {
            return;
        }

        const value = Math.max(
            0,
            Number(score) || 0
        );

        this.scoreElement.textContent =
            `Score : ${value}`;
    }

    updateLevel(level) {
        if (!this.levelElement) {
            return;
        }

        const value = Math.max(
            1,
            Number(level) || 1
        );

        this.levelElement.textContent =
            `Niveau : ${value}`;
    }

    updateXP(data) {
        if (!this.xpTextElement || !this.xpFillElement) {
            return;
        }

        const currentXP = Math.max(
            0,
            Number(data?.currentXP) || 0
        );

        const requiredXP = Math.max(
            1,
            Number(data?.requiredXP) || 1
        );

        const progress = Math.max(
            0,
            Math.min(
                1,
                currentXP / requiredXP
            )
        );

        this.xpTextElement.textContent =
            `XP : ${currentXP} / ${requiredXP}`;

        this.xpFillElement.style.width =
            `${progress * 100}%`;
    }

    register(name, component) {
        if (!name || !component) {
            return;
        }

        this.components.set(
            name,
            component
        );

        this.refreshFromComponents();
    }

    unregister(name) {
        this.components.delete(name);
    }

    get(name) {
        return (
            this.components.get(name) ??
            null
        );
    }

    show() {
        this.visible = true;
        this.applyVisibility();

        for (const component of this.components.values()) {
            component.show?.();
        }
    }

    hide() {
        this.visible = false;
        this.applyVisibility();

        for (const component of this.components.values()) {
            component.hide?.();
        }
    }

    applyVisibility() {
        if (!this.container) {
            return;
        }

        this.container.style.display =
            this.visible
                ? "block"
                : "none";
    }

    update(deltaTime) {
        if (!this.visible) {
            return;
        }

        for (const component of this.components.values()) {
            component.update?.(deltaTime);
        }
    }

    isVisible() {
        return this.visible;
    }

    destroy() {
        this.unregisterEvents();

        for (const component of this.components.values()) {
            component.destroy?.();
        }

        this.components.clear();

        if (this.container?.parentNode) {
            this.container.parentNode.removeChild(
                this.container
            );
        }

        this.container = null;
        this.playerNameElement = null;
        this.scoreElement = null;
        this.levelElement = null;
        this.xpTextElement = null;
        this.xpFillElement = null;
    }
} 
.

export class XPBar {
    constructor(game) {
        this.game = game;

        this.visible = true;

        this.currentXP = 0;
        this.requiredXP = 100;

        this.container = null;
        this.textElement = null;
        this.fillElement = null;
    }

    async initialize() {
        this.createDOM();
        this.render();

        console.log("XPBar initialisée.");
    }

    createDOM() {
        if (this.container) {
            return;
        }

        const container =
            document.createElement("div");

        Object.assign(container.style, {
            position: "absolute",
            left: "50%",
            bottom: "18px",
            transform: "translateX(-50%)",
            width: "min(420px, 55vw)",
            pointerEvents: "none",
            zIndex: "1001",
            fontFamily:
                "Arial, Helvetica, sans-serif",
            color: "#ffffff",
            textShadow:
                "0 1px 3px rgba(0,0,0,0.8)"
        });

        const text =
            document.createElement("div");

        Object.assign(text.style, {
            textAlign: "center",
            fontSize: "13px",
            fontWeight: "700",
            marginBottom: "5px"
        });

        text.textContent = "XP : 0 / 100";

        const background =
            document.createElement("div");

        Object.assign(background.style, {
            width: "100%",
            height: "12px",
            borderRadius: "999px",
            overflow: "hidden",
            background:
                "rgba(0,0,0,0.58)",
            border:
                "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.2)"
        });

        const fill =
            document.createElement("div");

        Object.assign(fill.style, {
            width: "0%",
            height: "100%",
            borderRadius: "999px",
            background:
                "linear-gradient(90deg, #d4af37, #f4d675)",
            transition:
                "width 180ms ease-out"
        });

        background.appendChild(fill);

        container.appendChild(text);
        container.appendChild(background);

        document.body.appendChild(container);

        this.container = container;
        this.textElement = text;
        this.fillElement = fill;

        this.applyVisibility();
    }

    setXP(
        currentXP,
        requiredXP = this.requiredXP
    ) {
        this.currentXP = Math.max(
            0,
            Number(currentXP) || 0
        );

        this.requiredXP = Math.max(
            1,
            Number(requiredXP) || 1
        );

        this.render();

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(
            "hud:xp-changed",
            {
                currentXP: this.currentXP,
                requiredXP: this.requiredXP
            }
        );
    }

    addXP(amount) {
        this.setXP(
            this.currentXP +
            (Number(amount) || 0),
            this.requiredXP
        );
    }

    getProgress() {
        return Math.min(
            this.currentXP /
            this.requiredXP,
            1
        );
    }

    getXP() {
        return this.currentXP;
    }

    getRequiredXP() {
        return this.requiredXP;
    }

    render() {
        if (!this.textElement || !this.fillElement) {
            return;
        }

        const progress =
            this.getProgress();

        this.textElement.textContent =
            `XP : ${this.currentXP} / ${this.requiredXP}`;

        this.fillElement.style.width =
            `${progress * 100}%`;
    }

    show() {
        this.visible = true;
        this.applyVisibility();
    }

    hide() {
        this.visible = false;
        this.applyVisibility();
    }

    applyVisibility() {
        if (!this.container) {
            return;
        }

        this.container.style.display =
            this.visible
                ? "block"
                : "none";
    }

    isVisible() {
        return this.visible;
    }

    update(deltaTime) {
        if (!this.visible) {
            return;
        }
    }

    destroy() {
        if (this.container?.parentNode) {
            this.container.parentNode.removeChild(
                this.container
            );
        }

        this.container = null;
        this.textElement = null;
        this.fillElement = null;
    }
}

export class JoinButton {

    }

            return;

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "lobby:join-requested"

        );

        this.enabled = true;

    }
