        this.enabled = false;

    }

    show() {

        this.visible = true;

    }

    hide() {

        this.visible = false;

    }

    isEnabled() {

        return this.enabled;

    }

    isVisible() {

        return this.visible;

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        if (!this.visible) {

            return;

        }

    }

}

export class LobbyUI {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.visible = false;

        this.players = [];

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("LobbyUI initialisé.");

    }

    show() {

        this.visible = true;

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "lobby:ui-shown"

        );

    }

    hide() {

        this.visible = false;

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "lobby:ui-hidden"

        );

    }

    setPlayers(players) {

        this.players = Array.isArray(players)

            ? [...players]

            : [];

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "lobby:players-updated",

            this.players

        );

    }

    addPlayer(player) {

        if (!player) {

            return;

        }

        this.players.push(player);

    }

    removePlayer(playerId) {

        this.players = this.players.filter(

            player => player?.id !== playerId

        );

    }

    getPlayers() {

        return [...this.players];

    }

    isVisible() {

        return this.visible;

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        if (!this.visible) {

            return;

        }

    }

}

export class UsernameInput {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.value = "";

        this.enabled = true;

        this.visible = true;

        this.maxLength = 16;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("UsernameInput initialisé.");

    }

    setValue(value) {

        if (!this.enabled) {

            return;

        }

        this.value = String(value ?? "")

            .slice(0, this.maxLength);

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "lobby:username-changed",

            this.value

        );

    }

    getValue() {

        return this.value;

    }

    clear() {

        this.setValue("");

    }

    isValid() {

        const username = this.value.trim();

        return username.length >= 3;

    }

    enable() {

        this.enabled = true;

    }

    disable() {

        this.enabled = false;

    }

    show() {

        this.visible = true;

    }

export class Minimap {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.visible = true;

        this.zoom = 1;

        this.center = {

            x: 0,

            y: 0

        };

        this.markers = [];

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("Minimap initialisée.");

    }

    setCenter(x, y) {

        this.center.x = Number(x) || 0;

        this.center.y = Number(y) || 0;

    }

    getCenter() {

        return {

            x: this.center.x,

            y: this.center.y

        };

    }

    setZoom(zoom) {

        this.zoom = Math.max(

            0.1,

            Number(zoom) || 1

        );

    }

    getZoom() {

        return this.zoom;

    }

    setMarkers(markers) {

        this.markers = Array.isArray(markers)

            ? [...markers]

            : [];

    }

    addMarker(marker) {

        if (!marker) {

            return;

        }

        this.markers.push(marker);

    }

    removeMarker(markerId) {

        this.markers = this.markers.filter(

            marker => marker?.id !== markerId

        );

    getMarkers() {

        return [...this.markers];

    }

    show() {

        this.visible = true;

    }

    hide() {

        this.visible = false;

    }

    isVisible() {

        return this.visible;

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        if (!this.visible) {

    }

export class MinimapMarkers {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.markers = new Map();

        this.visible = true;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("MinimapMarkers initialisé.");

    }

    add(marker) {

        if (!marker || marker.id === undefined) {

            return;

        }

        this.markers.set(

            marker.id,

            { ...marker }

        );

    }

    remove(markerId) {

        this.markers.delete(markerId);

    }

    update(markerId, data) {

        if (!this.markers.has(markerId)) {

            return;

        }

        const marker = this.markers.get(markerId);

        this.markers.set(

            markerId,

            {

                ...marker,

                ...data

            }

        );

    }

    clear() {

        this.markers.clear();

    }

    get(markerId) {

        return this.markers.get(markerId) ?? null;

    }

    getAll() {

        return [...this.markers.values()];

    }

    show() {

        this.visible = true;

    }

    hide() {

        this.visible = false;

    }

    isVisible() {

        return this.visible;

    }

    updateDisplay(deltaTime) {

        if (!this.visible) {

            return;

        }

    }

}

export class MinimapPlayer {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.visible = true;

        this.position = {

            x: 0,

            y: 0

        };

        this.rotation = 0;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("MinimapPlayer initialisé.");

    }

    setPosition(x, y) {

        this.position.x = Number(x) || 0;

        this.position.y = Number(y) || 0;

    }

    setRotation(rotation) {

        this.rotation = Number(rotation) || 0;

    }

    updateFromPlayer(player) {

        if (!player) {

            return;

        }

        if (player.position) {

            this.setPosition(

                player.position.x,

                player.position.z

            );

        }

        if (player.rotation) {

            this.setRotation(

                player.rotation.y ?? 0

            );

        }

    }

    getPosition() {

        return {

            x: this.position.x,

            y: this.position.y

        };

    }

    getRotation() {

        return this.rotation;

    }

    show() {

        this.visible = true;

    }

    hide() {

        this.visible = false;

    }

    isVisible() {

        return this.visible;

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        if (!this.visible) {

            return;

        }

        this.updateFromPlayer(

            this.game.localPlayer

        );

    }

}

export class MinimapRenderer {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.visible = true;

        this.minimap = null;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.minimap = this.game.minimap ?? null;

        console.log("MinimapRenderer initialisé.");

    }

    render() {

        if (!this.visible || !this.minimap) {

            return;

        }

        const markers = this.game.minimapMarkers
            ?.getAll?.() ?? [];

        const player = this.game.minimapPlayer;

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "minimap:render",

            {

                center: this.minimap.getCenter?.(),

                zoom: this.minimap.getZoom?.(),

                player: player?.getPosition?.(),

                rotation: player?.getRotation?.(),

                markers

            }

        );

    }

    show() {

        this.visible = true;

    }

    hide() {

        this.visible = false;

    }

    isVisible() {

        return this.visible;

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        this.render();

    }

}

export class QuizTimer {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.visible = true;

        this.duration = 0;

        this.remaining = 0;

        this.running = false;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("QuizTimer initialisé.");

    }

    start(duration) {

        this.duration = Math.max(

            0,

            Number(duration) || 0

        );

        this.remaining = this.duration;

        this.running = true;

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "quiz:timer-started",

            this.remaining

        );

    }

    stop() {

        this.running = false;

    }

    reset() {

        this.remaining = this.duration;

        this.running = false;

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        if (!this.running) {

            return;

        }

        this.remaining = Math.max(

            0,

            this.remaining - deltaTime

        );

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "quiz:timer-updated",

            this.remaining

        );

        if (this.remaining <= 0) {

            this.running = false;

            this.game.eventBus?.emit(

                "quiz:timer-finished"

            );

        }

    }

    getRemaining() {

        return this.remaining;

    }

    getDuration() {

        return this.duration;

    }

    isRunning() {

        return this.running;

    }

    show() {

        this.visible = true;

    }

    hide() {

        this.visible = false;

    }

    isVisible() {

        return this.visible;

    }

}

CHEMIN : C:\Users\hp\Downloads\ISLAMCRAFT V2.0\client\src\world\WorldManager.js 
========================================== 
import { WorldSeed } from "./WorldSeed.js";
import { TerrainGenerator } from "./generation/TerrainGenerator.js";
import { StreamingRadius } from "./streaming/StreamingRadius.js";
import { ChunkManager } from "./chunks/ChunkManager.js";
import { WorldStreamer } from "./streaming/WorldStreamer.js";

export class WorldManager {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.seed = null;
        this.terrainGenerator = null;
        this.streamingRadius = null;
        this.chunkManager = null;
        this.streamer = null;

        this.initialized = false;
    }


    async initialize() {

        /*
         * SEED
         */

        this.seed =
            new WorldSeed(this.game);

        await this.seed.initialize();


        /*
         * GÉNÉRATION DU TERRAIN
         */

        this.terrainGenerator =
            new TerrainGenerator(this.game);

        await this.terrainGenerator.initialize();

        this.game.terrainGenerator =
            this.terrainGenerator;


        /*
         * DISTANCE DE STREAMING
         */

        this.streamingRadius =
            new StreamingRadius(this.game);

        await this.streamingRadius.initialize();

        this.game.streamingRadius =
            this.streamingRadius;


        /*
         * CHUNKS
         */

        this.chunkManager =
            new ChunkManager(this.game);

        await this.chunkManager.initialize();


        /*
         * STREAMING DU MONDE
         */

        this.streamer =
            new WorldStreamer(
                this.game,
                this.chunkManager
            );

        await this.streamer.initialize();


        this.initialized = true;

        console.log(
            "WorldManager initialisé."
        );
    }


    update(deltaTime) {

        if (!this.initialized) {
            return;
        }

        this.streamer?.update(
            deltaTime
        );

        this.chunkManager?.update(
            deltaTime
        );
    }


    getSeed() {

        return this.seed?.getSeed();
    }


    getChunkManager() {

        return this.chunkManager;
    }


    getStreamer() {

        return this.streamer;
    }


    getTerrainGenerator() {

        return this.terrainGenerator;
    }


    getStreamingRadius() {

        return this.streamingRadius;
    }


    destroy() {

        this.streamer?.destroy();
        this.chunkManager?.destroy();

        if (
            this.game.terrainGenerator ===
            this.terrainGenerator
        ) {
            this.game.terrainGenerator = null;
        }

        if (
            this.game.streamingRadius ===
            this.streamingRadius
        ) {
            this.game.streamingRadius = null;
        }

        this.streamer = null;
        this.chunkManager = null;
        this.streamingRadius = null;
        this.terrainGenerator = null;
        this.seed = null;

        this.initialized = false;
    }

} 
. 
========================================== 
CHEMIN : C:\Users\hp\Downloads\ISLAMCRAFT V2.0\client\src\world\WorldSeed.js 
========================================== 
export class WorldSeed {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.seed = 0;

        this.randomState = 0;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.generateSeed();

        console.log("WorldSeed initialisé :", this.seed);

    }

    generateSeed() {

        if (

            this.game.config.world.seed !== undefined &&

            this.game.config.world.seed !== null

        ) {

            this.seed = this.game.config.world.seed;

        }

        else {

            this.seed = Date.now();

        }

        this.randomState = this.seed;

    }

    setSeed(seed) {

        this.seed = Number(seed);

        this.randomState = this.seed;

    }

    getSeed() {

        return this.seed;

    }

    reset() {

        this.randomState = this.seed;

    }

    random() {

        this.randomState += 0x6D2B79F5;

        let t = this.randomState;

        t = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), t | 1);

        t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), t | 61);

        return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;

    }

    randomRange(min, max) {

        return min + (max - min) * this.random();

    }

    randomInt(min, max) {

        return Math.floor(

            this.randomRange(min, max + 1)

        );

    }

    chance(probability) {

        return this.random() <= probability;

    }

} 
.

import { DesertBiome } from "./DesertBiome.js";
import { PlainsBiome } from "./PlainsBiome.js";
import { ForestBiome } from "./ForestBiome.js";

export class BiomeManager {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.biomes = new Map();

    }

    async initialize() {

        const desert = new DesertBiome(this.game);
        await desert.initialize();

        const plains = new PlainsBiome(this.game);
        await plains.initialize();

        const forest = new ForestBiome(this.game);
        await forest.initialize();

        this.biomes.set(

            desert.id,

            desert

        );

        this.biomes.set(

            plains.id,

            plains

        );

        this.biomes.set(

            forest.id,

            forest

        );

        console.log("BiomeManager initialisé.");

    }

    get(id) {

        return this.biomes.get(id);

    }

    has(id) {

        return this.biomes.has(id);

    }

    getAll() {

        return [

            ...this.biomes.values()

        ];

    }

    getBiomeData(id) {

        const biome = this.get(id);

        if (!biome) {

            return null;

        }

        return biome.getData();

    }

}

export class DesertBiome {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.id = "desert";

        this.name = "Desert";

        this.data = {};

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.data = {

            terrain: {

                groundColor: 0xd9c27a,

                fogColor: 0xf3deb0,

                maxHeight: 18,

                roughness: 0.15

            },

            vegetation: {

                enabled: false,

                density: 0,

                types: []

            },

            objects: {

                density: 0.08,

                types: [

                    "rock",

                    "deadBush",

                    "cactus"

                ]

            },

            structures: {

                density: 0.03,

                types: [

                    "desert_ruins",

                    "desert_temple"

                ]

            },

            loot: {

                chestChance: 0.015

            },

            ambient: {

                music: "desert",

                wind: true

            }

        };

        console.log("DesertBiome initialisé.");

    }

    getData() {

        return this.data;

    }

    getObjectTypes() {

        return this.data.objects.types;

    }

    getStructureTypes() {

        return this.data.structures.types;

    }

    getChestChance() {

        return this.data.loot.chestChance;

    }

}

import * as THREE from "three";

export class Chunk {

    constructor(game, chunkX, chunkZ) {

        this.game = game;

        this.chunkX = chunkX;
        this.chunkZ = chunkZ;

        this.key = `${chunkX}:${chunkZ}`;

        this.group = new THREE.Group();

        this.group.name = this.key;

        this.group.matrixAutoUpdate = true;

        this.generated = false;
        this.loaded = false;
        this.visible = false;

        this.biome = null;

        this.terrain = null;

/*
 * Blocs supprimés manuellement par le joueur.
 *
 * La génération du terrain reste intacte.
 * Ces coordonnées permettent de mémoriser
 * les blocs cassés.
 */
this.removedBlocks = new Set();

this.placedBlocks = new Map();

this.entities = [];

        this.objects = [];

        this.structures = [];

        this.meshes = [];

        this.boundingBox = new THREE.Box3();

        this.center = new THREE.Vector3();

        this.size =
            this.game.config.world.chunkSize;

        this.computeWorldPosition();

    }

    computeWorldPosition() {

        const worldX = this.chunkX * this.size;
        const worldZ = this.chunkZ * this.size;

        this.group.position.set(

            worldX,

            0,

            worldZ

        );

        this.center.set(

            worldX + this.size * 0.5,

            0,

            worldZ + this.size * 0.5

        );

    }

    add(object) {

        this.group.add(object);

        this.objects.push(object);

    }

    remove(object) {

        this.group.remove(object);

        this.objects = this.objects.filter(

            o => o !== object

        );

    }

    addEntity(entity) {

        this.entities.push(entity);

    }

    removeEntity(entity) {

        this.entities = this.entities.filter(

            e => e !== entity

        );

    }

    addMesh(mesh) {

        this.meshes.push(mesh);

        this.group.add(mesh);

    }

    getBlockKey(localX, localY, localZ) {

    return `${localX}:${localY}:${localZ}`;

}


isBlockRemoved(localX, localY, localZ) {

    return this.removedBlocks.has(
        this.getBlockKey(
            localX,
            localY,
            localZ
        )
    );

}


getHighestSolidY(localX, localZ) {

    if (
        !Array.isArray(this.terrain)
    ) {

        return -1;

    }


    const rawHeight =
        Number(
            this.terrain[localZ]?.[localX]
        );


    let highestY =
        Number.isFinite(rawHeight)
            ? Math.floor(rawHeight)
            : -1;


    /*
     * Les blocs construits peuvent
     * dépasser la hauteur originale
     * de la colonne.
     */

    for (
        const key of this.placedBlocks.keys()
    ) {

        const parts =
            key.split(":").map(Number);

        if (parts.length !== 3) {

            continue;

        }


        const [
            x,
            y,
            z
        ] = parts;


        if (
            x === localX &&
            z === localZ &&
            y > highestY
        ) {

            highestY = y;

        }

    }


    /*
     * Chercher le bloc réellement
     * le plus haut qui existe encore.
     */

    for (
        let y = highestY;
        y >= 0;
        y--
    ) {

        const key =
            this.getBlockKey(
                localX,
                y,
                localZ
            );


        if (
            this.removedBlocks.has(key)
        ) {

            continue;

        }


        if (
            this.placedBlocks.has(key)
        ) {

            return y;

        }


        if (
            Number.isFinite(rawHeight) &&
            y <= Math.floor(rawHeight)
        ) {

            return y;

        }

    }


    return -1;

}


getBlockType(localX, localY, localZ) {

    if (
        !Number.isInteger(localX) ||
        !Number.isInteger(localY) ||
        !Number.isInteger(localZ)
    ) {

        return null;

    }


    if (
        localX < 0 ||
        localX >= this.size ||
        localZ < 0 ||
        localZ >= this.size ||
        localY < 0
    ) {

        return null;

    }


    const key =
        this.getBlockKey(
            localX,
            localY,
            localZ
        );


    /*
     * Un bloc supprimé n'existe plus.
     */

    if (
        this.removedBlocks.has(key)
    ) {

        return null;

    }


    /*
     * Un bloc construit par le joueur
     * garde exactement son type.
     */

    if (
        this.placedBlocks.has(key)
    ) {

        return this.placedBlocks.get(key);

    }


    const rawHeight =
        Number(
            this.terrain?.[localZ]?.[localX]
        );


    if (
        !Number.isFinite(rawHeight)
    ) {

        return null;

    }


    const topY =
        Math.floor(rawHeight);


    /*
     * Aucun bloc généré au-dessus
     * de la hauteur originale.
     */

    if (
        localY > topY
    ) {

        return null;

    }


    const highestY =
        this.getHighestSolidY(
            localX,
            localZ
        );


    if (
        localY === highestY
    ) {

        return "grass";

    }


    if (
        localY >= highestY - 3
    ) {

        return "dirt";

    }


    return "stone";

}


setPlacedBlock(
    localX,
    localY,
    localZ,
    blockType
) {

    if (
        !Number.isInteger(localX) ||
        !Number.isInteger(localY) ||
        !Number.isInteger(localZ)
    ) {

        return false;

    }


    if (
        localX < 0 ||
        localX >= this.size ||
        localY < 0 ||
        localY >= (
            this.game.config?.world?.worldHeight
            ?? 128
        ) ||
        localZ < 0 ||
        localZ >= this.size
    ) {

        return false;

    }


    const validTypes = [
        "grass",
        "dirt",
        "stone"
    ];


    if (
        !validTypes.includes(blockType)
    ) {

        return false;

    }


    const key =
        this.getBlockKey(
            localX,
            localY,
            localZ
        );


    this.placedBlocks.set(
        key,
        blockType
    );


    /*
     * Si cet emplacement avait été
     * supprimé auparavant, le nouveau
     * bloc le réactive.
     */

    this.removedBlocks.delete(key);


    return true;

}


removeBlock(
    localX,
    localY,
    localZ
) {

    if (
        localX < 0 ||
        localX >= this.size ||
        localY < 0 ||
        localZ < 0 ||
        localZ >= this.size
    ) {

        return false;

    }


    const key =
        this.getBlockKey(
            localX,
            localY,
            localZ
        );


    /*
     * Un bloc construit est retiré
     * de la carte des blocs construits.
     */

    this.placedBlocks.delete(key);


    /*
     * Un bloc généré est mémorisé
     * comme supprimé.
     */

    this.removedBlocks.add(key);


    return true;

}

    setBiome(biome) {

        this.biome = biome;

    }

    markGenerated() {

        this.generated = true;

    }

    markLoaded() {

        this.loaded = true;

    }

    setVisible(value) {

        this.visible = value;

        this.group.visible = value;

    }

    updateBoundingBox() {

        this.boundingBox.setFromObject(

            this.group

        );

    }

    getWorldPosition() {

        return this.group.position;

    }

    destroy() {

    for (const child of this.group.children) {

        if (child.geometry) {

            child.geometry.dispose();

        }

        if (child.material) {

            if (Array.isArray(child.material)) {

                child.material.forEach(
                    material => material.dispose()
                );

            }
            else {

                child.material.dispose();

            }

        }

    }

    this.group.clear();

    this.objects.length = 0;

    this.structures.length = 0;

    this.entities.length = 0;

    this.meshes.length = 0;

    this.terrain = null;

    this.biome = null;

    this.generated = false;

    this.loaded = false;

    this.visible = false;

}

}

export class ChunkCache {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.cache = new Map();

        const configuredMax =
            this.game.config?.world?.maxCachedChunks;

        this.maxCachedChunks =
            Number.isFinite(configuredMax)
                ? Math.max(
                    0,
                    Math.floor(configuredMax)
                )
                : 512;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log(
            "ChunkCache initialisé."
        );

    }

    has(key) {

        return this.cache.has(key);

    }

    get(key) {

        const entry =
            this.cache.get(key);

        if (!entry) {

            return null;

        }

        entry.lastAccess =
            performance.now();

        return entry.chunk;

    }

    save(chunk) {

        if (!chunk || !chunk.key) {

            return;

        }

        /*
         * Un chunk déjà détruit ne doit jamais
         * entrer dans le cache.
         */

        if (
            !chunk.group ||
            !chunk.terrain
        ) {

            return;

        }

        /*
         * Si le cache est désactivé,
         * ne rien conserver.
         */

        if (
            this.maxCachedChunks <= 0
        ) {

            return;

        }

        /*
         * Retirer une ancienne entrée avant
         * de remplacer le chunk.
         */

        this.cache.delete(
            chunk.key
        );

        this.cache.set(
            chunk.key,
            {
                chunk,
                lastAccess:
                    performance.now()
            }
        );

        this.cleanup();

    }

    touch(key) {

        const entry =
            this.cache.get(key);

        if (!entry) {

            return;

        }

        entry.lastAccess =
            performance.now();

    }

    restore(key) {

        const entry =
            this.cache.get(key);

        if (!entry) {

            return null;

        }

        /*
         * Le chunk doit encore être
         * utilisable.
         */

        if (
            !entry.chunk ||
            !entry.chunk.group ||
            !entry.chunk.terrain
        ) {

            this.cache.delete(key);

            return null;

        }

        entry.lastAccess =
            performance.now();

        return entry.chunk;

    }

    remove(key) {

        this.cache.delete(
            key
        );

    }

    clear() {

        this.cache.clear();

    }

    cleanup() {

        if (
            this.cache.size <=
            this.maxCachedChunks
        ) {

            return;

        }

        const entries =
            [...this.cache.entries()];

        entries.sort(
            (a, b) =>
                a[1].lastAccess -
                b[1].lastAccess
        );

        while (
            this.cache.size >
            this.maxCachedChunks
        ) {

            const oldest =
                entries.shift();

            if (!oldest) {

                break;

            }

            this.cache.delete(
                oldest[0]
            );

        }

    }

}

export class ChunkCoordinates {

    static getChunkSize(game) {

        return game.config.world.chunkSize;

    }

    static worldToChunk(game, x, z) {

        const size = this.getChunkSize(game);

        return {

            chunkX: Math.floor(x / size),

            chunkZ: Math.floor(z / size)

        };

    }

    static chunkToWorld(game, chunkX, chunkZ) {

        const size = this.getChunkSize(game);

        return {

            x: chunkX * size,

            z: chunkZ * size

        };

    }

    static getChunkKey(chunkX, chunkZ) {

        return `${chunkX}:${chunkZ}`;

    }

    static parseChunkKey(key) {

        const [x, z] = key.split(":").map(Number);

        return {

            chunkX: x,

            chunkZ: z

        };

    }

    static localPosition(game, x, z) {

        const size = this.getChunkSize(game);

        const chunk = this.worldToChunk(game, x, z);

        return {

            chunkX: chunk.chunkX,

            chunkZ: chunk.chunkZ,

            localX: x - chunk.chunkX * size,

            localZ: z - chunk.chunkZ * size

        };

    }

    static worldPosition(game, chunkX, chunkZ, localX, localZ) {

        const size = this.getChunkSize(game);

        return {

            x: chunkX * size + localX,

            z: chunkZ * size + localZ

        };

    }

    static distance(chunkAX, chunkAZ, chunkBX, chunkBZ) {

        return Math.max(

            Math.abs(chunkAX - chunkBX),

            Math.abs(chunkAZ - chunkBZ)

        );

    }

    static insideRadius(

        chunkAX,

        chunkAZ,

        chunkBX,

        chunkBZ,

        radius

    ) {

        return (

            this.distance(

                chunkAX,

                chunkAZ,

                chunkBX,

                chunkBZ

            ) <= radius

        );

    }

}

import { ChunkRenderer } from "./ChunkRenderer.js";

export class ChunkLoader {

    constructor(game, chunkManager) {

        this.game = game;

        this.chunkManager =
            chunkManager;

        this.renderer = null;

        this.loadingQueue = [];

        this.queued = new Set();

        this.loading = false;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.renderer =
            new ChunkRenderer(
                this.game
            );

        await this.renderer.initialize();

        console.log(
            "ChunkLoader initialisé."
        );

    }

    update() {

        this.processQueue();

    }

    loadChunk(chunkX, chunkZ) {

        const key =
            `${chunkX}:${chunkZ}`;

        if (
            this.chunkManager.hasChunk(
                chunkX,
                chunkZ
            )
        ) {

            return this.chunkManager.getChunk(
                chunkX,
                chunkZ
            );

        }

        if (
            this.queued.has(key)
        ) {

            return null;

        }

        this.queued.add(key);

this.loadingQueue.push({
    chunkX,
    chunkZ,
    key
});

return null;

    }

    processQueue() {

        if (this.loading) {

            return;

        }

        if (this.loadingQueue.length === 0) {

            return;

        }

        this.loading = true;

        const request =
            this.loadingQueue.shift();

        this.queued.delete(
            request.key
        );

        try {

            const chunk =
                this.chunkManager.createChunk(
                    request.chunkX,
                    request.chunkZ
                );

            if (!chunk) {

                return;

            }

            /*
             * Générer le chunk s'il ne l'est pas encore.
             */

            if (!chunk.generated) {

                const terrainGenerator =
                    this.game.terrainGenerator ??
                    this.game.world?.terrainGenerator ??
                    this.game.worldManager?.terrainGenerator;

                if (
                    terrainGenerator &&
                    typeof terrainGenerator.generate === "function"
                ) {

                    terrainGenerator.generate(
                        chunk
                    );

                }
                else {

                    console.warn(
                        "TerrainGenerator introuvable."
                    );

                }

            }

            /*
             * Le générateur utilise chunk.generated
             * comme indicateur de réussite.
             */

            if (!chunk.generated) {

                console.warn(
                    `Échec génération chunk ${chunk.key}.`
                );

            }

            /*
             * Générer l'environnement uniquement
             * après génération réussie.
             */

            if (chunk.generated) {

                const environmentManager =
                    this.game.environmentManager ??
                    this.game.world?.environmentManager ??
                    this.game.worldManager?.environmentManager;

                if (
                    environmentManager &&
                    typeof environmentManager.generateChunk === "function"
                ) {

                    environmentManager.generateChunk(
                        chunk
                    );

                }

            }

            /*
             * Ajouter le chunk à la scène.
             */

            if (chunk.generated) {

    this.renderer.render(
        chunk
    );


    /*
     * POSITIONNEMENT INITIAL DU JOUEUR
     *
     * On ne positionne le joueur que lorsque
     * le chunk central est réellement généré.
     */

    const player =
        this.game.localPlayer;

    if (
        player &&
        request.chunkX === 0 &&
        request.chunkZ === 0 &&
        Array.isArray(chunk.terrain)
    ) {

        const spawnX = 0;
        const spawnZ = 0;

        const terrainHeight =
            Number(
                chunk.terrain[spawnZ]?.[spawnX]
            );

        if (
            Number.isFinite(
                terrainHeight
            )
        ) {

            const spawnY =
                Math.floor(
                    terrainHeight
                ) + 0.1;

            player.setPosition(
                spawnX,
                spawnY,
                spawnZ
            );

            console.log(
                "Spawn joueur :",
                spawnX,
                spawnY,
                spawnZ
            );
        }
    }
}

        }
        catch (error) {

            console.error(
                `Erreur lors du chargement du chunk ${request.key}:`,
                error
            );

            this.game.eventBus?.emit(
                "world:chunk-load-error",
                {
                    chunkX: request.chunkX,
                    chunkZ: request.chunkZ,
                    error
                }
            );

        }
        finally {
    this.loading = false;
}

    }

    clearQueue() {

        this.loadingQueue.length = 0;

        this.queued.clear();

    }

    destroy() {

        this.clearQueue();

        this.loading = false;

        this.renderer?.clear?.();

        this.renderer = null;

    }

}

import { Chunk } from "./Chunk.js";
import { ChunkLoader } from "./ChunkLoader.js";
import { ChunkUnloader } from "./ChunkUnloader.js";
import { ChunkCache } from "./ChunkCache.js";

export class ChunkManager {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.chunks = new Map();

        this.loader = null;

        this.unloader = null;

        this.cache = null;

        this.initialized = false;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.cache =
            new ChunkCache(this.game);

        await this.cache.initialize();

        this.loader =
            new ChunkLoader(
                this.game,
                this
            );

        await this.loader.initialize();

        this.unloader =
            new ChunkUnloader(
                this.game,
                this
            );

        await this.unloader.initialize();

        this.initialized = true;

        console.log(
            "ChunkManager initialisé."
        );

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        if (!this.initialized) {
            return;
        }

        this.loader?.update(deltaTime);

        this.unloader?.update(deltaTime);

    }

    getKey(chunkX, chunkZ) {

        return `${chunkX}:${chunkZ}`;

    }

    hasChunk(chunkX, chunkZ) {

        const key =
            typeof chunkX === "string"
                ? chunkX
                : this.getKey(
                    chunkX,
                    chunkZ
                );

        return this.chunks.has(key);

    }

    getChunk(chunkX, chunkZ) {

        const key =
            typeof chunkX === "string"
                ? chunkX
                : this.getKey(
                    chunkX,
                    chunkZ
                );

        return this.chunks.get(key);

    }

    createChunk(chunkX, chunkZ) {

        const key =
            this.getKey(
                chunkX,
                chunkZ
            );

        /*
         * Le chunk est déjà chargé.
         */

        if (
            this.chunks.has(key)
        ) {

            return this.chunks.get(key);

        }

        /*
         * Le chunk existe dans le cache.
         * On le récupère au lieu d'en créer
         * un nouveau.
         */

        if (
            this.cache?.has(key)
        ) {

            const cachedChunk =
                this.cache.restore(key);

            if (cachedChunk) {

                this.chunks.set(
                    key,
                    cachedChunk
                );

                return cachedChunk;

            }

        }

        /*
         * Création normale du chunk.
         */

        const chunk =
            new Chunk(
                this.game,
                chunkX,
                chunkZ
            );

        this.chunks.set(
            key,
            chunk
        );

        return chunk;

    }

    loadChunk(chunkX, chunkZ) {

        if (!this.loader) {
            return null;
        }

        return this.loader.loadChunk(
            chunkX,
            chunkZ
        );

    }

    registerChunk(chunk) {

        if (!chunk) {
            return;
        }

        this.chunks.set(
            chunk.key,
            chunk
        );

    }

    removeChunk(chunkX, chunkZ) {

        const key =
            typeof chunkX === "string"
                ? chunkX
                : this.getKey(
                    chunkX,
                    chunkZ
                );

        const chunk =
            this.chunks.get(key);

        if (!chunk) {
            return;
        }

        /*
         * Retirer le chunk de la scène.
         */

        this.loader?.renderer?.remove?.(
            chunk
        );

        /*
         * Sauvegarder le chunk dans le cache
         * avant de le supprimer.
         */

        if (this.cache) {

            this.cache.save(
                chunk
            );

        }

        /*
         * Détruire le chunk.
         */

        if (
            typeof chunk.destroy === "function"
        ) {

            chunk.destroy();

        }

        /*
         * Retirer le chunk de la liste
         * des chunks actuellement chargés.
         */

        this.chunks.delete(key);

    }

    getLoadedChunks() {

        return this.chunks;

    }

    getChunkCount() {

        return this.chunks.size;

    }

    clear() {

        for (
            const chunk of this.chunks.values()
        ) {

            /*
             * Retirer le chunk de la scène.
             */

            this.loader?.renderer?.remove?.(
                chunk
            );

            /*
             * Détruire le chunk.
             */

            if (
                typeof chunk.destroy === "function"
            ) {

                chunk.destroy();

            }

        }

        this.chunks.clear();

    }

    rebuildChunk(chunk) {

    if (!chunk) {

        return;

    }


    this.loader?.renderer?.rebuild?.(
        chunk
    );

}

    destroy() {

        this.loader?.destroy();

        this.unloader?.destroy();

        this.clear();

        this.loader = null;

        this.unloader = null;

        this.cache?.clear?.();

        this.cache = null;

        this.initialized = false;

    }

}

import * as THREE from "three";

export class ChunkRenderer {

    createTerrainMesh(chunk) {

    if (
        !chunk ||
        !chunk.terrain ||
        chunk.terrain.length === 0
    ) {

        return null;

    }


    const size =
        chunk.size;


    let grassCount = 0;
    let dirtCount = 0;
    let stoneCount = 0;


    /*
     * Trouver la hauteur maximale
     * nécessaire, y compris les blocs
     * construits par le joueur.
     */

    let maxY = 0;


    for (
        let localZ = 0;
        localZ < size;
        localZ++
    ) {

        for (
            let localX = 0;
            localX < size;
            localX++
        ) {

            const rawHeight =
                Number(
                    chunk.terrain[localZ]?.[localX]
                );


            if (
                Number.isFinite(rawHeight)
            ) {

                maxY =
                    Math.max(
                        maxY,
                        Math.floor(rawHeight)
                    );

            }

        }

    }


    /*
     * Les blocs placés peuvent être
     * plus hauts que le terrain original.
     */

    for (
        const key of chunk.placedBlocks.keys()
    ) {

        const parts =
            key.split(":").map(Number);


        if (
            parts.length !== 3
        ) {

            continue;

        }


        maxY =
            Math.max(
                maxY,
                parts[1]
            );

    }


    /*
     * Première passe :
     * compter chaque type de bloc.
     */

    for (
        let localZ = 0;
        localZ < size;
        localZ++
    ) {

        for (
            let localX = 0;
            localX < size;
            localX++
        ) {

            for (
                let y = 0;
                y <= maxY;
                y++
            ) {

                const blockType =
                    chunk.getBlockType(
                        localX,
                        y,
                        localZ
                    );


                if (
                    blockType === "grass"
                ) {

                    grassCount++;

                }
                else if (
                    blockType === "dirt"
                ) {

                    dirtCount++;

                }
                else if (
                    blockType === "stone"
                ) {

                    stoneCount++;

                }

            }

        }

    }


    const group =
        new THREE.Group();


    group.name =
        `terrain_${chunk.key}`;


    const blockGeometry =
        new THREE.BoxGeometry(
            1,
            1,
            1
        );


    const grassMaterial =
        new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
            color: 0x6ea84a,
            roughness: 1,
            metalness: 0
        });


    const dirtMaterial =
        new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
            color: 0x8d6e63,
            roughness: 1,
            metalness: 0
        });


    const stoneMaterial =
        new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
            color: 0x777777,
            roughness: 1,
            metalness: 0
        });


    const grassMesh =
        new THREE.InstancedMesh(
            blockGeometry,
            grassMaterial,
            Math.max(1, grassCount)
        );


    const dirtMesh =
        new THREE.InstancedMesh(
            blockGeometry,
            dirtMaterial,
            Math.max(1, dirtCount)
        );


    const stoneMesh =
        new THREE.InstancedMesh(
            blockGeometry,
            stoneMaterial,
            Math.max(1, stoneCount)
        );


    grassMesh.castShadow = true;
    grassMesh.receiveShadow = true;

    dirtMesh.castShadow = true;
    dirtMesh.receiveShadow = true;

    stoneMesh.castShadow = true;
    stoneMesh.receiveShadow = true;


    const matrix =
        new THREE.Matrix4();


    let grassIndex = 0;
    let dirtIndex = 0;
    let stoneIndex = 0;


    /*
     * Deuxième passe :
     * placer chaque bloc selon
     * son véritable type.
     */

    for (
        let localZ = 0;
        localZ < size;
        localZ++
    ) {

        for (
            let localX = 0;
            localX < size;
            localX++
        ) {

            for (
                let y = 0;
                y <= maxY;
                y++
            ) {

                const blockType =
                    chunk.getBlockType(
                        localX,
                        y,
                        localZ
                    );


                if (!blockType) {

                    continue;

                }


                matrix.makeTranslation(
                    localX + 0.5,
                    y + 0.5,
                    localZ + 0.5
                );


                if (
                    blockType === "grass"
                ) {

                    grassMesh.setMatrixAt(
                        grassIndex,
                        matrix
                    );

                    grassIndex++;

                }
                else if (
                    blockType === "dirt"
                ) {

                    dirtMesh.setMatrixAt(
                        dirtIndex,
                        matrix
                    );

                    dirtIndex++;

                }
                else if (
                    blockType === "stone"
                ) {

                    stoneMesh.setMatrixAt(
                        stoneIndex,
                        matrix
                    );

                    stoneIndex++;

                }

            }

        }

    }


    grassMesh.count =
        grassIndex;

    dirtMesh.count =
        dirtIndex;

    stoneMesh.count =
        stoneIndex;


    grassMesh.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate =
        true;

    dirtMesh.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate =
        true;

    stoneMesh.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate =
        true;


    grassMesh.userData = {
        type: "blockMesh",
        blockType: "grass",
        chunkKey: chunk.key
    };


    dirtMesh.userData = {
        type: "blockMesh",
        blockType: "dirt",
        chunkKey: chunk.key
    };


    stoneMesh.userData = {
        type: "blockMesh",
        blockType: "stone",
        chunkKey: chunk.key
    };


    if (grassIndex > 0) {

        group.add(
            grassMesh
        );

    }


    if (dirtIndex > 0) {

        group.add(
            dirtMesh
        );

    }


    if (stoneIndex > 0) {

        group.add(
            stoneMesh
        );

    }


    group.userData = {
        type: "voxelTerrain",
        chunkKey: chunk.key,
        size
    };


    return group;

}

getBiomeColor(biome) {

    switch (biome) {

        case "desert":

            return 0xd9c27a;

        case "forest":

            return 0x3f8c3a;

        case "plains":

            return 0x6ea84a;

        default:

            return 0x6ea84a;

    }

}

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.scene = null;

        this.renderedChunks = new Map();

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.scene =
            this.game.scene.getScene();

        console.log(
            "ChunkRenderer initialisé."
        );

    }

    render(chunk) {

    if (!chunk) {

        return;

    }

    if (
        this.renderedChunks.has(
            chunk.key
        )
    ) {

        return;

    }

    if (
        chunk.terrain &&
        chunk.meshes.length === 0
    ) {

        const terrainMesh =
            this.createTerrainMesh(
                chunk
            );

        if (terrainMesh) {

            chunk.addMesh(
                terrainMesh
            );

        }

    }

    this.scene.add(
        chunk.group
    );

    chunk.setVisible(true);

    chunk.markLoaded();

    this.renderedChunks.set(
        chunk.key,
        chunk
    );

}
rebuild(chunk) {

    if (!chunk) {

        return;

    }


    /*
     * Supprimer l'ancien mesh du terrain.
     */

    for (
        const mesh of chunk.meshes
    ) {

        chunk.group.remove(mesh);


        mesh.traverse?.(
            object => {

                if (object.geometry) {

                    object.geometry.dispose();

                }


                if (object.material) {

                    if (
                        Array.isArray(
                            object.material
                        )
                    ) {

                        object.material.forEach(
                            material => {

                                material.dispose();

                            }
                        );

                    }
                    else {

                        object.material.dispose();

                    }

                }

            }
        );

    }


    chunk.meshes.length = 0;


    /*
     * Recréer le terrain avec les
     * blocs actuellement présents.
     */

    const terrainMesh =
        this.createTerrainMesh(
            chunk
        );


    if (terrainMesh) {

        chunk.addMesh(
            terrainMesh
        );

    }

}

    hide(chunk) {

        if (!chunk) {
            return;
        }

        chunk.setVisible(false);

    }

    show(chunk) {

        if (!chunk) {
            return;
        }

        chunk.setVisible(true);

    }

    remove(chunk) {

        if (!chunk) {
            return;
        }

        if (
            !this.renderedChunks.has(
                chunk.key
            )
        ) {
            return;
        }

        this.scene.remove(
            chunk.group
        );

        this.renderedChunks.delete(
            chunk.key
        );

    }

    isRendered(chunk) {

        return this.renderedChunks.has(
            chunk.key
        );

    }

    clear() {

        if (!this.scene) {
            return;
        }

        for (
            const chunk of
            this.renderedChunks.values()
        ) {

            this.scene.remove(
                chunk.group
            );

        }

        this.renderedChunks.clear();

    }

    update() {

        // Réservé aux futures optimisations :
        // - GPU Culling
        // - Occlusion Culling
        // - Distance Rendering
        // - Instancing dynamique

    }

    destroy() {

        this.clear();

        this.scene = null;

        this.game = null;

    }

}

export class ChunkUnloader {

    constructor(game, chunkManager) {

        this.game = game;

        this.chunkManager =
            chunkManager;

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log(
            "ChunkUnloader initialisé."
        );

    }

    update() {

        const player =
            this.game.localPlayer;

        if (!player) {
            return;
        }

        const chunkSize =
            this.game.config?.world?.chunkSize;

        const unloadDistance =
            this.game.config?.world?.unloadDistance;

        /*
         * Vérifier la configuration.
         */

        if (
            !Number.isFinite(chunkSize) ||
            chunkSize <= 0
        ) {

            return;

        }

        if (
            !Number.isFinite(unloadDistance) ||
            unloadDistance < 0
        ) {

            return;

        }

        /*
         * Calculer le chunk dans lequel
         * se trouve actuellement le joueur.
         */

        const playerChunkX =
            Math.floor(
                player.position.x / chunkSize
            );

        const playerChunkZ =
            Math.floor(
                player.position.z / chunkSize
            );

        /*
         * Récupérer les chunks actuellement
         * chargés.
         */

        const loadedChunks =
            this.chunkManager.getLoadedChunks();

        /*
         * Créer une liste des chunks à retirer.
         *
         * On ne les supprime pas directement
         * pendant le parcours de la Map.
         */

        const chunksToRemove = [];

        for (
            const chunk of loadedChunks.values()
        ) {

            if (!chunk) {
                continue;
            }

            const dx =
                chunk.chunkX -
                playerChunkX;

            const dz =
                chunk.chunkZ -
                playerChunkZ;

            /*
             * Distance en grille.
             *
             * Exemple :
             *
             * joueur = 0:0
             * chunk  = 0:5
             *
             * distance = 5
             */

            const distance =
                Math.max(
                    Math.abs(dx),
                    Math.abs(dz)
                );

            /*
             * Le chunk est encore assez proche.
             */

            if (
                distance <= unloadDistance
            ) {

                continue;

            }

            chunksToRemove.push({
                chunkX: chunk.chunkX,
                chunkZ: chunk.chunkZ
            });

        }

        /*
         * Supprimer maintenant les chunks
         * qui sont trop éloignés.
         */

        for (
            const coordinates of chunksToRemove
        ) {

            this.chunkManager.removeChunk(
                coordinates.chunkX,
                coordinates.chunkZ
            );

        }

    }

    destroy() {

        this.chunkManager = null;

    }

}

export class ChunkWorker {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.worker = null;

        this.enabled = false;

        this.callbacks = new Map();

        this.requestId = 0;

        this.initialized = false;

    }

    async initialize() {

        /*
         * Pour le moment, aucun Worker réel
         * n'est lancé ici.
         *
         * La génération reste exécutée
         * côté client principal.
         *
         * Cela évite de charger ce même fichier
         * comme Worker et de créer une boucle
         * de Worker incorrecte.
         */

        this.worker = null;

        this.enabled = false;

        this.initialized = true;

        console.log(
            "ChunkWorker initialisé en mode fallback."
        );

    }

    generateChunk(data) {

        /*
         * Aucun Worker actif :
         * retourner directement les données.
         */

        if (
            !this.enabled ||
            !this.worker
        ) {

            return Promise.resolve(
                data
            );

        }

        return new Promise(
            (resolve, reject) => {

                const id =
                    ++this.requestId;

                this.callbacks.set(
                    id,
                    {
                        resolve,
                        reject
                    }
                );

                try {

                    this.worker.postMessage({

                        id,

                        type:
                            "GENERATE_CHUNK",

                        payload:
                            data

                    });

                }
                catch (error) {

                    this.callbacks.delete(
                        id
                    );

                    reject(error);

                }

            }
        );

    }

    onMessage(event) {

        if (!event?.data) {

            return;

        }

        const {

            id,

            payload,

            error

        } = event.data;

        const callback =
            this.callbacks.get(id);

        if (!callback) {

            return;

        }

        this.callbacks.delete(id);

        if (error) {

            callback.reject(
                new Error(error)
            );

            return;

        }

        callback.resolve(
            payload
        );

    }

    onError(error) {

        for (
            const callback
            of this.callbacks.values()
        ) {

            callback.reject(
                error
            );

        }

        this.callbacks.clear();

    }

    terminate() {

        if (this.worker) {

            this.worker.terminate();

        }

        this.worker = null;

        for (
            const callback
            of this.callbacks.values()
        ) {

            callback.reject(
                new Error(
                    "ChunkWorker terminated."
                )
            );

        }

        this.callbacks.clear();

        this.enabled = false;

        this.initialized = false;

    }

}

import { TreeManager } from "./TreeManager.js";
import { StructureManager } from "./StructureManager.js";
import { ObjectManager } from "./ObjectManager.js";

export class EnvironmentManager {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.treeManager = null;

        this.structureManager = null;

        this.objectManager = null;

        this.initialized = false;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.treeManager =
            new TreeManager(this.game);

        await this.treeManager.initialize();

        this.structureManager =
            new StructureManager(this.game);

        await this.structureManager.initialize();

        this.objectManager =
            new ObjectManager(this.game);

        await this.objectManager.initialize();

        this.initialized = true;

        console.log(
            "EnvironmentManager initialisé."
        );

    }

    generateChunk(chunk) {

        if (
            !this.initialized ||
            !chunk
        ) {

            return;

        }

        /*
         * Générer les arbres.
         */

        this.treeManager?.generate(
            chunk
        );

        /*
         * Générer les structures.
         */

        this.structureManager?.generate(
            chunk
        );

        /*
         * Générer les autres objets.
         */

        this.objectManager?.generate(
            chunk
        );

    }

    unloadChunk(chunk) {

        if (!chunk) {

            return;

        }

        /*
         * Nettoyer les arbres.
         */

        this.treeManager?.remove(
            chunk
        );

        /*
         * Nettoyer les structures.
         */

        this.structureManager?.remove(
            chunk
        );

        /*
         * Nettoyer les autres objets.
         */

        this.objectManager?.remove(
            chunk
        );

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        if (!this.initialized) {

            return;

        }

        this.treeManager?.update(
            deltaTime
        );

        this.structureManager?.update(
            deltaTime
        );

        this.objectManager?.update(
            deltaTime
        );

    }

    destroy() {

        /*
         * Nettoyage général.
         */

        this.treeManager = null;

        this.structureManager = null;

        this.objectManager = null;

        this.initialized = false;

    }

}

export class InteractiveEnvironment {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.interactives = new Map();

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log(
            "InteractiveEnvironment initialisé."
        );

    }

    register(object) {

        if (!object) {

            return null;

        }

        const id =
            object.userData?.id ??
            crypto.randomUUID();

        /*
         * Enregistrer l'identifiant
         * directement sur l'objet.
         */

        if (!object.userData) {

            object.userData = {};

        }

        object.userData.id = id;

        this.interactives.set(
            id,
            object
        );

        return id;

    }

    unregister(id) {

        if (!id) {

            return false;

        }

        return this.interactives.delete(
            id
        );

    }

    get(id) {

        if (!id) {

            return null;

        }

        return this.interactives.get(
            id
        ) ?? null;

    }

    getNearby(position, radius = 3) {

        if (!position) {

            return [];

        }

        const numericRadius =
            Number(radius);

        if (
            !Number.isFinite(
                numericRadius
            ) ||
            numericRadius < 0
        ) {

            return [];

        }

        const results = [];

        const radiusSquared =
            numericRadius *
            numericRadius;

        for (
            const object of
            this.interactives.values()
        ) {

            if (!object?.position) {

                continue;

            }

            const distanceSquared =
                position.distanceToSquared(
                    object.position
                );

            if (
                distanceSquared <=
                radiusSquared
            ) {

                results.push(
                    object
                );

            }

        }

        return results;

    }

    update(playerPosition) {

        if (!playerPosition) {

            this.game.nearbyInteractives = [];

            return;

        }

        const nearby =
            this.getNearby(
                playerPosition,
                3
            );

        this.game.nearbyInteractives =
            nearby;

    }

    clear() {

        this.interactives.clear();

        if (this.game) {

            this.game.nearbyInteractives =
                [];

        }

    }

    destroy() {

        this.clear();

        this.game = null;

    }

}

import * as THREE from "three";

export class ObjectManager {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.chunkObjects = new Map();

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("ObjectManager initialisé.");

    }

    generate(chunk) {

        if (!chunk || !chunk.objects) {

            return;

        }

        /*
         * Éviter de générer deux fois
         * les objets du même chunk.
         */

        if (this.chunkObjects.has(chunk.key)) {

            return;

        }

        const objects = [];

        for (const object of chunk.objects) {

            if (
                object.type === "tree" ||
                object.type === "smallTree"
            ) {

                continue;

            }

            const mesh =
                this.createObject(object);

            if (!mesh) {

                continue;

            }

            objects.push(mesh);

            /*
             * Le mesh appartient maintenant
             * directement au chunk.
             */

            chunk.add(mesh);

        }

        this.chunkObjects.set(

            chunk.key,

            objects

        );

    }

    createObject(object) {

        if (!object) {

            return null;

        }

        let geometry;

        let material;

        switch (object.type) {

            case "rock":

                geometry =
                    new THREE.DodecahedronGeometry(
                        0.7,
                        0
                    );

                material =
                    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({

                        color: 0x7a7a7a

                    });

                break;

            case "bush":

                geometry =
                    new THREE.SphereGeometry(
                        0.6,
                        10,
                        10
                    );

                material =
                    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({

                        color: 0x3e8d35

                    });

                break;

            case "cactus":

                geometry =
                    new THREE.CylinderGeometry(
                        0.25,
                        0.3,
                        2,
                        8
                    );

                material =
                    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({

                        color: 0x2d8a43

                    });

                break;

            default:

                geometry =
                    new THREE.BoxGeometry(
                        0.6,
                        0.6,
                        0.6
                    );

                material =
                    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({

                        color: 0xffffff

                    });

                break;

        }

        const mesh =
            new THREE.Mesh(
                geometry,
                material
            );

        mesh.position.copy(
            object.position
        );

        mesh.userData = {

            id: object.id,

            type: object.type,

            biome: object.biome

        };

        mesh.castShadow = true;

        mesh.receiveShadow = true;

        return mesh;

    }

    remove(chunk) {

        if (!chunk) {

            return;

        }

        const objects =
            this.chunkObjects.get(
                chunk.key
            );

        if (!objects) {

            return;

        }

        for (const object of objects) {

            chunk.remove(object);

            this.disposeObject(object);

        }

        this.chunkObjects.delete(
            chunk.key
        );

    }

    disposeObject(object) {

        if (!object) {

            return;

        }

        object.traverse(child => {

            if (child.geometry) {

                child.geometry.dispose();

            }

            if (child.material) {

                if (Array.isArray(child.material)) {

                    child.material.forEach(
                        material => {

                            material.dispose();

                        }
                    );

                }

                else {

                    child.material.dispose();

                }

            }

        });

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        /*
         * Futures améliorations :
         *
         * - animations
         * - rotation
         * - physique
         * - effets
         * - instancing
         * - LOD
         */

    }

}

import * as THREE from "three";

export class StructureManager {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.chunkStructures = new Map();

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("StructureManager initialisé.");

    }

    generate(chunk) {

        if (!chunk || !chunk.structures) {

            return;

        }

        /*
         * Ne pas générer plusieurs fois
         * les structures du même chunk.
         */

        if (this.chunkStructures.has(chunk.key)) {

            return;

        }

        const structures = [];

        for (const data of chunk.structures) {

            const structure =
                this.createStructure(data);

            if (!structure) {

                continue;

            }

            structures.push(structure);

            /*
             * La structure appartient
             * directement au chunk.
             */

            chunk.add(structure);

        }

        this.chunkStructures.set(

            chunk.key,

            structures

        );

    }

    createStructure(data) {

        if (!data) {

            return null;

        }

        const group =
            new THREE.Group();

        group.name =
            data.id ??
            "structure";

        const baseGeometry =
            new THREE.BoxGeometry(
                4,
                2,
                4
            );

        const baseMaterial =
            new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({

                color: 0x8d6e63

            });

        const base =
            new THREE.Mesh(
                baseGeometry,
                baseMaterial
            );

        base.position.y = 1;

        base.castShadow = true;

        base.receiveShadow = true;

        group.add(base);

        const roofGeometry =
            new THREE.ConeGeometry(
                3.2,
                2,
                4
            );

        const roofMaterial =
            new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({

                color: 0x5d4037

            });

        const roof =
            new THREE.Mesh(
                roofGeometry,
                roofMaterial
            );

        roof.position.y = 3;

        roof.rotation.y =
            Math.PI / 4;

        roof.castShadow = true;

        roof.receiveShadow = true;

        group.add(roof);

        if (data.position) {

            group.position.copy(
                data.position
            );

        }

        group.rotation.y =
            Number(data.rotation) || 0;

        group.userData = {

            id: data.id,

            type: data.type,

            biome: data.biome

        };

        return group;

    }

    remove(chunk) {

        if (!chunk) {

            return;

        }

        const structures =
            this.chunkStructures.get(
                chunk.key
            );

        if (!structures) {

            return;

        }

        for (const structure of structures) {

            chunk.remove(structure);

            this.disposeObject(structure);

        }

        this.chunkStructures.delete(
            chunk.key
        );

    }

    disposeObject(object) {

        if (!object) {

            return;

        }

        object.traverse(child => {

            if (child.geometry) {

                child.geometry.dispose();

            }

            if (child.material) {

                if (Array.isArray(child.material)) {

                    child.material.forEach(
                        material => {

                            material.dispose();

                        }
                    );

                }

                else {

                    child.material.dispose();

                }

            }

        });

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        /*
         * Futures améliorations :
         *
         * - animations
         * - portes
         * - destruction
         * - interactions
         * - LOD
         * - streaming avancé
         */

    }

}

import * as THREE from "three";

export class TreeManager {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.chunkTrees = new Map();

    }

    async initialize() {

        console.log("TreeManager initialisé.");

    }

    generate(chunk) {

    if (!chunk || !chunk.objects) {

        return;

    }

    const trees = [];

    const chunkWorldX =
        chunk.chunkX * chunk.size;

    const chunkWorldZ =
        chunk.chunkZ * chunk.size;

    for (const object of chunk.objects) {

        if (
            object.type !== "tree" &&
            object.type !== "smallTree"
        ) {

            continue;

        }

        const tree =
            this.createTree(object);

        tree.position.x -= chunkWorldX;

        tree.position.z -= chunkWorldZ;

        trees.push(tree);

        chunk.add(tree);

    }

    this.chunkTrees.set(
        chunk.key,
        trees
    );

}

    createTree(object) {

        const group = new THREE.Group();

        const trunkGeometry = new THREE.CylinderGeometry(

            0.25,
            0.35,
            2,
            8

        );

        const trunkMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({

            color: 0x6d4c41

        });

        const trunk = new THREE.Mesh(

            trunkGeometry,

            trunkMaterial

        );

        trunk.position.y = 1;

        group.add(trunk);

        const leavesGeometry = new THREE.SphereGeometry(

            1.2,

            12,

            12

        );

        const leavesMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({

            color: 0x2e7d32

        });

        const leaves = new THREE.Mesh(

            leavesGeometry,

            leavesMaterial

        );

        leaves.position.y = 2.6;

        group.add(leaves);

        group.position.copy(

            object.position

        );

        group.userData.type = "tree";

        group.userData.id = object.id;

        return group;

    }

    remove(chunk) {

    const trees =
        this.chunkTrees.get(
            chunk.key
        );

    if (!trees) {

        return;

    }

    for (const tree of trees) {

        chunk.remove(tree);

        this.disposeObject(tree);

    }

    this.chunkTrees.delete(
        chunk.key
    );

}

disposeObject(object) {

    object.traverse(child => {

        if (child.geometry) {

            child.geometry.dispose();

        }

        if (child.material) {

            if (Array.isArray(child.material)) {

                child.material.forEach(
                    material => material.dispose()
                );

            }

            else {

                child.material.dispose();

            }

        }

    });

}

    update(deltaTime) {

        // Animations futures :
        // vent
        // saisons
        // destruction
        // croissance

    }

}

import { NoiseGenerator } from "./NoiseGenerator.js";

export class BiomeGenerator {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.noise = null;

        this.moistureFrequency = 0.003;

        this.temperatureFrequency = 0.002;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.noise =
            new NoiseGenerator(
                this.game
            );

        await this.noise.initialize();

        this.moistureFrequency =
            Number(
                this.game.config?.world?.moistureFrequency ?? 0.003
            );

        this.temperatureFrequency =
            Number(
                this.game.config?.world?.temperatureFrequency ?? 0.002
            );

        if (
            !Number.isFinite(this.moistureFrequency) ||
            this.moistureFrequency <= 0
        ) {

            this.moistureFrequency = 0.003;

        }

        if (
            !Number.isFinite(this.temperatureFrequency) ||
            this.temperatureFrequency <= 0
        ) {

            this.temperatureFrequency = 0.002;

        }

        console.log(
            "BiomeGenerator initialisé."
        );

    }

    generate(chunk) {

        if (!chunk) {

            return "plains";

        }

        const chunkSize =
            Number(
                this.game.config?.world?.chunkSize
            );

        if (
            !Number.isFinite(chunkSize) ||
            chunkSize <= 0
        ) {

            return "plains";

        }

        const worldX =
            chunk.chunkX *
            chunkSize;

        const worldZ =
            chunk.chunkZ *
            chunkSize;

        return this.getBiome(
            worldX,
            worldZ
        );

    }

    getBiome(worldX, worldZ) {

        if (!this.noise) {

            return "plains";

        }

        /*
         * NoiseGenerator.sample() applique déjà
         * sa fréquence interne.
         *
         * On compense cette fréquence ici afin
         * que les fréquences spécifiques aux biomes
         * soient appliquées exactement une seule fois.
         */

        const noiseFrequency =
            Number(
                this.noise.frequency
            );

        const baseFrequency =
            Number.isFinite(noiseFrequency) &&
            noiseFrequency > 0
                ? noiseFrequency
                : 0.01;

        const moistureX =
            (
                worldX *
                this.moistureFrequency
            ) / baseFrequency;

        const moistureZ =
            (
                worldZ *
                this.moistureFrequency
            ) / baseFrequency;

        const temperatureX =
            (
                worldX *
                this.temperatureFrequency +
                5000
            ) / baseFrequency;

        const temperatureZ =
            (
                worldZ *
                this.temperatureFrequency +
                5000
            ) / baseFrequency;

        const moisture =
            this.noise.sample(
                moistureX,
                moistureZ
            );

        const temperature =
            this.noise.sample(
                temperatureX,
                temperatureZ
            );

        if (
            temperature > 0.65 &&
            moisture < 0.35
        ) {

            return "desert";

        }

        if (
            moisture > 0.60
        ) {

            return "forest";

        }

        return "plains";

    }

}

import { NoiseGenerator } from "./NoiseGenerator.js";

export class HeightGenerator {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.noise = null;

        this.chunkSize = 0;

        this.baseHeight = 0;

        this.heightScale = 4;

        this.frequency = 0.01;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.chunkSize =
            this.game.config?.world?.chunkSize ?? 16;

        this.baseHeight =
            this.game.config?.world?.baseHeight ?? 0;

        this.heightScale =
            this.game.config?.world?.heightScale ?? 4;

        this.frequency =
            this.game.config?.world?.terrainFrequency ?? 0.005;

        this.noise =
            new NoiseGenerator(this.game);

        await this.noise.initialize();

        console.log(
            "HeightGenerator initialisé."
        );

    }

    generate(chunk) {

        if (!chunk || !this.noise) {

            return [];

        }

        const terrain = [];

        const worldStartX =
            chunk.chunkX * this.chunkSize;

        const worldStartZ =
            chunk.chunkZ * this.chunkSize;

        for (
            let z = 0;
            z < this.chunkSize;
            z++
        ) {

            const row = [];

            for (
                let x = 0;
                x < this.chunkSize;
                x++
            ) {

                const worldX =
                    worldStartX + x;

                const worldZ =
                    worldStartZ + z;

                const noise =
                    this.noise.sample(
                        worldX * this.frequency,
                        worldZ * this.frequency
                    );

                const height =
                    this.baseHeight +
                    noise * this.heightScale;

                row.push(height);

            }

            terrain.push(row);

        }

        return terrain;

    }

    getHeight(terrain, localX, localZ) {

        if (
            !Array.isArray(terrain) ||
            terrain.length === 0
        ) {

            return 0;

        }

        const z =
            Math.max(
                0,
                Math.min(
                    terrain.length - 1,
                    Math.floor(localZ)
                )
            );

        const row = terrain[z];

        if (
            !Array.isArray(row) ||
            row.length === 0
        ) {

            return 0;

        }

        const x =
            Math.max(
                0,
                Math.min(
                    row.length - 1,
                    Math.floor(localX)
                )
            );

        return row[x];

    }

}
