CHEMIN : C:\Users\hp\Downloads\ISLAMCRAFT V2.0\client\src\world\generation\NoiseGenerator.js 
========================================== 
export class NoiseGenerator {

constructor(game) {

    this.game = game;

    this.seed = 0;

    this.frequency = 0.01;

    this.octaves = 4;

    this.persistence = 0.5;

    this.lacunarity = 2.0;

}

async initialize() {

    const worldSeed =
        this.game.worldManager?.getSeed?.();

    this.seed =
        worldSeed ??
        this.game.config?.world?.seed ??
        12345;

    this.frequency =
        this.game.config?.world?.noiseFrequency ?? 0.01;

    this.octaves =
        Math.max(
            1,
            Math.floor(
                Number(
                    this.game.config?.world?.noiseOctaves ?? 4
                )
            )
        );

    this.persistence =
        Number(
            this.game.config?.world?.noisePersistence ?? 0.5
        );

    this.lacunarity =
        Number(
            this.game.config?.world?.noiseLacunarity ?? 2.0
        );

    console.log("NoiseGenerator initialisé.");

}

sample(x, z) {

    let amplitude = 1;

    let frequency = this.frequency;

    let value = 0;

    let maxAmplitude = 0;

    for (
        let octave = 0;
        octave < this.octaves;
        octave++
    ) {

        value +=
            this.random2D(
                x * frequency,
                z * frequency
            ) * amplitude;

        maxAmplitude += amplitude;

        amplitude *= this.persistence;

        frequency *= this.lacunarity;

    }

    if (maxAmplitude <= 0) {

        return 0.5;

    }

    value /= maxAmplitude;

    return Math.max(
        0,
        Math.min(
            1,
            (value + 1) * 0.5
        )
    );

}

random2D(x, z) {

    const n =
        Math.sin(

            x * 127.1 +

            z * 311.7 +

            this.seed * 74.7

        ) * 43758.5453123;

    return (
        n - Math.floor(n)
    ) * 2 - 1;

}

}

import * as THREE from "three";

export class ObjectGenerator {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.chunkSize = 0;

        this.seed = 0;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.chunkSize =
            this.game.config?.world?.chunkSize ?? 16;

        this.seed =
            this.game.worldSeed?.getSeed?.() ??
            this.game.config?.world?.seed ??
            12345;

        console.log(
            "ObjectGenerator initialisé."
        );

    }

    generate(chunk, terrain, biomeId) {

        if (!chunk || !terrain || !biomeId) {

            return;

        }

        const biome =
            this.game.worldManager
                ?.getBiomeManager?.()
                ?.get?.(biomeId);

        /*
         * Pour l'instant, on récupère les données
         * directement depuis la configuration
         * du biome si le BiomeManager n'est pas
         * exposé par WorldManager.
         */

        let biomeData = null;

        if (biome?.getData) {

            biomeData = biome.getData();

        }

        if (!biomeData) {

            biomeData =
                this.getFallbackBiomeData(
                    biomeId
                );

        }

        const objects = [];

        const objectConfig =
            biomeData.objects;

        if (
            !objectConfig ||
            !Array.isArray(objectConfig.types)
        ) {

            chunk.objects = objects;

            return;

        }

        const density =
            Number(objectConfig.density) || 0;

        if (density <= 0) {

            chunk.objects = objects;

            return;

        }

        const attempts =
            Math.max(
                0,
                Math.floor(
                    this.chunkSize *
                    this.chunkSize *
                    density
                )
            );

        for (
            let i = 0;
            i < attempts;
            i++
        ) {

            const random =
                this.random(
                    chunk,
                    i
                );

            const localX =
                Math.floor(
                    random.x *
                    this.chunkSize
                );

            const localZ =
                Math.floor(
                    random.z *
                    this.chunkSize
                );

            const height =
                terrain[
                    Math.min(
                        localZ,
                        terrain.length - 1
                    )
                ]?.[
                    Math.min(
                        localX,
                        terrain[0].length - 1
                    )
                ];

            if (!Number.isFinite(height)) {

                continue;

            }

            const typeIndex =
                Math.floor(
                    random.type *
                    objectConfig.types.length
                );

            const type =
                objectConfig.types[
                    Math.min(
                        typeIndex,
                        objectConfig.types.length - 1
                    )
                ];

            objects.push({

                id:
                    `object_${chunk.key}_${i}`,

                type,

                biome: biomeId,

                position:
                    new THREE.Vector3(

                        chunk.chunkX *
                            this.chunkSize +
                            localX,

                        height,

                        chunk.chunkZ *
                            this.chunkSize +
                            localZ

                    ),

                generated: true

            });

        }

        chunk.objects = objects;

    }

    getFallbackBiomeData(biomeId) {

        switch (biomeId) {

            case "desert":

                return {

                    objects: {

                        density: 0.08,

                        types: [
                            "rock",
                            "deadBush",
                            "cactus"
                        ]

                    }

                };

            case "forest":

                return {

                    objects: {

                        density: 0.35,

                        types: [
                            "tree",
                            "rock",
                            "bush",
                            "log"
                        ]

                    }

                };

            case "plains":

                return {

                    objects: {

                        density: 0.15,

                        types: [
                            "bush",
                            "rock",
                            "smallTree"
                        ]

                    }

                };

            default:

                return {

                    objects: {

                        density: 0,

                        types: []

                    }

                };

        }

    }

    random(chunk, index) {

        const base =
            chunk.chunkX * 928371 +
            chunk.chunkZ * 689917 +
            index * 421;

        const x =
            this.hash(base + 1);

        const z =
            this.hash(base + 2);

        const type =
            this.hash(base + 3);

        return {

            x,
            z,
            type

        };

    }

    hash(value) {

        const n =
            Math.sin(
                value +
                this.seed * 0.0001
            ) *
            43758.5453123;

        return (
            n -
            Math.floor(n)
        );

    }

}

import * as THREE from "three";

export class StructureGenerator {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.chunkSize = 0;

        this.spawnChance = 0.08;

        this.seed = 987654;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.chunkSize =

            this.game.config.world.chunkSize;

        this.spawnChance =

            this.game.config.world.structureChance ?? 0.08;

        console.log("StructureGenerator initialisé.");

    }

    generate(chunk, terrain, biome) {

        if (!terrain) {

            chunk.structures = [];

            return;

        }

        const structures = [];

        if (!this.shouldGenerate(chunk, biome)) {

            chunk.structures = structures;

            return;

        }

        const localX = Math.floor(this.chunkSize * 0.5);
        const localZ = Math.floor(this.chunkSize * 0.5);

        const height =

            terrain[
                Math.min(localZ, terrain.length - 1)
            ][
                Math.min(localX, terrain[0].length - 1)
            ];

        structures.push({

            id: `structure_${chunk.key}`,

            type: this.getStructureType(biome),

            biome,

            position: new THREE.Vector3(

                chunk.chunkX * this.chunkSize + localX,

                height,

                chunk.chunkZ * this.chunkSize + localZ

            ),

            rotation:

                this.random(chunk) * Math.PI * 2,

            generated: true

        });

        chunk.structures = structures;

    }

    shouldGenerate(chunk, biome) {

        if (biome === "desert") {

            return this.random(chunk) < this.spawnChance * 0.5;

        }

        if (biome === "forest") {

            return this.random(chunk) < this.spawnChance * 1.2;

        }

        return this.random(chunk) < this.spawnChance;

    }

    getStructureType(biome) {

        switch (biome) {

            case "forest":
                return "forest_ruins";

            case "desert":
                return "desert_ruins";

            case "plains":
                return "camp";

            default:
                return "structure";

        }

    }

    random(chunk) {

        const n = Math.sin(

            chunk.chunkX * 92821 +

            chunk.chunkZ * 68917 +

            this.seed

        ) * 43758.5453123;

        return n - Math.floor(n);

    }

}

import { HeightGenerator } from "./HeightGenerator.js";
import { BiomeGenerator } from "./BiomeGenerator.js";
import { ObjectGenerator } from "./ObjectGenerator.js";
import { StructureGenerator } from "./StructureGenerator.js";

export class TerrainGenerator {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.heightGenerator = null;
        this.biomeGenerator = null;
        this.objectGenerator = null;
        this.structureGenerator = null;

        this.initialized = false;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.heightGenerator = new HeightGenerator(this.game);
        await this.heightGenerator.initialize();

        this.biomeGenerator = new BiomeGenerator(this.game);
        await this.biomeGenerator.initialize();

        this.objectGenerator = new ObjectGenerator(this.game);
        await this.objectGenerator.initialize();

        this.structureGenerator = new StructureGenerator(this.game);
        await this.structureGenerator.initialize();

        this.initialized = true;

        console.log("TerrainGenerator initialisé.");

    }

    generate(chunk) {

    if (!this.initialized || !chunk) {

        return false;

    }

    const terrain =
        this.heightGenerator.generate(chunk);

    if (!terrain) {

        return false;

    }

    chunk.terrain = terrain;

    const biome =
        this.biomeGenerator.generate(chunk);

    if (!biome) {

        return false;

    }

    chunk.setBiome(biome);

    this.objectGenerator.generate(
        chunk,
        terrain,
        biome
    );

    this.structureGenerator.generate(
        chunk,
        terrain,
        biome
    );

    chunk.markGenerated();

    return true;

}

}

export class GenerationScheduler {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.queue = [];

        this.running = false;

        this.maxConcurrent = 1;

        this.activeJobs = 0;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.queue = [];

        this.running = false;

        this.activeJobs = 0;

        console.log("GenerationScheduler initialisé.");

    }

    schedule(task, priority = 0) {

        if (typeof task !== "function") {

            return false;

        }

        this.queue.push({

            task,

            priority

        });

        this.queue.sort(

            (a, b) => b.priority - a.priority

        );

        this.process();

        return true;

    }

    process() {

        if (this.running) {

            return;

        }

        this.running = true;

        while (

            this.activeJobs < this.maxConcurrent &&

            this.queue.length > 0

        ) {

            const job = this.queue.shift();

            this.activeJobs++;

            Promise.resolve()

                .then(() => job.task())

                .catch(error => {

                    this.game.eventBus?.emit(

                        "world:generation-error",

                        error

                    );

                })

                .finally(() => {

                    this.activeJobs--;

                    this.running = false;

                    this.process();

                });

        }

        if (this.activeJobs === 0) {

            this.running = false;

        }

    }

    clear() {

        this.queue.length = 0;

    }

    getQueueSize() {

        return this.queue.length;

    }

    isRunning() {

        return this.running;

    }

}

export class PriorityQueue {

    constructor() {

        this.items = [];

    }

    enqueue(item, priority = 0) {

        this.items.push({

            item,

            priority

        });

        this.items.sort(

            (a, b) => b.priority - a.priority

        );

    }

    dequeue() {

        if (this.items.length === 0) {

            return null;

        }

        return this.items.shift().item;

    }

    peek() {

        if (this.items.length === 0) {

            return null;

        }

        return this.items[0].item;

    }

    remove(item) {

        const index = this.items.findIndex(

            entry => entry.item === item

        );

        if (index === -1) {

            return false;

        }

        this.items.splice(index, 1);

        return true;

    }

    clear() {

        this.items.length = 0;

    }

    size() {

        return this.items.length;

    }

    isEmpty() {

        return this.items.length === 0;

    }

}

export class StreamingRadius {

    constructor(game) {

        this.game = game;

        this.radius = 4;

        this.previousRadius = this.radius;

    }

    async initialize() {

        const configuredRadius =

            this.game.config?.world?.renderDistance;

        if (Number.isFinite(configuredRadius)) {

            this.radius = Math.max(

                0,

                Math.floor(configuredRadius)

            );

            this.previousRadius = this.radius;

        }

        console.log("StreamingRadius initialisé.");

    }

    setRadius(radius) {

        const value = Number(radius);

        if (!Number.isFinite(value)) {

            return;

        }

        const nextRadius = Math.max(

            0,

            Math.floor(value)

        );

        if (nextRadius === this.radius) {

            return;

        }

        this.previousRadius = this.radius;

        this.radius = nextRadius;

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(

            "world:streaming-radius-changed",

            {

                radius: this.radius,

                previousRadius: this.previousRadius

            }

        );

    }

    getRadius() {

        return this.radius;

    }

    getPreviousRadius() {

        return this.previousRadius;

    }

    getRequiredChunks(centerX, centerZ) {

        const chunks = [];

        for (

            let x = centerX - this.radius;

            x <= centerX + this.radius;

            x++

        ) {

            for (

                let z = centerZ - this.radius;

                z <= centerZ + this.radius;

                z++

            ) {

                chunks.push({

                    x,

                    z

                });

            }

        }

        return chunks;

    }

    contains(centerX, centerZ, chunkX, chunkZ) {

        return (

            Math.abs(chunkX - centerX) <= this.radius &&

            Math.abs(chunkZ - centerZ) <= this.radius

        );

    }

}

export class WorldStreamer {

    constructor(game, chunkManager) {

        this.game = game;

        this.chunkManager =
            chunkManager ?? null;

        this.streamingRadius = null;

        this.active = false;

        this.lastChunkX = null;

        this.lastChunkZ = null;

    }

    async initialize() {

        this.streamingRadius =
            this.game.streamingRadius ?? null;

        this.active = true;

        console.log(
            "WorldStreamer initialisé."
        );

    }

    update() {

        if (!this.active) {
            return;
        }

        const player =
            this.game.localPlayer;

        if (
            !player ||
            !this.chunkManager
        ) {
            return;
        }

        const chunkSize =
            this.game.config?.world?.chunkSize;

        if (
            !Number.isFinite(chunkSize) ||
            chunkSize <= 0
        ) {
            return;
        }

        const chunkX = Math.floor(
            player.position.x / chunkSize
        );

        const chunkZ = Math.floor(
            player.position.z / chunkSize
        );

        /*
         * Ne recalculer que si le joueur
         * change de chunk.
         */

        if (
            chunkX === this.lastChunkX &&
            chunkZ === this.lastChunkZ
        ) {
            return;
        }

        this.lastChunkX = chunkX;

        this.lastChunkZ = chunkZ;

        this.streamAround(
            chunkX,
            chunkZ
        );

    }

    streamAround(centerX, centerZ) {

        if (!this.streamingRadius) {
            return;
        }

        const chunks =
    this.streamingRadius
        .getRequiredChunks(
            centerX,
            centerZ
        );

chunks.sort(
    (a, b) => {

        const distanceA =
            Math.max(
                Math.abs(
                    a.x - centerX
                ),
                Math.abs(
                    a.z - centerZ
                )
            );

        const distanceB =
            Math.max(
                Math.abs(
                    b.x - centerX
                ),
                Math.abs(
                    b.z - centerZ
                )
            );

        return distanceA - distanceB;
    }
);

for (
    const coordinates of chunks
) {

            const chunkX =
                coordinates.x;

            const chunkZ =
                coordinates.z;

            if (
                this.chunkManager.hasChunk(
                    chunkX,
                    chunkZ
                )
            ) {
                continue;
            }

            this.chunkManager.loadChunk(
                chunkX,
                chunkZ
            );

        }

        this.game.eventBus?.emit(
            "world:streaming-updated",
            {
                centerX,
                centerZ,
                chunks
            }
        );

    }

    stop() {

        this.active = false;

    }

    start() {

        this.active = true;

    }

    isActive() {

        return this.active;

    }

    destroy() {

        this.active = false;

        this.chunkManager = null;

        this.streamingRadius = null;

        this.lastChunkX = null;

        this.lastChunkZ = null;

    }

}

CHEMIN : C:\Users\hp\Downloads\ISLAMCRAFT V2.0\scripts\assets\validateAssets.js

import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

const __filename = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
const __dirname = path.dirname(__filename);

const projectRoot = path.resolve(__dirname, "../..");
const assetsDirectory = path.join(projectRoot, "client", "assets");

const requiredDirectories = [
    "models",
    "textures",
    "animations",
    "audio"
];

let errors = 0;

function reportError(message) {

    console.error(`[ASSETS] ERROR: ${message}`);

    errors++;

}

function reportSuccess(message) {

    console.log(`[ASSETS] OK: ${message}`);

}

function validateDirectory(directory) {

    if (!fs.existsSync(directory)) {

        reportError(`Dossier manquant: ${directory}`);

        return false;

    }

    if (!fs.statSync(directory).isDirectory()) {

        reportError(`Ce chemin n'est pas un dossier: ${directory}`);

        return false;

    }

    reportSuccess(`Dossier trouvé: ${path.relative(projectRoot, directory)}`);

    return true;

}

function validateAssets() {

    console.log("Validation des assets ISLAMCRAFT...");

    if (!validateDirectory(assetsDirectory)) {

        return false;

    }

    for (const directory of requiredDirectories) {

        validateDirectory(

            path.join(assetsDirectory, directory)

        );

    }

    return errors === 0;

}

const valid = validateAssets();

if (!valid) {

    process.exitCode = 1;

    console.error(

        `Validation des assets échouée avec ${errors} erreur(s).`

    );

} else {

    console.log("Validation des assets réussie.");

}

CHEMIN : C:\Users\hp\Downloads\ISLAMCRAFT V2.0\scripts\data\validateItems.js

import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

const __filename = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
const __dirname = path.dirname(__filename);

const dataDirectory = path.resolve(

    __dirname,

    "../../data/items"

);

const files = [

    "items.json",

    "bonuses.json",

    "lootTables.json"

];

let errors = 0;

function error(message) {

    console.error(`[ITEMS] ERROR: ${message}`);

    errors++;

}

function loadJson(fileName) {

    const filePath = path.join(

        dataDirectory,

        fileName

    );

    if (!fs.existsSync(filePath)) {

        error(`Fichier manquant: ${fileName}`);

        return null;

    }

    try {

        return JSON.parse(

            fs.readFileSync(filePath, "utf8")

        );

    } catch (exception) {

        error(

            `JSON invalide dans ${fileName}: ${exception.message}`

        );

        return null;

    }

}

function validateItems() {

    const itemsData = loadJson("items.json");

    const bonusesData = loadJson("bonuses.json");

    const lootData = loadJson("lootTables.json");

    if (!itemsData || !bonusesData || !lootData) {

        return false;

    }

    if (!Array.isArray(itemsData.items)) {

        error("items.json doit contenir un tableau 'items'.");

    }

    if (!Array.isArray(bonusesData.bonuses)) {

        error("bonuses.json doit contenir un tableau 'bonuses'.");

    }

    if (!Array.isArray(lootData.lootTables)) {

        error(

            "lootTables.json doit contenir un tableau 'lootTables'."

        );

    }

    const itemIds = new Set(

        (itemsData.items || []).map(item => item.id)

    );

    for (const table of lootData.lootTables || []) {

        if (!table.id || !Array.isArray(table.entries)) {

            error(

                `Table de loot invalide: ${table.id ?? "sans id"}.`

            );

            continue;

        }

        for (const entry of table.entries) {

            if (!itemIds.has(entry.item)) {

                error(

                    `Item '${entry.item}' utilisé par '${table.id}' introuvable.`

                );

            }

        }

    }

    return errors === 0;

}

if (!validateItems()) {

    process.exitCode = 1;

    console.error("Validation des items échouée.");

} else {

    console.log("Validation des items réussie.");

}

import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

const __filename = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
const __dirname = path.dirname(__filename);

const quizDirectory = path.resolve(

    __dirname,

    "../../data/quiz"

);

let errors = 0;

function error(message) {

    console.error(`[QUIZ] ERROR: ${message}`);

    errors++;

}

function loadJson(fileName) {

    const filePath = path.join(

        quizDirectory,

        fileName

    );

    if (!fs.existsSync(filePath)) {

        error(`Fichier manquant: ${fileName}`);

        return null;

    }

    try {

        return JSON.parse(

            fs.readFileSync(filePath, "utf8")

        );

    } catch (exception) {

        error(

            `JSON invalide dans ${fileName}: ${exception.message}`

        );

        return null;

    }

}

function validateQuiz() {

    const categories = loadJson("categories.json");
    const difficulties = loadJson("difficulties.json");
    const questions = loadJson("questions.json");

    if (!categories || !difficulties || !questions) {

        return false;

    }

    if (!Array.isArray(categories.categories)) {

        error(

            "categories.json doit contenir 'categories'."

        );

    }

    if (!Array.isArray(difficulties.difficulties)) {

        error(

            "difficulties.json doit contenir 'difficulties'."

        );

    }

    if (!Array.isArray(questions.questions)) {

        error(

            "questions.json doit contenir 'questions'."

        );

        return false;

    }

    const categoryIds = new Set(

        (categories.categories || []).map(

            category => category.id

        )

    );

    const difficultyIds = new Set(

        (difficulties.difficulties || []).map(

            difficulty => difficulty.id

        )

    );

    const questionIds = new Set();

    for (const question of questions.questions) {

        if (!question.id) {

            error("Une question ne possède pas d'identifiant.");

        } else if (questionIds.has(question.id)) {

            error(`Identifiant de question dupliqué: ${question.id}.`);

        } else {

            questionIds.add(question.id);

        }

        if (!categoryIds.has(question.category)) {

            error(

                `Catégorie '${question.category}' introuvable pour ${question.id}.`

            );

        }

        if (!difficultyIds.has(question.difficulty)) {

            error(

                `Difficulté '${question.difficulty}' introuvable pour ${question.id}.`

            );

        }

        if (!Array.isArray(question.answers)) {

            error(

                `Réponses manquantes pour ${question.id}.`

            );

            continue;

        }

        if (

            !Number.isInteger(question.correctAnswer) ||

            question.correctAnswer < 0 ||

            question.correctAnswer >= question.answers.length

        ) {

            error(

                `Réponse correcte invalide pour ${question.id}.`

            );

        }

    }

    return errors === 0;

}

if (!validateQuiz()) {

    process.exitCode = 1;

    console.error("Validation du quiz échouée.");

} else {

    console.log("Validation du quiz réussie.");

}

import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

const __filename = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
const __dirname = path.dirname(__filename);

const skinsDirectory = path.resolve(

    __dirname,

    "../../data/skins"

);

let errors = 0;

function error(message) {

    console.error(`[SKINS] ERROR: ${message}`);

    errors++;

}

function loadJson(fileName) {

    const filePath = path.join(

        skinsDirectory,

        fileName

    );

    if (!fs.existsSync(filePath)) {

        error(`Fichier manquant: ${fileName}`);

        return null;

    }

    try {

        return JSON.parse(

            fs.readFileSync(filePath, "utf8")

        );

    } catch (exception) {

        error(

            `JSON invalide dans ${fileName}: ${exception.message}`

        );

        return null;

    }

}

function validateSkins() {

    const skinsData = loadJson("skins.json");
    const animationsData = loadJson("animations.json");

    if (!skinsData || !animationsData) {

        return false;

    }

    if (!Array.isArray(skinsData.skins)) {

        error(

            "skins.json doit contenir un tableau 'skins'."

        );

        return false;

    }

    if (!Array.isArray(animationsData.animations)) {

        error(

            "animations.json doit contenir un tableau 'animations'."

        );

        return false;

    }

    const animationIds = new Set(

        animationsData.animations.map(

            animation => animation.id

        )

    );

    const skinIds = new Set();

    for (const skin of skinsData.skins) {

        if (!skin.id) {

            error("Une skin ne possède pas d'identifiant.");

            continue;

        }

        if (skinIds.has(skin.id)) {

            error(`Skin dupliquée: ${skin.id}.`);

        }

        skinIds.add(skin.id);

        if (!Array.isArray(skin.animations)) {

            error(

                `Animations manquantes pour la skin ${skin.id}.`

            );

            continue;

        }

        for (const animationId of skin.animations) {

            if (!animationIds.has(animationId)) {

                error(

                    `Animation '${animationId}' introuvable pour ${skin.id}.`

                );

            }

        }

    }

    return errors === 0;

}

if (!validateSkins()) {

    process.exitCode = 1;

    console.error("Validation des skins échouée.");

} else {

    console.log("Validation des skins réussie.");

}

CHEMIN : C:\Users\hp\Downloads\ISLAMCRAFT V2.0\scripts\world\generateSeed.js

import crypto from "node:crypto";

const DEFAULT_SEED_LENGTH = 16;

export function generateSeed(length = DEFAULT_SEED_LENGTH) {

    if (!Number.isInteger(length) || length <= 0) {

        throw new TypeError(

            "La longueur de la seed doit être un entier positif."

        );

    }

    return crypto.randomBytes(length).toString("hex");

}

export function generateNumericSeed() {

    const buffer = crypto.randomBytes(8);

    return Number(

        BigInt.asUintN(

            32,

            buffer.readBigUInt64BE(0)

        )

    );

}

export function normalizeSeed(seed) {

    if (seed === undefined || seed === null) {

        return generateSeed();

    }

    if (typeof seed === "number") {

        if (!Number.isFinite(seed)) {

            throw new TypeError("Seed numérique invalide.");

        }

        return Math.trunc(seed).toString();

    }

    const normalized = String(seed).trim();

    if (!normalized) {

        return generateSeed();

    }

    return normalized;

}

export function hashSeed(seed) {

    return crypto

        .createHash("sha256")

        .update(normalizeSeed(seed))

        .digest("hex");

}

if (import.meta.url === `file://${process.argv[1]}`) {

    const seed = normalizeSeed(process.argv[2]);

    console.log(seed);

}

CHEMIN : C:\Users\hp\Downloads\ISLAMCRAFT V2.0\scripts\world\validateWorld.js 
========================================== 
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

const __filename = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
const __dirname = path.dirname(__filename);

const projectRoot = path.resolve(__dirname, "../..");
const worldDirectory = path.join(projectRoot, "data");

const requiredFiles = [
    "biomes/biomes.json",
    "biomes/desert.json",
    "biomes/forest.json",
    "biomes/plains.json"
];

let errors = 0;

function reportError(message) {

    console.error(`[WORLD] ERROR: ${message}`);

    errors++;

}

function loadJson(relativePath) {

    const filePath = path.join(

        worldDirectory,

        relativePath

    );

    if (!fs.existsSync(filePath)) {

        reportError(`Fichier manquant: ${relativePath}`);

        return null;

    }

    try {

        return JSON.parse(

            fs.readFileSync(filePath, "utf8")

        );

    } catch (error) {

        reportError(

            `JSON invalide dans ${relativePath}: ${error.message}`

        );

        return null;

    }

}

function validateBiome(biome, expectedId) {

    if (!biome || typeof biome !== "object") {

        reportError(

            `Biome invalide: ${expectedId}`

        );

        return;

    }

    if (biome.id !== expectedId) {

        reportError(

            `Identifiant incorrect pour ${expectedId}.`

        );

    }

    if (typeof biome.name !== "string" || !biome.name.trim()) {

        reportError(

            `Nom manquant pour le biome ${expectedId}.`

        );

    }

    if (!biome.terrain || typeof biome.terrain !== "object") {

        reportError(

            `Terrain manquant pour ${expectedId}.`

        );

    }

    if (

        !biome.environment ||

        typeof biome.environment !== "object"

    ) {

        reportError(

            `Environnement manquant pour ${expectedId}.`

        );

    }

    if (!Array.isArray(biome.vegetation)) {

        reportError(

            `Végétation invalide pour ${expectedId}.`

        );

    }

    if (!Array.isArray(biome.objects)) {

        reportError(

            `Objets invalides pour ${expectedId}.`

        );

    }

}

function validateWorld() {

    const registry = loadJson("biomes/biomes.json");

    const desert = loadJson("biomes/desert.json");
    const forest = loadJson("biomes/forest.json");
    const plains = loadJson("biomes/plains.json");

    if (!registry || !desert || !forest || !plains) {

        return false;

    }

    if (!Array.isArray(registry.biomes)) {

        reportError(

            "biomes.json doit contenir un tableau 'biomes'."

        );

        return false;

    }

    const expectedBiomes = {

        desert,

        forest,

        plains

    };

    for (const [id, biome] of Object.entries(expectedBiomes)) {

        validateBiome(biome, id);

    }

    const registryIds = new Set();

    for (const entry of registry.biomes) {

        if (!entry || typeof entry !== "object") {

            reportError("Entrée de biome invalide dans biomes.json.");

            continue;

        }

        if (!entry.id) {

            reportError(

                "Une entrée de biomes.json ne possède pas d'id."

            );

            continue;

        }

        if (registryIds.has(entry.id)) {

            reportError(

                `Biome dupliqué dans biomes.json: ${entry.id}.`

            );

        }

        registryIds.add(entry.id);

        if (!expectedBiomes[entry.id]) {

            reportError(

                `Biome référencé mais absent: ${entry.id}.`

            );

        }

        if (typeof entry.file !== "string") {

            reportError(

                `Fichier manquant pour le biome ${entry.id}.`

            );

        }

    }

    for (const id of Object.keys(expectedBiomes)) {

        if (!registryIds.has(id)) {

            reportError(

                `Biome ${id} absent de biomes.json.`

            );

        }

    }

    return errors === 0;

}

const valid = validateWorld();

if (!valid) {

    process.exitCode = 1;

    console.error(

        `Validation du monde échouée avec ${errors} erreur(s).`

    );

} else {

    console.log("Validation du monde réussie.");

}

import express from "express";
import http from "node:http";
import path from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import { Server as SocketIOServer } from "socket.io";

import { GameServer } from "./core/GameServer.js";

const __filename =
    fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);

const __dirname =
    path.dirname(__filename);

const ROOT_DIR =
    path.resolve(
        __dirname,
        "../.."
    );

const CLIENT_DIR =
    path.join(
        ROOT_DIR,
        "client"
    );

const HOST =
    process.env.HOST ??
    "0.0.0.0";

const PORT =
    Number(
        process.env.PORT ?? 3000
    );

const app =
    express();

const httpServer =
    http.createServer(app);

const io =
    new SocketIOServer(
        httpServer,
        {
            cors: {
                origin: true,
                credentials: true
            }
        }
    );

const gameServer =
    new GameServer();

app.disable(
    "x-powered-by"
);

app.use(
    express.json({
        limit: "1mb"
    })
);

app.get(
    "/health",
    (_req, res) => {

        res.status(200).json({

            status: "ok",

            server:
                "ISLAMCRAFT",

            timestamp:
                Date.now()

        });

    }
);

const NODE_MODULES_DIR =
    path.join(
        ROOT_DIR,
        "node_modules"
    );

app.use(
    "/node_modules",
    express.static(
        NODE_MODULES_DIR
    )
);

app.use(
    express.static(
        CLIENT_DIR
    )
);

app.get(
    "/",
    (_req, res) => {

        res.sendFile(
            path.join(
                CLIENT_DIR,
                "index.html"
            )
        );

    }
);

io.on(
    "connection",
    socket => {

        console.log(
            `Client connected: ${socket.id}`
        );

        socket.emit(
            "connection:accepted",
            {
                id:
                    socket.id,

                timestamp:
                    Date.now()
            }
        );

        socket.on(
            "disconnect",
            reason => {

                console.log(
                    `Client disconnected: ${socket.id} (${reason})`
                );

            }
        );

    }
);

async function start() {

    await gameServer.start();

    await new Promise(
        (resolve, reject) => {

            httpServer.once(
                "error",
                reject
            );

            httpServer.listen(
                PORT,
                HOST,
                resolve
            );

        }
    );

    console.log(
        `ISLAMCRAFT HTTP server listening on ${HOST}:${PORT}`
    );

    console.log(
        `ISLAMCRAFT client available at http://localhost:${PORT}`
    );

    console.log(
        `ISLAMCRAFT Socket.IO available at http://localhost:${PORT}`
    );

}

async function shutdown() {

    console.log(
        "Stopping ISLAMCRAFT..."
    );

    io.close();

    await new Promise(
        resolve => {

            httpServer.close(
                () => resolve()
            );

        }
    );

    if (
        typeof gameServer.stop ===
        "function"
    ) {

        await gameServer.stop();

    }

    process.exit(
        0
    );

}

process.on(
    "SIGINT",
    shutdown
);

process.on(
    "SIGTERM",
    shutdown
);

start().catch(
    error => {

        console.error(
            "Failed to start ISLAMCRAFT:",
            error
        );

        process.exit(
            1
        );

    }
);

CHEMIN : C:\Users\hp\Downloads\ISLAMCRAFT V2.0\server\src\antiCheat\ActionValidator.js 
========================================== 
export class ActionValidator {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.maxActionsPerSecond =
            options.maxActionsPerSecond ?? 20;

        this.history = new Map();

    }

    validate(playerId, action, timestamp = Date.now()) {

        if (!playerId || !action) {

            return {
                valid: false,
                reason: "invalid_action"
            };

        }

        let playerHistory = this.history.get(playerId);

        if (!playerHistory) {

            playerHistory = [];

            this.history.set(playerId, playerHistory);

        }

        const windowStart = timestamp - 1000;

        while (

            playerHistory.length > 0 &&

            playerHistory[0] < windowStart

        ) {

            playerHistory.shift();

        }

        if (

            playerHistory.length >=

            this.maxActionsPerSecond

        ) {

            return {
                valid: false,
                reason: "action_rate_exceeded"
            };

        }

        playerHistory.push(timestamp);

        return {
            valid: true,
            reason: null
        };

    }

    clearPlayer(playerId) {

        this.history.delete(playerId);

    }

    clear() {

        this.history.clear();

    }

}

export class MovementValidator {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.maxDistancePerSecond =
            options.maxDistancePerSecond ?? 12;

        this.players = new Map();

    }

    validate(playerId, position, timestamp = Date.now()) {

        if (

            !playerId ||

            !position ||

            !Number.isFinite(position.x) ||

            !Number.isFinite(position.y) ||

            !Number.isFinite(position.z)

        ) {

            return {
                valid: false,
                reason: "invalid_position"
            };

        }

        const previous = this.players.get(playerId);

        this.players.set(playerId, {

            x: position.x,

            y: position.y,

            z: position.z,

            timestamp

        });

        if (!previous) {

            return {
                valid: true,
                reason: null,
                distance: 0,
                speed: 0
            };

        }

        const elapsed =

            Math.max(

                (timestamp - previous.timestamp) / 1000,

                0.001

            );

        const dx = position.x - previous.x;

        const dy = position.y - previous.y;

        const dz = position.z - previous.z;

        const distance = Math.sqrt(

            dx * dx +

            dy * dy +

            dz * dz

        );

        const speed = distance / elapsed;

        if (speed > this.maxDistancePerSecond) {

            return {
                valid: false,
                reason: "movement_speed_exceeded",
                distance,
                speed
            };

        }

        return {
            valid: true,
            reason: null,
            distance,
            speed
        };

    }

    clearPlayer(playerId) {

        this.players.delete(playerId);

    }

    clear() {

        this.players.clear();

    }

}

export class ScoreValidator {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.maxRewardPerAction =
            options.maxRewardPerAction ?? 1000;

        this.players = new Map();

    }

    validate(playerId, reward, reason = "") {

        if (!playerId || !Number.isFinite(reward)) {

            return {
                valid: false,
                reason: "invalid_score"
            };

        }

        if (reward < 0) {

            return {
                valid: false,
                reason: "negative_reward"
            };

        }

        if (reward > this.maxRewardPerAction) {

            return {
                valid: false,
                reason: "reward_exceeded"
            };

        }

        const previous =

            this.players.get(playerId) ?? 0;

        const total = previous + reward;

        this.players.set(playerId, total);

        return {
            valid: true,
            reason: null,
            reward,
            total,
            source: reason
        };

    }

    getTotal(playerId) {

        return this.players.get(playerId) ?? 0;

    }

    resetPlayer(playerId) {

        this.players.delete(playerId);

    }

    clear() {

        this.players.clear();

    }

}

export class SpeedValidator {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.maxSpeed =
            options.maxSpeed ?? 12;

        this.tolerance =
            options.tolerance ?? 1.25;

        this.players = new Map();

    }

    validate(playerId, velocity, timestamp = Date.now()) {

        if (

            !playerId ||

            !velocity ||

            !Number.isFinite(velocity.x) ||

            !Number.isFinite(velocity.y) ||

            !Number.isFinite(velocity.z)

        ) {

            return {
                valid: false,
                reason: "invalid_velocity"
            };

        }

        const speed = Math.sqrt(

            velocity.x * velocity.x +

            velocity.y * velocity.y +

            velocity.z * velocity.z

        );

        const allowedSpeed =

            this.maxSpeed * this.tolerance;

        const result = {

            valid: speed <= allowedSpeed,

            reason:

                speed <= allowedSpeed

                    ? null

                    : "speed_exceeded",

            speed,

            allowedSpeed,

            timestamp

        };

        this.players.set(playerId, {

            speed,

            timestamp

        });

        return result;

    }

    getLastSpeed(playerId) {

        return (

            this.players.get(playerId)?.speed ?? 0

        );

    }

    clearPlayer(playerId) {

        this.players.delete(playerId);

    }

    clear() {

        this.players.clear();

    }

}

export class SuspiciousActivityDetector {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.threshold =
            options.threshold ?? 3;

        this.windowMs =
            options.windowMs ?? 10000;

        this.activities = new Map();

    }

    record(playerId, type, details = {}, timestamp = Date.now()) {

        if (!playerId || !type) {

            return {
                suspicious: false,
                reason: "invalid_activity"
            };

        }

        let playerActivities =
            this.activities.get(playerId);

        if (!playerActivities) {

            playerActivities = [];

            this.activities.set(

                playerId,

                playerActivities

            );

        }

        const windowStart =
            timestamp - this.windowMs;

        const recent =
            playerActivities.filter(

                activity =>
                    activity.timestamp >= windowStart

            );

        recent.push({

            type,

            details,

            timestamp

        });

        this.activities.set(

            playerId,

            recent

        );

        const sameTypeCount =
            recent.filter(

                activity =>
                    activity.type === type

            ).length;

        const suspicious =
            sameTypeCount >= this.threshold;

        return {

            suspicious,

            reason: suspicious

                ? "repeated_activity"

                : null,

            type,

            count: sameTypeCount

        };

    }

    getRecent(playerId, timestamp = Date.now()) {

        const activities =
            this.activities.get(playerId) ?? [];

        const windowStart =
            timestamp - this.windowMs;

        return activities.filter(

            activity =>
                activity.timestamp >= windowStart

        );

    }

    clearPlayer(playerId) {

        this.activities.delete(playerId);

    }

    clear() {

        this.activities.clear();

    }

}

export class CleanupManager {

    constructor() {

        this.callbacks = new Set();

        this.cleaned = false;

    }

    register(callback) {

        if (typeof callback !== "function") {

            throw new TypeError(

                "Le callback de nettoyage doit être une fonction."

            );

        }

        this.callbacks.add(callback);

        return () => {

            this.callbacks.delete(callback);

        };

    }

    async cleanup() {

        if (this.cleaned) {

            return;

        }

        this.cleaned = true;

        const callbacks = [

            ...this.callbacks

        ];

        this.callbacks.clear();

        for (const callback of callbacks) {

            try {

                await callback();

            } catch (error) {

                console.error(

                    "Erreur pendant le nettoyage:",

                    error

                );

            }

        }

    }

    reset() {

        this.cleaned = false;

    }

    clear() {

        this.callbacks.clear();

    }

    size() {

        return this.callbacks.size;

    }

}

export class GameLoop {

    constructor({

        clock,

        update,

        tickRate = 20

    } = {}) {

        this.clock = clock;

        this.updateCallback =

            typeof update === "function"

                ? update

                : null;

        this.tickRate = Math.max(

            1,

            Number(tickRate) || 20

        );

        this.interval =

            1000 / this.tickRate;

        this.timer = null;

        this.running = false;

        this.processing = false;

    }

    start() {

        if (this.running) {

            return;

        }

        this.running = true;

        this.clock?.start();

        this.timer = setInterval(

            () => this.step(),

            this.interval

        );

    }

    async step() {

        if (!this.running || this.processing) {

            return;

        }

        this.processing = true;

        try {

            const deltaTime =

                this.clock

                    ? this.clock.update()

                    : this.interval / 1000;

            if (this.updateCallback) {

                await this.updateCallback(

                    deltaTime,

                    this.clock?.getTick() ?? 0

                );

            }

        } finally {

            this.processing = false;

        }

    }

    stop() {

        if (this.timer !== null) {

            clearInterval(this.timer);

            this.timer = null;

        }

        this.running = false;

        this.clock?.stop();

    }

    isRunning() {

        return this.running;

    }

    getTickRate() {

        return this.tickRate;

    }

}

import { ServerConfig } from "./ServerConfig.js";
import { ServerClock } from "./ServerClock.js";
import { GameLoop } from "./GameLoop.js";
import { CleanupManager } from "./CleanupManager.js";

export class GameServer {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.config = options.config ??

            new ServerConfig(options);

        this.clock = options.clock ??

            new ServerClock();

        this.cleanup = options.cleanup ??

            new CleanupManager();

        this.loop = null;

        this.started = false;

        this.starting = false;

        this.stopping = false;

        this.components = new Map();

    }

    registerComponent(name, component) {

        if (!name) {

            throw new TypeError(

                "Le nom du composant est obligatoire."

            );

        }

        if (!component) {

            throw new TypeError(

                `Composant invalide: ${name}.`

            );

        }

        this.components.set(name, component);

        if (typeof component.stop === "function") {

            this.cleanup.register(

                () => component.stop()

            );

        } else if (typeof component.destroy === "function") {

            this.cleanup.register(

                () => component.destroy()

            );

        }

        return component;

    }

    getComponent(name) {

        return this.components.get(name) ?? null;

    }

    async initialize() {

        if (this.started || this.starting) {

            return;

        }

        this.starting = true;

        try {

            this.config.load();

            this.clock.tickRate = this.config.getTickRate();

            this.clock.tickDuration =

                1000 / this.clock.tickRate;

            this.loop = new GameLoop({

                clock: this.clock,

                tickRate: this.config.getTickRate(),

                update: (

                    deltaTime,

                    tick

                ) => this.update(

                    deltaTime,

                    tick

                )

            });

            for (const component of this.components.values()) {

                if (

                    typeof component.initialize ===

                    "function"

                ) {

                    await component.initialize(this);

                }

            }

        } finally {

            this.starting = false;

        }

    }

    async start() {

        if (this.started) {

            return;

        }

        await this.initialize();

        this.started = true;

        this.stopping = false;

        this.loop.start();

        console.log(

            `ISLAMCRAFT server started on ` +

            `${this.config.getHost()}:` +

            `${this.config.getPort()}`

        );

    }

    async update(deltaTime, tick) {

        for (const component of this.components.values()) {

            if (typeof component.update !== "function") {

                continue;

            }

            await component.update(

                deltaTime,

                tick

            );

        }

    }

    async stop() {

        if (!this.started || this.stopping) {

            return;

        }

        this.stopping = true;

        this.loop?.stop();

        await this.cleanup.cleanup();

        this.started = false;

        this.stopping = false;

        console.log("ISLAMCRAFT server stopped.");

    }

    isRunning() {

        return this.started;

    }

    getStatus() {

        return {

            running: this.started,

            starting: this.starting,

            stopping: this.stopping,

            tick: this.clock.getTick(),

            uptime: this.clock.getElapsedTime(),

            components: this.components.size

        };

    }

}

export class ServerClock {

    constructor(tickRate = 20) {

        this.tickRate = Math.max(

            1,

            Number(tickRate) || 20

        );

        this.tickDuration =

            1000 / this.tickRate;

        this.startTime = 0;

        this.lastTime = 0;

        this.tick = 0;

        this.deltaTime = 0;

        this.running = false;

    }

    start() {

        const now = Date.now();

        this.startTime = now;

        this.lastTime = now;

        this.tick = 0;

        this.deltaTime = 0;

        this.running = true;

    }

    update() {

        if (!this.running) {

            return 0;

        }

        const now = Date.now();

        this.deltaTime = Math.max(

            0,

            (now - this.lastTime) / 1000

        );

        this.lastTime = now;

        this.tick++;

        return this.deltaTime;

    }

    stop() {

        this.running = false;

    }

    getTick() {

        return this.tick;

    }

    getDeltaTime() {

        return this.deltaTime;

    }

    getElapsedTime() {

        if (!this.startTime) {

            return 0;

        }

        return (Date.now() - this.startTime) / 1000;

    }

    getTickDuration() {

        return this.tickDuration;

    }

    getTickRate() {

        return this.tickRate;

    }

    isRunning() {

        return this.running;

    }

}

import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

const __filename = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
const __dirname = path.dirname(__filename);

const projectRoot = path.resolve(__dirname, "../../..");

export class ServerConfig {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.projectRoot = projectRoot;

        this.configPath = options.configPath ??

            path.join(

                this.projectRoot,

                "config",

                "server.json"

            );

        this.values = {};

    }

    load() {

        if (!fs.existsSync(this.configPath)) {

            throw new Error(

                `Configuration serveur introuvable: ${this.configPath}`

            );

        }

        const raw = fs.readFileSync(

            this.configPath,

            "utf8"

        );

        this.values = JSON.parse(raw);

        return this.values;

    }

    get(path, fallback = undefined) {

        const parts = String(path).split(".");

        let current = this.values;

        for (const part of parts) {

            if (

                current === null ||

                current === undefined ||

                typeof current !== "object" ||

                !(part in current)

            ) {

                return fallback;

            }

            current = current[part];

        }

        return current;

    }

    getHost() {

        return this.get(

            "server.host",

            "0.0.0.0"

        );

    }

    getPort() {

        return this.get(

            "server.port",

            3000

        );

    }

    getMaxPlayers() {

        return this.get(

            "server.maxPlayers",

            100

        );

    }

    getTickRate() {

        return this.get(

            "server.tickRate",

            20

        );

    }

    getWorldChunkSize() {

        return this.get(

            "world.chunkSize",

            16

        );

    }

    getWorldRenderDistance() {

        return this.get(

            "world.renderDistance",

            8

        );

    }

    getNetworkTimeout() {

        return this.get(

            "network.timeout",

            10000

        );

    }

    getHeartbeatInterval() {

        return this.get(

            "network.heartbeatInterval",

            5000

        );

    }

}

export class EntityFactory {

    constructor() {

        this.types = new Map();

    }

    register(type, EntityClass) {

        if (!type || typeof EntityClass !== "function") {

            throw new TypeError(

                "Type et classe d'entité valides requis."

            );

        }

        this.types.set(type, EntityClass);

        return this;

    }

    create(type, data = {}) {

        const EntityClass = this.types.get(type);

        if (!EntityClass) {

            throw new Error(

                `Type d'entité inconnu: ${type}`

            );

        }

        return new EntityClass(data);

    }

    has(type) {

        return this.types.has(type);

    }

    unregister(type) {

        return this.types.delete(type);

    }

    clear() {

        this.types.clear();

    }

    getTypes() {

        return [...this.types.keys()];

    }

}

export class EntityManager {

    constructor() {

        this.entities = new Map();

        this.nextId = 1;

    }

    generateId() {

        return `entity_${this.nextId++}`;

    }

    add(entity, id = null) {

        if (!entity) {

            throw new TypeError(

                "Une entité est requise."

            );

        }

        const entityId =
            id ??
            entity.id ??
            this.generateId();

        if (this.entities.has(entityId)) {

            throw new Error(

                `Entité déjà enregistrée: ${entityId}`

            );

        }

        entity.id = entityId;

        this.entities.set(entityId, entity);

        return entity;

    }

    get(id) {

        return this.entities.get(id) ?? null;

    }

    has(id) {

        return this.entities.has(id);

    }

    remove(id) {

        const entity = this.entities.get(id);

        if (!entity) {

            return false;

        }

        if (typeof entity.destroy === "function") {

            entity.destroy();

        }

        this.entities.delete(id);

        return true;

    }

    update(deltaTime, tick) {

        for (const entity of this.entities.values()) {

            if (typeof entity.update === "function") {

                entity.update(deltaTime, tick);

            }

        }

    }

    getAll() {

        return [...this.entities.values()];

    }

    getByType(type) {

        return this.getAll().filter(

            entity => entity.type === type

        );

    }

    clear() {

        for (const entity of this.entities.values()) {

            if (typeof entity.destroy === "function") {

                entity.destroy();

            }

        }

        this.entities.clear();

    }

    get size() {

        return this.entities.size;

    }

}

import {
    ChestState,
    isValidChestState
} from "./ChestState.js";

export class ChestEntity {

    constructor(data = {}) {

        this.id = data.id ?? null;

        this.type = "chest";

        this.position = {

            x: Number(data.position?.x ?? 0),

            y: Number(data.position?.y ?? 0),

            z: Number(data.position?.z ?? 0)

        };

        this.lootTable =

            data.lootTable ?? "common_chest";

        this.state =

            isValidChestState(data.state)

                ? data.state

                : ChestState.CLOSED;

        this.openedBy = data.openedBy ?? null;

        this.lastOpenedAt =

            Number(data.lastOpenedAt ?? 0);

        this.respawnAt =

            Number(data.respawnAt ?? 0);

        this.interactionDistance =

            Number(data.interactionDistance ?? 3);

    }

    setState(state) {

        if (!isValidChestState(state)) {

            throw new Error(

                `État de coffre invalide: ${state}`

            );

        }

        this.state = state;

    }

    setPosition(x, y, z) {

        this.position.x = Number(x);

        this.position.y = Number(y);

        this.position.z = Number(z);

    }

    canInteract(playerPosition) {

        if (!playerPosition) {

            return false;

        }

        if (

            this.state !== ChestState.CLOSED

        ) {

            return false;

        }

        const dx =

            Number(playerPosition.x) -

            this.position.x;

        const dy =

            Number(playerPosition.y) -

            this.position.y;

        const dz =

            Number(playerPosition.z) -

            this.position.z;

        return (

            dx * dx +

            dy * dy +

            dz * dz

        ) <= this.interactionDistance ** 2;

    }

    open(playerId, timestamp = Date.now()) {

        if (

            this.state !== ChestState.CLOSED

        ) {

            return false;

        }

        this.openedBy = playerId ?? null;

        this.lastOpenedAt = timestamp;

        this.setState(ChestState.OPEN);

        return true;

    }

    empty() {

        this.setState(ChestState.EMPTY);

    }

    scheduleRespawn(timestamp) {

        this.respawnAt = Number(timestamp);

        this.setState(ChestState.RESPAWNING);

    }

    respawn() {

        this.openedBy = null;

        this.respawnAt = 0;

        this.setState(ChestState.CLOSED);

    }

    isReadyToRespawn(timestamp = Date.now()) {

        return (

            this.state === ChestState.RESPAWNING &&

            this.respawnAt > 0 &&

            timestamp >= this.respawnAt

        );

    }

    serialize() {

        return {

            id: this.id,

            type: this.type,

            position: { ...this.position },

            lootTable: this.lootTable,

            state: this.state,

            openedBy: this.openedBy,

            lastOpenedAt: this.lastOpenedAt,

            respawnAt: this.respawnAt

        };

    }

    destroy() {

        this.openedBy = null;

        this.state = ChestState.EMPTY;

    }

}

import { ChestEntity } from "./ChestEntity.js";
import { ChestState } from "./ChestState.js";

export class ChestManager {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.chests = new Map();

        this.defaultLootTable =

            options.defaultLootTable ??

            "common_chest";

    }

    initialize(server) {

        this.server = server;

        return this;

    }

    addChest(data = {}) {

        const chest =

            data instanceof ChestEntity

                ? data

                : new ChestEntity({

                    ...data,

                    lootTable:

                        data.lootTable ??

                        this.defaultLootTable

                });

        if (!chest.id) {

            throw new Error(

                "Un identifiant de coffre est requis."

            );

        }

        if (this.chests.has(chest.id)) {

            throw new Error(

                `Coffre déjà présent: ${chest.id}`

            );

        }

        this.chests.set(chest.id, chest);

        return chest;

    }

    removeChest(chestId) {

        const chest = this.chests.get(chestId);

        if (!chest) {

            return false;

        }

        chest.destroy();

        this.chests.delete(chestId);

        return true;

    }

    getChest(chestId) {

        return this.chests.get(chestId) ?? null;

    }

    getChests() {

        return [...this.chests.values()];

    }

    getAvailableChests() {

        return this.getChests().filter(

            chest =>

                chest.state === ChestState.CLOSED

        );

    }

    openChest(chestId, playerId, playerPosition) {

        const chest = this.getChest(chestId);

        if (!chest) {

            return {

                success: false,

                reason: "chest_not_found"

            };

        }

        if (!chest.canInteract(playerPosition)) {

            return {

                success: false,

                reason: "cannot_interact"

            };

        }

        if (!chest.open(playerId)) {

            return {

                success: false,

                reason: "chest_not_available"

            };

        }

        return {

            success: true,

            chest

        };

    }

    update(deltaTime, tick) {

        for (const chest of this.chests.values()) {

            if (chest.isReadyToRespawn()) {

                chest.respawn();

            }

        }

    }

    clear() {

        for (const chest of this.chests.values()) {

            chest.destroy();

        }

        this.chests.clear();

    }

    get size() {

        return this.chests.size;

    }

}

export class ChestRespawnManager {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.respawnDelay =

            Number(options.respawnDelay ?? 300000);

        this.chestManager =

            options.chestManager ?? null;

    }

    initialize(server) {

        this.server = server;

        if (!this.chestManager) {

            this.chestManager =

                server.getComponent?.("chests") ??

                null;

        }

        return this;

    }

    schedule(chest, delay = this.respawnDelay) {

        if (!chest) {

            return false;

        }

        const respawnAt =

            Date.now() +

            Math.max(0, Number(delay));

        chest.scheduleRespawn(respawnAt);

        return respawnAt;

    }

    scheduleAfterOpen(chest) {

        return this.schedule(

            chest,

            this.respawnDelay

        );

    }

    update(timestamp = Date.now()) {

        if (!this.chestManager) {

            return 0;

        }

        let respawned = 0;

        for (

            const chest

            of this.chestManager.getChests()

        ) {

            if (chest.isReadyToRespawn(timestamp)) {

                chest.respawn();

                respawned++;

            }

        }

        return respawned;

    }

}

export function isValidChestState(state) {

import {
    PlayerState,
    isValidPlayerState
} from "./PlayerState.js";

export class PlayerEntity {

    constructor(data = {}) {

        this.id = data.id ?? null;

        this.type = "player";

        this.name = data.name ?? "Player";

        this.state =

            isValidPlayerState(data.state)

                ? data.state

                : PlayerState.CONNECTING;

        this.position = {

            x: Number(data.position?.x ?? 0),

            y: Number(data.position?.y ?? 0),

            z: Number(data.position?.z ?? 0)

        };

        this.rotation = {

            x: Number(data.rotation?.x ?? 0),

            y: Number(data.rotation?.y ?? 0),

            z: Number(data.rotation?.z ?? 0)

        };

        this.velocity = {

            x: 0,

            y: 0,

            z: 0

        };

        this.health = Number(data.health ?? 100);

        this.maxHealth = Number(data.maxHealth ?? 100);

        this.xp = Number(data.xp ?? 0);

        this.level = Number(data.level ?? 1);

        this.score = Number(data.score ?? 0);

        this.skinId = data.skinId ?? "default";

        this.socketId = data.socketId ?? null;

        this.connectedAt = Date.now();

    }

    setState(state) {

        if (!isValidPlayerState(state)) {

            throw new Error(

                `État joueur invalide: ${state}`

            );

        }

        this.state = state;

    }

    setPosition(x, y, z) {

        this.position.x = Number(x);

        this.position.y = Number(y);

        this.position.z = Number(z);

    }

    setRotation(x, y, z) {

        this.rotation.x = Number(x);

        this.rotation.y = Number(y);

        this.rotation.z = Number(z);

    }

    setVelocity(x, y, z) {

        this.velocity.x = Number(x);

        this.velocity.y = Number(y);

        this.velocity.z = Number(z);

    }

    addXp(amount) {

        const value = Number(amount);

        if (!Number.isFinite(value) || value < 0) {

            return false;

        }

        this.xp += value;

        this.updateLevel();

        return true;

    }

    updateLevel() {

        this.level = Math.max(

            1,

            Math.floor(this.xp / 100) + 1

        );

    }

    addScore(amount) {

        const value = Number(amount);

        if (!Number.isFinite(value) || value < 0) {

            return false;

        }

        this.score += value;

        return true;

    }

    damage(amount) {

        const value = Number(amount);

        if (!Number.isFinite(value) || value < 0) {

            return false;

        }

        this.health = Math.max(

            0,

            this.health - value

        );

        if (this.health === 0) {

            this.setState(PlayerState.DEAD);

        }

        return true;

    }

    heal(amount) {

        const value = Number(amount);

        if (!Number.isFinite(value) || value < 0) {

            return false;

        }

        this.health = Math.min(

            this.maxHealth,

            this.health + value

        );

        return true;

    }

    respawn(position = {}) {

        this.setPosition(

            position.x ?? 0,

            position.y ?? 0,

            position.z ?? 0

        );

        this.health = this.maxHealth;

        this.setState(PlayerState.ACTIVE);

    }

    update(deltaTime) {

        if (this.state !== PlayerState.ACTIVE) {

            return;

        }

        this.position.x +=

            this.velocity.x * deltaTime;

        this.position.y +=

            this.velocity.y * deltaTime;

        this.position.z +=

            this.velocity.z * deltaTime;

    }

    serialize() {

        return {

            id: this.id,

            type: this.type,

            name: this.name,

            state: this.state,

            position: { ...this.position },

            rotation: { ...this.rotation },

            velocity: { ...this.velocity },

            health: this.health,

            maxHealth: this.maxHealth,

            xp: this.xp,

            level: this.level,

            score: this.score,

            skinId: this.skinId

        };

    }

    destroy() {

        this.socketId = null;

        this.setState(PlayerState.DISCONNECTED);

    }

}

import { PlayerEntity } from "./PlayerEntity.js";
import { PlayerState } from "./PlayerState.js";

export class PlayerManager {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.players = new Map();

        this.maxPlayers =

            Number(options.maxPlayers ?? 100);

    }

    initialize(server) {

        this.server = server;

        return this;

    }

    addPlayer(data = {}) {

        if (this.players.size >= this.maxPlayers) {

            throw new Error(

                "Nombre maximum de joueurs atteint."

            );

        }

        const player =

            data instanceof PlayerEntity

                ? data

                : new PlayerEntity(data);

        if (!player.id) {

            throw new Error(

                "Un identifiant joueur est requis."

            );

        }

        if (this.players.has(player.id)) {

            throw new Error(

                `Joueur déjà présent: ${player.id}`

            );

        }

        player.setState(PlayerState.ACTIVE);

        this.players.set(player.id, player);

        return player;

    }

    removePlayer(playerId) {

        const player = this.players.get(playerId);

        if (!player) {

            return false;

        }

        player.destroy();

        this.players.delete(playerId);

        return true;

    }

    getPlayer(playerId) {

        return this.players.get(playerId) ?? null;

    }

    getPlayerBySocket(socketId) {

        for (const player of this.players.values()) {

            if (player.socketId === socketId) {

                return player;

            }

        }

        return null;

    }

    hasPlayer(playerId) {

        return this.players.has(playerId);

    }

    getPlayers() {

        return [...this.players.values()];

    }

    update(deltaTime, tick) {

        for (const player of this.players.values()) {

            player.update(deltaTime, tick);

        }

    }

    broadcast(callback) {

        if (typeof callback !== "function") {

            return;

        }

        for (const player of this.players.values()) {

            callback(player);

        }

    }

    clear() {

        for (const player of this.players.values()) {

            player.destroy();

        }

        this.players.clear();

    }

    get size() {

        return this.players.size;

    }

}

export class PlayerSpawnManager {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.spawns = [];

        this.defaultSpawn = {

            x: Number(options.x ?? 0),

            y: Number(options.y ?? 0),

            z: Number(options.z ?? 0)

        };

        if (Array.isArray(options.spawns)) {

            for (const spawn of options.spawns) {

                this.addSpawn(spawn);

            }

        }

    }

    addSpawn(position) {

        if (!this.isValidPosition(position)) {

            throw new TypeError(

                "Position de spawn invalide."

            );

        }

        const spawn = {

            x: Number(position.x),

            y: Number(position.y),

            z: Number(position.z)

        };

        this.spawns.push(spawn);

        return spawn;

    }

    removeSpawn(index) {

        if (

            !Number.isInteger(index) ||

            index < 0 ||

            index >= this.spawns.length

        ) {

            return false;

        }

        this.spawns.splice(index, 1);

        return true;

    }

    getSpawn(index = null) {

        if (this.spawns.length === 0) {

            return { ...this.defaultSpawn };

        }

        if (

            Number.isInteger(index) &&

            this.spawns[index]

        ) {

            return { ...this.spawns[index] };

        }

        const randomIndex = Math.floor(

            Math.random() * this.spawns.length

        );

        return { ...this.spawns[randomIndex] };

    }

    spawnPlayer(player, index = null) {

        if (!player) {

            throw new TypeError(

                "Un joueur est requis."

            );

        }

        const position = this.getSpawn(index);

        player.respawn(position);

        return position;

    }

    isValidPosition(position) {

        return Boolean(

            position &&

            Number.isFinite(Number(position.x)) &&

            Number.isFinite(Number(position.y)) &&

            Number.isFinite(Number(position.z))

        );

    }

    getAllSpawns() {

        return this.spawns.map(

            spawn => ({ ...spawn })

        );

    }

    clear() {

        this.spawns.length = 0;

    }

}

import { WorldObjectEntity } from "./WorldObjectEntity.js";

export class InteractiveEntity extends WorldObjectEntity {

    constructor(data = {}) {

        super({

            ...data,

            type: data.type ?? "interactive"

        });

        this.interactionDistance =

            Number(data.interactionDistance ?? 3);

        this.cooldown =

            Number(data.cooldown ?? 0);

        this.lastInteractionAt =

            Number(data.lastInteractionAt ?? 0);

        this.interactionEnabled =

            data.interactionEnabled !== false;

    }

    setInteractionEnabled(enabled) {

        this.interactionEnabled = Boolean(enabled);

    }

    canInteract(playerPosition, timestamp = Date.now()) {

        if (!this.active || !this.interactionEnabled) {

            return false;

        }

        if (!playerPosition) {

            return false;

        }

        if (

            this.cooldown > 0 &&

            timestamp - this.lastInteractionAt <

                this.cooldown

        ) {

            return false;

        }

        const dx =

            Number(playerPosition.x) -

            this.position.x;

        const dy =

            Number(playerPosition.y) -

            this.position.y;

        const dz =

            Number(playerPosition.z) -

            this.position.z;

        const distanceSquared =

            dx * dx +

            dy * dy +

            dz * dz;

        return (

            distanceSquared <=

            this.interactionDistance ** 2

        );

    }

    interact(player, timestamp = Date.now()) {

        if (!this.canInteract(

            player?.position,

            timestamp

        )) {

            return {

                success: false,

                reason: "interaction_unavailable"

            };

        }

        this.lastInteractionAt = timestamp;

        return {

            success: true,

            entityId: this.id,

            playerId: player?.id ?? null

        };

    }

    update(deltaTime, tick) {

        super.update(deltaTime, tick);

    }

    serialize() {

        return {

            ...super.serialize(),

            interactionDistance:

                this.interactionDistance,

            cooldown: this.cooldown,

            lastInteractionAt:

                this.lastInteractionAt,

            interactionEnabled:

                this.interactionEnabled

        };

    }

}
