export class WorldObjectEntity {

    constructor(data = {}) {

        this.id = data.id ?? null;

        this.type = data.type ?? "world_object";

        this.name = data.name ?? this.type;

        this.position = {

            x: Number(data.position?.x ?? 0),

            y: Number(data.position?.y ?? 0),

            z: Number(data.position?.z ?? 0)

        };

        this.rotation = {

            x: Number(data.rotation?.x ?? 0),

            y: Number(data.rotation?.y ?? 0),

            z: Number(data.rotation?.z ?? 0)

        };

        this.scale = {

            x: Number(data.scale?.x ?? 1),

            y: Number(data.scale?.y ?? 1),

            z: Number(data.scale?.z ?? 1)

        };

        this.active = data.active !== false;

        this.metadata = {

            ...(data.metadata ?? {})

        };

    }

    setPosition(x, y, z) {

        this.position.x = Number(x);

        this.position.y = Number(y);

        this.position.z = Number(z);

    }

    setRotation(x, y, z) {

        this.rotation.x = Number(x);

        this.rotation.y = Number(y);

        this.rotation.z = Number(z);

    }

    setScale(x, y, z) {

        this.scale.x = Number(x);

        this.scale.y = Number(y);

        this.scale.z = Number(z);

    }

    setActive(active) {

        this.active = Boolean(active);

    }

    setMetadata(key, value) {

        if (!key) {

            return false;

        }

        this.metadata[key] = value;

        return true;

    }

    getMetadata(key, fallback = null) {

        return this.metadata[key] ?? fallback;

    }

    update(deltaTime, tick) {

        if (!this.active) {

            return;

        }

    }

    serialize() {

        return {

            id: this.id,

            type: this.type,

            name: this.name,

            position: { ...this.position },

            rotation: { ...this.rotation },

            scale: { ...this.scale },

            active: this.active,

            metadata: { ...this.metadata }

        };

    }

    destroy() {

        this.active = false;

        this.metadata = {};

    }

}

import { GameSession } from "./GameSession.js";
import { MatchManager } from "./MatchManager.js";

export class GameManager {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.sessions = new Map();

        this.matchManager =
            options.matchManager ??
            new MatchManager(options);

        this.nextSessionId = 1;

        this.server = null;

        this.running = false;

    }

    initialize(server = null) {

        this.server = server;

        return this;

    }

    generateSessionId() {

        return `session_${this.nextSessionId++}`;

    }

    createSession(data = {}) {

        const id =
            data.id ??
            this.generateSessionId();

        if (this.sessions.has(id)) {

            throw new Error(
                `Session déjà existante: ${id}`
            );

        }

        const session =
            new GameSession({

                ...data,

                id

            });

        this.sessions.set(
            id,
            session
        );

        return session;

    }

    getSession(id) {

        return (
            this.sessions.get(id) ??
            null
        );

    }

    hasSession(id) {

        return this.sessions.has(id);

    }

    removeSession(id) {

        const session =
            this.sessions.get(id);

        if (!session) {

            return false;

        }

        session.clearPlayers();

        this.sessions.delete(id);

        return true;

    }

    addPlayer(sessionId, player) {

        const session =
            this.getSession(sessionId);

        if (!session) {

            return false;

        }

        return session.addPlayer(
            player
        );

    }

    removePlayer(sessionId, playerId) {

        const session =
            this.getSession(sessionId);

        if (!session) {

            return false;

        }

        return session.removePlayer(
            playerId
        );

    }

    startSession(
        sessionId,
        timestamp = Date.now()
    ) {

        const session =
            this.getSession(sessionId);

        if (!session) {

            return false;

        }

        return session.start(
            timestamp
        );

    }

    finishSession(
        sessionId,
        timestamp = Date.now()
    ) {

        const session =
            this.getSession(sessionId);

        if (!session) {

            return false;

        }

        return session.finish(
            timestamp
        );

    }

    start() {

        this.running = true;

        return this;

    }

    stop() {

        this.running = false;

        this.matchManager.update();

        return this;

    }

    update(
        deltaTime = 0,
        timestamp = Date.now()
    ) {

        if (!this.running) {

            return;

        }

        this.matchManager.update(
            timestamp
        );

        for (const session of this.sessions.values()) {

            if (
                session.status !== "active"
            ) {

                continue;

            }

            for (
                const player
                of session.getPlayers()
            ) {

                if (
                    typeof player.update ===
                    "function"
                ) {

                    player.update(
                        deltaTime
                    );

                }

            }

        }

    }

    getSessions() {

        return [
            ...this.sessions.values()
        ];

    }

    getActiveSessions() {

        return this.getSessions().filter(
            session =>
                session.status === "active"
        );

    }

    clear() {

        for (const session of this.sessions.values()) {

            session.clearPlayers();

        }

        this.sessions.clear();

        this.matchManager.clear();

    }

    destroy() {

        this.stop();

        this.clear();

        this.server = null;

    }

}

export class GameSession {

    constructor(data = {}) {

        this.id =
            data.id ?? null;

        this.status =
            data.status ?? "waiting";

        this.players = new Map();

        this.createdAt =
            Number(
                data.createdAt ?? Date.now()
            );

        this.startedAt =
            Number(
                data.startedAt ?? 0
            );

        this.endedAt =
            Number(
                data.endedAt ?? 0
            );

        this.metadata = {

            ...(data.metadata ?? {})

        };

    }

    addPlayer(player) {

        if (!player || !player.id) {

            return false;

        }

        if (this.players.has(player.id)) {

            return false;

        }

        this.players.set(
            player.id,
            player
        );

        return true;

    }

    removePlayer(playerId) {

        return this.players.delete(
            playerId
        );

    }

    getPlayer(playerId) {

        return (
            this.players.get(playerId) ??
            null
        );

    }

    hasPlayer(playerId) {

        return this.players.has(
            playerId
        );

    }

    getPlayers() {

        return [
            ...this.players.values()
        ];

    }

    start(timestamp = Date.now()) {

        if (
            this.status !== "waiting"
        ) {

            return false;

        }

        this.status = "active";

        this.startedAt = timestamp;

        return true;

    }

    finish(timestamp = Date.now()) {

        if (
            this.status === "finished"
        ) {

            return false;

        }

        this.status = "finished";

        this.endedAt = timestamp;

        return true;

    }

    setStatus(status) {

        if (!status) {

            return false;

        }

        this.status = status;

        return true;

    }

    setMetadata(key, value) {

        if (!key) {

            return false;

        }

        this.metadata[key] = value;

        return true;

    }

    getMetadata(key, fallback = null) {

        return (
            this.metadata[key] ??
            fallback
        );

    }

    clearPlayers() {

        this.players.clear();

    }

    serialize() {

        return {

            id: this.id,

            status: this.status,

            players: this.getPlayers().map(
                player =>
                    typeof player.serialize === "function"
                        ? player.serialize()
                        : player
            ),

            createdAt:
                this.createdAt,

            startedAt:
                this.startedAt,

            endedAt:
                this.endedAt,

            metadata:
                { ...this.metadata }

        };

    }

}

import { MatchTimer } from "./MatchTimer.js";

export class MatchManager {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.matches = new Map();

        this.defaultDuration =
            Number(
                options.matchDuration ?? 600000
            );

        this.nextId = 1;

    }

    generateId() {

        return `match_${this.nextId++}`;

    }

    create(options = {}) {

        const id =
            options.id ??
            this.generateId();

        if (this.matches.has(id)) {

            throw new Error(
                `Match déjà existant: ${id}`
            );

        }

        const match = {

            id,

            status:
                "waiting",

            players:
                new Set(),

            timer:
                new MatchTimer(
                    options.duration ??
                    this.defaultDuration
                ),

            createdAt:
                Date.now(),

            metadata:
                {
                    ...(options.metadata ?? {})
                }

        };

        this.matches.set(
            id,
            match
        );

        return match;

    }

    get(id) {

        return (
            this.matches.get(id) ??
            null
        );

    }

    has(id) {

        return this.matches.has(id);

    }

    addPlayer(matchId, playerId) {

        const match =
            this.get(matchId);

        if (!match || !playerId) {

            return false;

        }

        match.players.add(
            playerId
        );

        return true;

    }

    removePlayer(matchId, playerId) {

        const match =
            this.get(matchId);

        if (!match) {

            return false;

        }

        return match.players.delete(
            playerId
        );

    }

    start(matchId, timestamp = Date.now()) {

        const match =
            this.get(matchId);

        if (!match) {

            return false;

        }

        match.status = "active";

        match.startedAt =
            timestamp;

        match.timer.start(
            timestamp
        );

        return true;

    }

    stop(matchId, timestamp = Date.now()) {

        const match =
            this.get(matchId);

        if (!match) {

            return false;

        }

        match.timer.pause(
            timestamp
        );

        match.status = "finished";

        match.endedAt =
            timestamp;

        return true;

    }

    update(timestamp = Date.now()) {

        for (const match of this.matches.values()) {

            if (
                match.status !== "active"
            ) {

                continue;

            }

            match.timer.update(
                timestamp
            );

            if (
                match.timer.isFinished(
                    timestamp
                )
            ) {

                match.status =
                    "finished";

                match.endedAt =
                    timestamp;

            }

        }

    }

    delete(matchId) {

        return this.matches.delete(
            matchId
        );

    }

    getAll() {

        return [
            ...this.matches.values()
        ];

    }

    getActive() {

        return this.getAll().filter(
            match =>
                match.status === "active"
        );

    }

    clear() {

        this.matches.clear();

    }

}

export class MatchTimer {

    constructor(duration = 0) {

        this.duration = Math.max(
            0,
            Number(duration)
        );

        this.remaining = this.duration;

        this.running = false;

        this.startedAt = 0;

        this.lastUpdate = 0;

    }

    start(timestamp = Date.now()) {

        if (this.running) {

            return false;

        }

        this.running = true;

        this.startedAt = timestamp;

        this.lastUpdate = timestamp;

        return true;

    }

    pause(timestamp = Date.now()) {

        if (!this.running) {

            return false;

        }

        this.update(timestamp);

        this.running = false;

        return true;

    }

    resume(timestamp = Date.now()) {

        if (this.running || this.remaining <= 0) {

            return false;

        }

        this.running = true;

        this.lastUpdate = timestamp;

        return true;

    }

    reset(duration = this.duration) {

        this.duration = Math.max(
            0,
            Number(duration)
        );

        this.remaining = this.duration;

        this.running = false;

        this.startedAt = 0;

        this.lastUpdate = 0;

    }

    update(timestamp = Date.now()) {

        if (!this.running) {

            return this.remaining;

        }

        const elapsed =
            Math.max(
                0,
                timestamp - this.lastUpdate
            );

        this.remaining = Math.max(
            0,
            this.remaining - elapsed
        );

        this.lastUpdate = timestamp;

        if (this.remaining <= 0) {

            this.running = false;

        }

        return this.remaining;

    }

    isFinished(timestamp = Date.now()) {

        this.update(timestamp);

        return this.remaining <= 0;

    }

    getRemaining(timestamp = Date.now()) {

        return this.update(timestamp);

    }

    getRemainingSeconds(timestamp = Date.now()) {

        return this.getRemaining(timestamp) / 1000;

    }

    serialize(timestamp = Date.now()) {

        return {

            duration: this.duration,

            remaining: this.getRemaining(timestamp),

            running: this.running,

            startedAt: this.startedAt

        };

    }

}

export class InteractionResolver {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.handlers = new Map();

        if (options.handlers) {

            for (
                const [
                    type,
                    handler
                ] of Object.entries(options.handlers)
            ) {

                this.register(
                    type,
                    handler
                );

            }

        }

    }

    register(type, handler) {

        if (!type) {

            throw new TypeError(
                "Un type d'interaction est requis."
            );

        }

        if (
            typeof handler !== "function"
        ) {

            throw new TypeError(
                "Le handler doit être une fonction."
            );

        }

        this.handlers.set(
            type,
            handler
        );

        return this;

    }

    unregister(type) {

        return this.handlers.delete(
            type
        );

    }

    has(type) {

        return this.handlers.has(
            type
        );

    }

    resolve(type) {

        return (
            this.handlers.get(type) ??
            null
        );

    }

    execute(
        type,
        context = {}
    ) {

        const handler =
            this.resolve(type);

        if (!handler) {

            return {

                success: false,

                reason:
                    "interaction_not_supported"

            };

        }

        try {

            const result =
                handler(context);

            return {

                success: true,

                result

            };

        } catch (error) {

            return {

                success: false,

                reason:
                    "interaction_failed",

                error

            };

        }

    }

    getTypes() {

        return [
            ...this.handlers.keys()
        ];

    }

}

import { InteractionValidator } from "./InteractionValidator.js";
import { InteractionResolver } from "./InteractionResolver.js";

export class InteractionSystem {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.validator =
            options.validator ??
            new InteractionValidator(options);

        this.resolver =
            options.resolver ??
            new InteractionResolver(options);

        this.history = [];

        this.maxHistory =
            Number(
                options.maxHistory ?? 100
            );

        this.enabled =
            options.enabled !== false;

    }

    initialize(server) {

        this.server = server;

        return this;

    }

    register(type, handler) {

        this.resolver.register(
            type,
            handler
        );

        return this;

    }

    unregister(type) {

        return this.resolver.unregister(
            type
        );

    }

    interact(
        player,
        target,
        interaction = {}
    ) {

        if (!this.enabled) {

            return {

                success: false,

                reason:
                    "interaction_system_disabled"

            };

        }

        const validation =
            this.validator.validate(
                player,
                target,
                interaction
            );

        if (!validation.valid) {

            return {

                success: false,

                reason:
                    validation.reason,

                distance:
                    validation.distance ??
                    null

            };

        }

        const type =
            interaction.type ??
            validation.type;

        const result =
            this.resolver.execute(
                type,
                {

                    player,

                    target,

                    interaction,

                    validation,

                    system: this

                }
            );

        this.record({

            playerId:
                player?.id ?? null,

            targetId:
                target?.id ?? null,

            type,

            success:
                result.success,

            timestamp:
                Date.now()

        });

        return result;

    }

    record(entry) {

        this.history.push(entry);

        while (
            this.history.length >
            this.maxHistory
        ) {

            this.history.shift();

        }

    }

    getHistory() {

        return [
            ...this.history
        ];

    }

    clearHistory() {

        this.history.length = 0;

    }

    setEnabled(enabled) {

        this.enabled =
            Boolean(enabled);

    }

}

export class InteractionValidator {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.maxDistance =
            Number(options.maxDistance ?? 6);

        this.allowedTypes =
            new Set(
                options.allowedTypes ?? [
                    "block",
                    "entity",
                    "item",
                    "chest",
                    "npc"
                ]
            );

    }

    validateDistance(player, target) {

        if (!player || !target) {

            return {
                valid: false,
                reason: "missing_target"
            };

        }

        const position =
            typeof player.getPosition === "function"
                ? player.getPosition()
                : player.position;

        const targetPosition =
            typeof target.getPosition === "function"
                ? target.getPosition()
                : target.position;

        if (!position || !targetPosition) {

            return {
                valid: false,
                reason: "missing_position"
            };

        }

        const dx =
            Number(position.x ?? 0) -
            Number(targetPosition.x ?? 0);

        const dy =
            Number(position.y ?? 0) -
            Number(targetPosition.y ?? 0);

        const dz =
            Number(position.z ?? 0) -
            Number(targetPosition.z ?? 0);

        const distance =
            Math.sqrt(
                dx * dx +
                dy * dy +
                dz * dz
            );

        return {
            valid:
                distance <= this.maxDistance,
            distance,
            reason:
                distance <= this.maxDistance
                    ? null
                    : "too_far"
        };

    }

    validateType(target) {

        if (!target) {

            return {
                valid: false,
                reason: "missing_target"
            };

        }

        const type =
            target.type ??
            target.interactionType ??
            target.kind;

        if (!type) {

            return {
                valid: false,
                reason: "missing_type"
            };

        }

        return {
            valid:
                this.allowedTypes.has(type),
            type,
            reason:
                this.allowedTypes.has(type)
                    ? null
                    : "invalid_type"
        };

    }

    validate(player, target, interaction = {}) {

        const distanceResult =
            this.validateDistance(
                player,
                target
            );

        if (!distanceResult.valid) {

            return distanceResult;

        }

        const typeResult =
            this.validateType(target);

        if (!typeResult.valid) {

            return typeResult;

        }

        if (
            interaction.type &&
            interaction.type !== typeResult.type
        ) {

            return {
                valid: false,
                reason: "interaction_type_mismatch"
            };

        }

        return {

            valid: true,

            distance:
                distanceResult.distance,

            type:
                typeResult.type

        };

    }

}

export class LootGenerator {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.seed = Number(
            options.seed ?? 0
        );

    }

    random() {

        const value =
            Math.sin(
                this.seed * 12.9898
            ) * 43758.5453123;

        this.seed =
            Math.trunc(
                value * 1000000
            );

        return (
            Math.abs(this.seed) %
            1000000
        ) / 1000000;

    }

    randomFromSeed(seed) {

        const value =
            Math.sin(
                Number(seed) * 12.9898
            ) * 43758.5453123;

        return (
            value -
            Math.floor(value)
        );

    }

    rollTable(table, context = {}) {

        if (!table || table.isEmpty()) {

            return [];

        }

        const results = [];

        const entries =
            table.getEntries();

        const rolls =
            Math.max(
                1,
                Number(table.rolls ?? 1)
            );

        for (let i = 0; i < rolls; i++) {

            const totalWeight =
                entries.reduce(
                    (total, entry) =>
                        total +
                        Math.max(
                            0,
                            Number(entry.weight ?? 1)
                        ),
                    0
                );

            if (totalWeight <= 0) {

                continue;

            }

            const roll =
                this.random() *
                totalWeight;

            let cursor = 0;

            for (const entry of entries) {

                const weight =
                    Math.max(
                        0,
                        Number(entry.weight ?? 1)
                    );

                cursor += weight;

                if (roll <= cursor) {

                    const chance =
                        Number(entry.chance ?? 1);

                    if (
                        chance >= 1 ||
                        this.random() <= chance
                    ) {

                        const min =
                            Math.max(
                                1,
                                Number(entry.min ?? 1)
                            );

                        const max =
                            Math.max(
                                min,
                                Number(entry.max ?? min)
                            );

                        const amount =
                            min +
                            Math.floor(
                                this.random() *
                                (max - min + 1)
                            );

                        results.push({

                            item:
                                entry.item,

                            amount,

                            metadata:
                                entry.metadata
                                    ? {
                                        ...entry.metadata
                                    }
                                    : {},

                            source:
                                context.source ?? null

                        });

                    }

                    break;

                }

            }

        }

        return results;

    }

}

import { LootTable } from "./LootTable.js";
import { LootGenerator } from "./LootGenerator.js";
import { RewardManager } from "./RewardManager.js";

export class LootManager {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.tables = new Map();

        this.generator =
            options.generator ??
            new LootGenerator(options);

        this.rewardManager =
            options.rewardManager ??
            new RewardManager(options);

        this.initialized = false;

    }

    initialize(services = {}) {

        if (services.inventoryService) {

            this.rewardManager.initialize(
                services
            );

        }

        this.initialized = true;

        return this;

    }

    registerTable(table) {

        const lootTable =
            table instanceof LootTable
                ? table
                : new LootTable(table);

        if (!lootTable.id) {

            throw new Error(
                "Une table de loot doit avoir un identifiant."
            );

        }

        this.tables.set(
            lootTable.id,
            lootTable
        );

        return lootTable;

    }

    unregisterTable(id) {

        return this.tables.delete(id);

    }

    getTable(id) {

        return (
            this.tables.get(id) ??
            null
        );

    }

    hasTable(id) {

        return this.tables.has(id);

    }

    generate(id, context = {}) {

        const table =
            this.getTable(id);

        if (!table) {

            return {

                success: false,

                reason:
                    "loot_table_not_found",

                rewards: []

            };

        }

        const rewards =
            this.generator.rollTable(
                table,
                context
            );

        return {

            success: true,

            tableId: id,

            rewards

        };

    }

    generateAndGive(
        player,
        tableId,
        context = {}
    ) {

        const generated =
            this.generate(
                tableId,
                context
            );

        if (!generated.success) {

            return generated;

        }

        const granted =
            this.rewardManager.give(
                player,
                generated.rewards
            );

        return {

            ...granted,

            tableId,

            generatedRewards:
                generated.rewards

        };

    }

    getTables() {

        return [
            ...this.tables.values()
        ];

    }

    serialize() {

        return this.getTables().map(
            table =>
                table.serialize()
        );

    }

    clear() {

        this.tables.clear();

    }

}

export class LootTable {

    constructor(data = {}) {

        this.id = data.id ?? null;

        this.rolls = Math.max(
            1,
            Number(data.rolls ?? 1)
        );

        this.entries = Array.isArray(data.entries)
            ? data.entries.map(entry => ({ ...entry }))
            : [];

    }

    addEntry(entry) {

        if (!entry || !entry.item) {

            throw new TypeError(
                "Une entrée de loot valide est requise."
            );

        }

        this.entries.push({
            ...entry
        });

        return this;

    }

    removeEntry(itemId) {

        const index = this.entries.findIndex(
            entry => entry.item === itemId
        );

        if (index === -1) {

            return false;

        }

        this.entries.splice(index, 1);

        return true;

    }

    getEntries() {

        return this.entries.map(
            entry => ({ ...entry })
        );

    }

    getTotalWeight() {

        return this.entries.reduce(
            (total, entry) =>
                total +
                Math.max(
                    0,
                    Number(entry.weight ?? 1)
                ),
            0
        );

    }

    isEmpty() {

        return this.entries.length === 0;

    }

    serialize() {

        return {

            id: this.id,

            rolls: this.rolls,

            entries: this.getEntries()

        };

    }

}

export class RewardManager {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.inventoryService =
            options.inventoryService ?? null;

        this.rewards = [];

        this.maxHistory =
            Number(
                options.maxHistory ?? 1000
            );

    }

    initialize(services = {}) {

        if (services.inventoryService) {

            this.inventoryService =
                services.inventoryService;

        }

        return this;

    }

    give(player, rewards = []) {

        if (!player) {

            return {
                success: false,
                reason: "missing_player"
            };

        }

        const granted = [];

        for (const reward of rewards) {

            if (
                !reward ||
                !reward.item
            ) {

                continue;

            }

            const amount =
                Math.max(
                    1,
                    Number(reward.amount ?? 1)
                );

            let accepted = true;

            if (
                this.inventoryService &&
                typeof this.inventoryService.addItem ===
                    "function"
            ) {

                accepted =
                    this.inventoryService.addItem(
                        player,
                        reward.item,
                        amount,
                        reward.metadata ?? {}
                    );

            } else if (
                typeof player.addItem ===
                    "function"
            ) {

                accepted =
                    player.addItem(
                        reward.item,
                        amount,
                        reward.metadata ?? {}
                    );

            }

            if (accepted === false) {

                continue;

            }

            const grantedReward = {

                playerId:
                    player.id ?? null,

                item:
                    reward.item,

                amount,

                metadata:
                    reward.metadata
                        ? {
                            ...reward.metadata
                        }
                        : {},

                timestamp:
                    Date.now()

            };

            granted.push(
                grantedReward
            );

            this.rewards.push(
                grantedReward
            );

        }

        this.trimHistory();

        return {

            success: true,

            rewards: granted

        };

    }

    trimHistory() {

        while (
            this.rewards.length >
            this.maxHistory
        ) {

            this.rewards.shift();

        }

    }

    getHistory(playerId = null) {

        if (playerId === null) {

            return [
                ...this.rewards
            ];

        }

        return this.rewards.filter(
            reward =>
                reward.playerId === playerId
        );

    }

    clearHistory() {

        this.rewards.length = 0;

    }

}

export class CollisionSystem {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.world =
            options.world ?? null;

        this.entityRadius =
            Number(options.entityRadius ?? 0.3);

        this.playerHeight =
            Number(options.playerHeight ?? 1.8);

    }

    initialize(world) {

        this.world = world;

        return this;

    }

    isSolid(x, y, z) {

        if (!this.world) {

            return false;

        }

        const chunkSize =
            Number(
                this.world.chunkSize ?? 16
            );

        const chunkX =
            Math.floor(x / chunkSize);

        const chunkZ =
            Math.floor(z / chunkSize);

        const chunk =
            this.world.getChunk?.(
                chunkX,
                chunkZ
            );

        if (!chunk) {

            return false;

        }

        const localX =
            Math.floor(
                x - chunkX * chunkSize
            );

        const localY =
            Math.floor(y);

        const localZ =
            Math.floor(
                z - chunkZ * chunkSize
            );

        const block =
            chunk.getBlock?.(
                localX,
                localY,
                localZ
            );

        if (block === null || block === undefined) {

            return false;

        }

        if (typeof block === "string") {

            return (
                block !== "air" &&
                block !== "water" &&
                block !== "lava"
            );

        }

        return block.solid !== false;

    }

    collides(position) {

        if (!position) {

            return true;

        }

        const radius =
            this.entityRadius;

        const height =
            this.playerHeight;

        const points = [

            [position.x - radius, position.z - radius],
            [position.x + radius, position.z - radius],
            [position.x - radius, position.z + radius],
            [position.x + radius, position.z + radius]

        ];

        const bottom =
            Math.floor(position.y);

        const top =
            Math.ceil(
                position.y + height
            );

        for (const [x, z] of points) {

            for (
                let y = bottom;
                y < top;
                y++
            ) {

                if (
                    this.isSolid(
                        x,
                        y,
                        z
                    )
                ) {

                    return true;

                }

            }

        }

        return false;

    }

    canMove(from, to) {

        if (!from || !to) {

            return false;

        }

        if (this.collides(to)) {

            return false;

        }

        return true;

    }

    resolvePosition(position, fallback = null) {

        if (
            !this.collides(position)
        ) {

            return {
                ...position
            };

        }

        if (fallback) {

            return {
                ...fallback
            };

        }

        return null;

    }

}

export class MovementReconciliation {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.maxHistory =
            Number(options.maxHistory ?? 32);

        this.tolerance =
            Number(options.tolerance ?? 0.1);

        this.history = new Map();

    }

    getPlayerHistory(playerId) {

        if (!this.history.has(playerId)) {

            this.history.set(
                playerId,
                []
            );

        }

        return this.history.get(
            playerId
        );

    }

    record(
        playerId,
        state
    ) {

        if (!playerId || !state) {

            return false;

        }

        const history =
            this.getPlayerHistory(
                playerId
            );

        history.push({

            ...state,

            timestamp:
                state.timestamp ??
                Date.now()

        });

        while (
            history.length >
            this.maxHistory
        ) {

            history.shift();

        }

        return true;

    }

    getLatest(playerId) {

        const history =
            this.getPlayerHistory(
                playerId
            );

        return (
            history[
                history.length - 1
            ] ?? null
        );

    }

    compare(
        authoritative,
        client
    ) {

        if (
            !authoritative ||
            !client
        ) {

            return {

                matching: false,

                reason:
                    "missing_state"

            };

        }

        const dx =
            Number(client.x ?? 0) -
            Number(authoritative.x ?? 0);

        const dy =
            Number(client.y ?? 0) -
            Number(authoritative.y ?? 0);

        const dz =
            Number(client.z ?? 0) -
            Number(authoritative.z ?? 0);

        const distance =
            Math.sqrt(
                dx * dx +
                dy * dy +
                dz * dz
            );

        return {

            matching:
                distance <= this.tolerance,

            distance,

            difference: {
                x: dx,
                y: dy,
                z: dz
            }

        };

    }

    reconcile(
        playerId,
        authoritative,
        client
    ) {

        const comparison =
            this.compare(
                authoritative,
                client
            );

        this.record(
            playerId,
            authoritative
        );

        if (comparison.matching) {

            return {

                corrected: false,

                state:
                    client,

                comparison

            };

        }

        return {

            corrected: true,

            state: {
                ...authoritative
            },

            comparison

        };

    }

    clear(playerId = null) {

        if (playerId === null) {

            this.history.clear();

            return;

        }

        this.history.delete(
            playerId
        );

    }

}

import { MovementValidator } from "./MovementValidator.js";
import { CollisionSystem } from "./CollisionSystem.js";
import { MovementReconciliation } from "./MovementReconciliation.js";

export class MovementSystem {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.validator =
            options.validator ??
            new MovementValidator(options);

        this.collision =
            options.collision ??
            new CollisionSystem(options);

        this.reconciliation =
            options.reconciliation ??
            new MovementReconciliation(options);

        this.players = new Map();

        this.enabled =
            options.enabled !== false;

    }

    initialize(world) {

        this.world = world;

        this.collision.initialize(
            world
        );

        return this;

    }

    registerPlayer(player) {

        if (!player || !player.id) {

            return false;

        }

        const position =
            typeof player.getPosition ===
            "function"
                ? player.getPosition()
                : (
                    player.position ?? {
                        x: 0,
                        y: 0,
                        z: 0
                    }
                );

        this.players.set(
            player.id,
            {

                player,

                position: {
                    ...position
                },

                velocity: {
                    x: 0,
                    y: 0,
                    z: 0
                }

            }
        );

        return true;

    }

    unregisterPlayer(playerId) {

        this.reconciliation.clear(
            playerId
        );

        return this.players.delete(
            playerId
        );

    }

    getState(playerId) {

        return (
            this.players.get(
                playerId
            ) ?? null
        );

    }

    move(
        playerId,
        nextPosition,
        velocity = {
            x: 0,
            y: 0,
            z: 0
        },
        deltaTime = 0
    ) {

        if (!this.enabled) {

            return {

                accepted: false,

                reason:
                    "movement_disabled"

            };

        }

        const state =
            this.getState(
                playerId
            );

        if (!state) {

            return {

                accepted: false,

                reason:
                    "player_not_registered"

            };

        }

        const validation =
            this.validator.validateMovement(
                state.position,
                nextPosition,
                velocity,
                deltaTime
            );

        if (!validation.valid) {

            return {

                accepted: false,

                reason:
                    validation.reason,

                validation

            };

        }

        if (
            !this.collision.canMove(
                state.position,
                nextPosition
            )
        ) {

            return {

                accepted: false,

                reason:
                    "collision"

            };

        }

        state.position = {
            ...nextPosition
        };

        state.velocity = {
            ...velocity
        };

        if (
            typeof state.player.setPosition ===
            "function"
        ) {

            state.player.setPosition(
                nextPosition.x,
                nextPosition.y,
                nextPosition.z
            );

        } else {

            state.player.position = {
                ...nextPosition
            };

        }

        this.reconciliation.record(
            playerId,
            {

                x: nextPosition.x,

                y: nextPosition.y,

                z: nextPosition.z,

                timestamp:
                    Date.now()

            }
        );

        return {

            accepted: true,

            position: {
                ...state.position
            },

            velocity: {
                ...state.velocity
            }

        };

    }

    reconcile(
        playerId,
        clientPosition
    ) {

        const state =
            this.getState(
                playerId
            );

        if (!state) {

            return {

                corrected: true,

                reason:
                    "player_not_registered"

            };

        }

        return this.reconciliation.reconcile(
            playerId,
            state.position,
            clientPosition
        );

    }

    update(deltaTime = 0) {

        if (!this.enabled) {

            return;

        }

        for (const state of this.players.values()) {

            if (
                typeof state.player.updateMovement ===
                "function"
            ) {

                state.player.updateMovement(
                    deltaTime
                );

            }

        }

    }

    clear() {

        this.players.clear();

        this.reconciliation.clear();

    }

}

export class MovementValidator {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.maxSpeed =
            Number(options.maxSpeed ?? 8);

        this.maxVerticalSpeed =
            Number(options.maxVerticalSpeed ?? 12);

        this.maxDeltaTime =
            Number(options.maxDeltaTime ?? 0.25);

        this.positionTolerance =
            Number(options.positionTolerance ?? 1.5);

    }

    validateDeltaTime(deltaTime) {

        const value = Number(deltaTime);

        if (!Number.isFinite(value) || value < 0) {

            return {
                valid: false,
                reason: "invalid_delta_time"
            };

        }

        if (value > this.maxDeltaTime) {

            return {
                valid: false,
                reason: "delta_time_too_large",
                deltaTime: value
            };

        }

        return {
            valid: true,
            deltaTime: value
        };

    }

    validateVelocity(velocity) {

        if (!velocity) {

            return {
                valid: false,
                reason: "missing_velocity"
            };

        }

        const x = Number(velocity.x ?? 0);
        const y = Number(velocity.y ?? 0);
        const z = Number(velocity.z ?? 0);

        if (
            !Number.isFinite(x) ||
            !Number.isFinite(y) ||
            !Number.isFinite(z)
        ) {

            return {
                valid: false,
                reason: "invalid_velocity"
            };

        }

        const horizontalSpeed =
            Math.sqrt(
                x * x +
                z * z
            );

        if (
            horizontalSpeed >
            this.maxSpeed
        ) {

            return {
                valid: false,
                reason: "horizontal_speed_exceeded",
                speed: horizontalSpeed
            };

        }

        if (
            Math.abs(y) >
            this.maxVerticalSpeed
        ) {

            return {
                valid: false,
                reason: "vertical_speed_exceeded",
                speed: Math.abs(y)
            };

        }

        return {
            valid: true,
            speed: horizontalSpeed
        };

    }

    validatePosition(position) {

        if (!position) {

            return {
                valid: false,
                reason: "missing_position"
            };

        }

        const x = Number(position.x);
        const y = Number(position.y);
        const z = Number(position.z);

        if (
            !Number.isFinite(x) ||
            !Number.isFinite(y) ||
            !Number.isFinite(z)
        ) {

            return {
                valid: false,
                reason: "invalid_position"
            };

        }

        return {
            valid: true,
            position: {
                x,
                y,
                z
            }
        };

    }

    validateMovement(
        currentPosition,
        nextPosition,
        velocity,
        deltaTime
    ) {

        const timeResult =
            this.validateDeltaTime(
                deltaTime
            );

        if (!timeResult.valid) {

            return timeResult;

        }

        const currentResult =
            this.validatePosition(
                currentPosition
            );

        if (!currentResult.valid) {

            return currentResult;

        }

        const nextResult =
            this.validatePosition(
                nextPosition
            );

        if (!nextResult.valid) {

            return nextResult;

        }

        const velocityResult =
            this.validateVelocity(
                velocity
            );

        if (!velocityResult.valid) {

            return velocityResult;

        }

        const dx =
            nextPosition.x -
            currentPosition.x;

        const dy =
            nextPosition.y -
            currentPosition.y;

        const dz =
            nextPosition.z -
            currentPosition.z;

        const distance =
            Math.sqrt(
                dx * dx +
                dy * dy +
                dz * dz
            );

        const expectedDistance =
            Math.sqrt(
                Number(velocity.x ?? 0) ** 2 +
                Number(velocity.y ?? 0) ** 2 +
                Number(velocity.z ?? 0) ** 2
            ) * deltaTime;

        if (
            Math.abs(
                distance -
                expectedDistance
            ) >
            this.positionTolerance
        ) {

            return {
                valid: false,
                reason: "movement_distance_mismatch",
                distance,
                expectedDistance
            };

        }

        return {

            valid: true,

            distance,

            expectedDistance,

            position: {
                ...nextPosition
            }

        };

    }

}

export class LevelManager {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.xpManager =
            options.xpManager ?? null;

        this.maxLevel =
            Number(options.maxLevel ?? 100);

        this.rewards = new Map();

        if (options.rewards) {

            this.loadRewards(
                options.rewards
            );

        }

    }

    initialize(xpManager) {

        this.xpManager = xpManager;

        return this;

    }

    setReward(level, reward) {

        level = Math.max(
            1,
            Math.floor(Number(level))
        );

        this.rewards.set(
            level,
            reward
        );

        return this;

    }

    loadRewards(rewards) {

        if (Array.isArray(rewards)) {

            for (const entry of rewards) {

                if (
                    entry &&
                    entry.level !== undefined
                ) {

                    this.setReward(
                        entry.level,
                        entry.reward
                    );

                }

            }

            return this;

        }

        if (
            rewards &&
            typeof rewards === "object"
        ) {

            for (const [level, reward] of
                Object.entries(rewards)
            ) {

                this.setReward(
                    Number(level),
                    reward
                );

            }

        }

        return this;

    }

    getLevel(totalXP) {

        if (!this.xpManager) {

            return 1;

        }

        return this.xpManager.getLevelFromXP(
            totalXP
        );

    }

    getReward(level) {

        return (
            this.rewards.get(
                Number(level)
            ) ?? null
        );

    }

    getRewardsBetween(
        previousLevel,
        currentLevel
    ) {

        const rewards = [];

        const start =
            Math.max(
                1,
                Number(previousLevel) + 1
            );

        const end =
            Math.min(
                this.maxLevel,
                Number(currentLevel)
            );

        for (
            let level = start;
            level <= end;
            level++
        ) {

            const reward =
                this.getReward(level);

            if (reward !== null) {

                rewards.push({

                    level,

                    reward

                });

            }

        }

        return rewards;

    }

    getAllRewards() {

        return [
            ...this.rewards.entries()
        ].map(
            ([level, reward]) => ({
                level,
                reward
            })
        );

    }

}

import { XPManager } from "./XPManager.js";
import { LevelManager } from "./LevelManager.js";
import { UnlockManager } from "./UnlockManager.js";

export class ProgressionManager {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.xpManager =
            options.xpManager ??
            new XPManager(options);

        this.levelManager =
            options.levelManager ??
            new LevelManager(options);

        this.unlockManager =
            options.unlockManager ??
            new UnlockManager(options);

        this.players = new Map();

    }

    initialize() {

        this.levelManager.initialize(
            this.xpManager
        );

        return this;

    }

    createPlayerProgression(
        playerId,
        data = {}
    ) {

        if (!playerId) {

            throw new TypeError(
                "Un identifiant joueur est requis."
            );

        }

        const progression = {

            playerId,

            xp:
                Math.max(
                    0,
                    Number(data.xp ?? 0)
                ),

            level:
                1,

            createdAt:
                Date.now(),

            updatedAt:
                Date.now()

        };

        progression.level =
            this.xpManager.getLevelFromXP(
                progression.xp
            );

        this.players.set(
            playerId,
            progression
        );

        return progression;

    }

    get(playerId) {

        return (
            this.players.get(playerId) ??
            this.createPlayerProgression(
                playerId
            )
        );

    }

    addXP(playerId, amount, context = {}) {

        const progression =
            this.get(playerId);

        const result =
            this.xpManager.addXP(
                progression.xp,
                amount
            );

        progression.xp =
            result.totalXP;

        progression.level =
            result.level;

        progression.updatedAt =
            Date.now();

        const rewards =
            this.levelManager.getRewardsBetween(
                result.previousLevel,
                result.level
            );

        const unlocked = [];

        for (const reward of rewards) {

            const unlockId =
                reward.reward?.unlock;

            if (!unlockId) {

                continue;

            }

            const unlockResult =
                this.unlockManager.tryUnlock(
                    playerId,
                    unlockId,
                    {

                        ...context,

                        level:
                            progression.level

                    }
                );

            if (unlockResult.success) {

                unlocked.push(
                    unlockResult
                );

            }

        }

        return {

            ...result,

            rewards,

            unlocked,

            progression: {
                ...progression
            }

        };

    }

    getProgress(playerId) {

        const progression =
            this.get(playerId);

        return this.xpManager.getProgress(
            progression.xp
        );

    }

    unlock(
        playerId,
        unlockId,
        context = {}
    ) {

        const progression =
            this.get(playerId);

        return this.unlockManager.tryUnlock(
            playerId,
            unlockId,
            {

                ...context,

                level:
                    progression.level

            }
        );

    }

    getUnlocks(playerId) {

        return this.unlockManager.getUnlocked(
            playerId
        );

    }

    removePlayer(playerId) {

        this.unlockManager.clearPlayer(
            playerId
        );

        return this.players.delete(
            playerId
        );

    }

    getAll() {

        return [
            ...this.players.values()
        ];

    }

    serializePlayer(playerId) {

        const progression =
            this.get(playerId);

        return {

            playerId:
                progression.playerId,

            xp:
                progression.xp,

            level:
                progression.level,

            progress:
                this.getProgress(
                    playerId
                ),

            unlocks:
                this.getUnlocks(
                    playerId
                ),

            updatedAt:
                progression.updatedAt

        };

    }

    clear() {

        this.players.clear();

        this.unlockManager.clear();

    }

}

export class UnlockManager {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.unlocks = new Map();

        this.playerUnlocks = new Map();

        if (options.unlocks) {

            this.load(
                options.unlocks
            );

        }

    }

    register(id, definition = {}) {

        if (!id) {

            throw new TypeError(
                "Un identifiant d'unlock est requis."
            );

        }

        this.unlocks.set(
            id,
            {

                id,

                ...definition

            }
        );

        return this.unlocks.get(id);

    }

    load(definitions) {

        if (Array.isArray(definitions)) {

            for (const definition of definitions) {

                if (definition?.id) {

                    this.register(
                        definition.id,
                        definition
                    );

                }

            }

        } else if (
            definitions &&
            typeof definitions === "object"
        ) {

            for (
                const [
                    id,
                    definition
                ] of Object.entries(definitions)
            ) {

                this.register(
                    id,
                    definition
                );

            }

        }

        return this;

    }

    get(id) {

        return (
            this.unlocks.get(id) ??
            null
        );

    }

    has(id) {

        return this.unlocks.has(id);

    }

    getPlayerUnlocks(playerId) {

        if (!this.playerUnlocks.has(playerId)) {

            this.playerUnlocks.set(
                playerId,
                new Set()
            );

        }

        return this.playerUnlocks.get(
            playerId
        );

    }

    isUnlocked(playerId, unlockId) {

        return this.getPlayerUnlocks(
            playerId
        ).has(unlockId);

    }

    unlock(playerId, unlockId) {

        if (!this.has(unlockId)) {

            return {

                success: false,

                reason:
                    "unlock_not_found"

            };

        }

        const unlocked =
            this.getPlayerUnlocks(
                playerId
            );

        if (unlocked.has(unlockId)) {

            return {

                success: true,

                alreadyUnlocked: true,

                unlock:
                    this.get(unlockId)

            };

        }

        unlocked.add(unlockId);

        return {

            success: true,

            alreadyUnlocked: false,

            unlock:
                this.get(unlockId)

        };

    }

    lock(playerId, unlockId) {

        const unlocked =
            this.getPlayerUnlocks(
                playerId
            );

        return unlocked.delete(
            unlockId
        );

    }

    checkRequirements(
        playerId,
        definition,
        context = {}
    ) {

        const requirements =
            definition?.requirements ?? [];

        for (const requirement of requirements) {

            if (
                requirement.type === "unlock"
            ) {

                if (
                    !this.isUnlocked(
                        playerId,
                        requirement.id
                    )
                ) {

                    return false;

                }

            } else if (
                requirement.type === "level"
            ) {

                if (
                    Number(context.level ?? 0) <
                    Number(requirement.value ?? 0)
                ) {

                    return false;

                }

            } else if (
                requirement.type === "item"
            ) {

                if (
                    typeof context.hasItem ===
                    "function" &&
                    !context.hasItem(
                        requirement.id,
                        requirement.amount ?? 1
                    )
                ) {

                    return false;

                }

            }

        }

        return true;

    }

    tryUnlock(
        playerId,
        unlockId,
        context = {}
    ) {

        const definition =
            this.get(unlockId);

        if (!definition) {

            return {

                success: false,

                reason:
                    "unlock_not_found"

            };

        }

        if (
            this.isUnlocked(
                playerId,
                unlockId
            )
        ) {

            return {

                success: true,

                alreadyUnlocked: true,

                unlock:
                    definition

            };

        }

        if (
            !this.checkRequirements(
                playerId,
                definition,
                context
            )
        ) {

            return {

                success: false,

                reason:
                    "requirements_not_met"

            };

        }

        return this.unlock(
            playerId,
            unlockId
        );

    }

    getUnlocked(playerId) {

        return [
            ...this.getPlayerUnlocks(
                playerId
            )
        ];

    }

    clearPlayer(playerId) {

        return this.playerUnlocks.delete(
            playerId
        );

    }

    clear() {

        this.unlocks.clear();

        this.playerUnlocks.clear();

    }

}

export class XPManager {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.baseXP =
            Number(options.baseXP ?? 100);

        this.growth =
            Number(options.growth ?? 1.15);

        this.maxLevel =
            Number(options.maxLevel ?? 100);

    }

    getRequiredXP(level) {

        level = Math.max(
            1,
            Math.floor(Number(level))
        );

        if (level >= this.maxLevel) {
            return 0;
        }

        return Math.floor(
            this.baseXP *
            Math.pow(
                this.growth,
                level - 1
            )
        );

    }

    getTotalXPForLevel(level) {

        level = Math.max(
            1,
            Math.floor(Number(level))
        );

        let total = 0;

        for (let i = 1; i < level; i++) {

            total += this.getRequiredXP(i);

        }

        return total;

    }

    getLevelFromXP(totalXP) {

        let xp = Math.max(
            0,
            Number(totalXP) || 0
        );

        let level = 1;

        while (
            level < this.maxLevel
        ) {

            const required =
                this.getRequiredXP(level);

            if (xp < required) {
                break;
            }

            xp -= required;

            level++;

        }

        return level;

    }

    getProgress(totalXP) {

        const level =
            this.getLevelFromXP(totalXP);

        if (level >= this.maxLevel) {

            return {
                level,
                currentXP: 0,
                requiredXP: 0,
                progress: 1,
                totalXP
            };

        }

        const levelStart =
            this.getTotalXPForLevel(level);

        const currentXP =
            Math.max(
                0,
                Number(totalXP) - levelStart
            );

        const requiredXP =
            this.getRequiredXP(level);

        return {

            level,

            currentXP,

            requiredXP,

            progress:
                requiredXP > 0
                    ? Math.min(
                        1,
                        currentXP / requiredXP
                    )
                    : 1,

            totalXP

        };

    }

    addXP(currentXP, amount) {

        const before =
            this.getLevelFromXP(
                currentXP
            );

        const totalXP =
            Math.max(
                0,
                Number(currentXP) || 0
            ) +
            Math.max(
                0,
                Number(amount) || 0
            );

        const after =
            this.getLevelFromXP(
                totalXP
            );

        return {

            totalXP,

            level:
                after,

            previousLevel:
                before,

            levelsGained:
                Math.max(
                    0,
                    after - before
                ),

            progress:
                this.getProgress(
                    totalXP
                )

        };

    }

}

import { LevelManager } from "./LevelManager.js";
import { UnlockManager } from "./UnlockManager.js";

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.xpManager =
            options.xpManager ??
            new XPManager(options);

        this.levelManager =
            options.levelManager ??
            new LevelManager(options);

        this.unlockManager =
            options.unlockManager ??
            new UnlockManager(options);

        this.players = new Map();

    }

    initialize() {

        this.levelManager.initialize(
            this.xpManager
        );

        return this;

    }

    createPlayerProgression(
        playerId,
        data = {}
    ) {

            throw new TypeError(
                "Un identifiant joueur est requis."
            );

        }

        const progression = {

            playerId,

            xp:
                Math.max(
                    0,
                    Number(data.xp ?? 0)
                ),

            level:
                1,

            createdAt:
                Date.now(),

            updatedAt:
                Date.now()

        };

        progression.level =
            this.xpManager.getLevelFromXP(
                progression.xp
            );

        this.players.set(
            playerId,
            progression
        );

        return progression;

    }

    get(playerId) {

        return (
            this.players.get(playerId) ??
            this.createPlayerProgression(
                playerId
            )
        );

    }

            this.get(playerId);

        const result =
            this.xpManager.addXP(
                progression.xp,
                amount
            );

        progression.xp =
            result.totalXP;

        progression.level =
            result.level;

        progression.updatedAt =
            Date.now();

        const rewards =
            this.levelManager.getRewardsBetween(
                result.previousLevel,
                result.level
            );

        const unlocked = [];

        for (const reward of rewards) {

            const unlockId =
                reward.reward?.unlock;

            if (!unlockId) {

                continue;

            }

            const unlockResult =
                this.unlockManager.tryUnlock(
                    playerId,
                    unlockId,
                    {

                        ...context,

                        level:
                            progression.level

                    }
                );

            if (unlockResult.success) {

                unlocked.push(
                    unlockResult
                );

            }

        }

        return {

            ...result,

            rewards,

            unlocked,

            progression: {
                ...progression
            }

        };

    }

    getProgress(playerId) {

        const progression =
            this.get(playerId);

        return this.xpManager.getProgress(
            progression.xp
        );

    }

    unlock(
        playerId,
        unlockId,
        context = {}
    ) {

        const progression =
            this.get(playerId);

        return this.unlockManager.tryUnlock(
            playerId,
            unlockId,
            {

                ...context,

                level:
                    progression.level

            }
        );

    }

    getUnlocks(playerId) {

        return this.unlockManager.getUnlocked(
            playerId
        );

    }

    removePlayer(playerId) {

        this.unlockManager.clearPlayer(
            playerId
        );

        return this.players.delete(
            playerId
        );

    }

    getAll() {

        return [
            ...this.players.values()
        ];

    }

    serializePlayer(playerId) {

        const progression =
            this.get(playerId);

        return {

            playerId:
                progression.playerId,

            xp:
                progression.xp,

            level:
                progression.level,

            progress:
                this.getProgress(
                    playerId
                ),

            unlocks:
                this.getUnlocks(
                    playerId
                ),

            updatedAt:
                progression.updatedAt

        };

    }

    clear() {

        this.players.clear();

        this.unlockManager.clear();

    }

}

export class DifficultyManager {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.difficulties = new Map();

        this.defaultDifficulty =
            options.defaultDifficulty ?? "easy";

        if (options.difficulties) {

            this.load(
                options.difficulties
            );

        }

    }

    register(id, definition = {}) {

        if (!id) {

            throw new TypeError(
                "Difficulty id is required."
            );

        }

        this.difficulties.set(
            id,
            {
                id,
                multiplier:
                    Number(
                        definition.multiplier ?? 1
                    ),
                timeLimit:
                    Number(
                        definition.timeLimit ?? 30
                    ),
                xpMultiplier:
                    Number(
                        definition.xpMultiplier ?? 1
                    ),
                ...definition
            }
        );

        return this;

    }

    load(definitions) {

        if (Array.isArray(definitions)) {

            for (const definition of definitions) {

                if (definition?.id) {

                    this.register(
                        definition.id,
                        definition
                    );

                }

            }

        } else if (
            definitions &&
            typeof definitions === "object"
        ) {

            for (
                const [id, definition]
                of Object.entries(definitions)
            ) {

                this.register(
                    id,
                    definition
                );

            }

        }

        return this;

    }

    get(id = this.defaultDifficulty) {

        return (
            this.difficulties.get(id) ??
            this.difficulties.get(
                this.defaultDifficulty
            ) ??
            null
        );

    }

    has(id) {

        return this.difficulties.has(id);

    }

    getIds() {

        return [
            ...this.difficulties.keys()
        ];

    }

    getXP(baseXP, difficulty) {

        const definition =
            this.get(difficulty);

        if (!definition) {

            return Math.max(
                0,
                Number(baseXP) || 0
            );

        }

        return Math.floor(
            Math.max(
                0,
                Number(baseXP) || 0
            ) *
            definition.xpMultiplier
        );

    }

    getTimeLimit(difficulty) {

        return this.get(
            difficulty
        )?.timeLimit ?? 30;

    }

}

export class QuestionSelector {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.random =
            options.random ??
            Math.random;

    }

    filter(questions, filters = {}) {

        if (!Array.isArray(questions)) {
            return [];
        }

        return questions.filter(
            question => {

                if (
                    filters.category &&
                    question.category !==
                        filters.category
                ) {

                    return false;

                }

                if (
                    filters.difficulty &&
                    question.difficulty !==
                        filters.difficulty
                ) {

                    return false;

                }

                if (
                    filters.type &&
                    question.type !==
                        filters.type
                ) {

                    return false;

                }

                if (
                    filters.tags?.length
                ) {

                    const tags =
                        Array.isArray(
                            question.tags
                        )
                            ? question.tags
                            : [];

                    if (
                        !filters.tags.some(
                            tag =>
                                tags.includes(tag)
                        )
                    ) {

                        return false;

                    }

                }

                return true;

            }
        );

    }

    shuffle(questions) {

        const result = [
            ...questions
        ];

        for (
            let i = result.length - 1;
            i > 0;
            i--
        ) {

            const j =
                Math.floor(
                    this.random() * (i + 1)
                );

            [
                result[i],
                result[j]
            ] = [
                result[j],
                result[i]
            ];

        }

        return result;

    }

    select(
        questions,
        count = 1,
        filters = {},
        excludeIds = []
    ) {

        const excluded =
            new Set(excludeIds);

        const filtered =
            this.filter(
                questions,
                filters
            ).filter(
                question =>
                    !excluded.has(
                        question.id
                    )
            );

        return this.shuffle(
            filtered
        ).slice(
            0,
            Math.max(
                0,
                Number(count)
            )
        );

    }

}
