import { AnswerValidator } from "./AnswerValidator.js";
import { DifficultyManager } from "./DifficultyManager.js";
import { QuestionSelector } from "./QuestionSelector.js";
import { QuizSession } from "./QuizSession.js";
import { QuizRewardManager } from "./QuizRewardManager.js";

export class QuizManager {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.questions = new Map();

        this.sessions = new Map();

        this.nextSessionId = 1;

        this.answerValidator =
            options.answerValidator ??
            new AnswerValidator(options);

        this.difficultyManager =
            options.difficultyManager ??
            new DifficultyManager(options);

        this.questionSelector =
            options.questionSelector ??
            new QuestionSelector(options);

        this.rewardManager =
            options.rewardManager ??
            new QuizRewardManager(options);

        if (Array.isArray(options.questions)) {

            this.loadQuestions(
                options.questions
            );

        }

    }

    initialize(services = {}) {

        this.rewardManager.initialize(
            services
        );

        return this;

    }

    loadQuestions(questions) {

        for (const question of questions) {

            if (question?.id) {

                this.questions.set(
                    question.id,
                    {
                        ...question
                    }
                );

            }

        }

        return this;

    }

    addQuestion(question) {

        if (
            !question ||
            !question.id
        ) {

            throw new TypeError(
                "A valid question with an id is required."
            );

        }

        this.questions.set(
            question.id,
            {
                ...question
            }
        );

        return question;

    }

    removeQuestion(id) {

        return this.questions.delete(id);

    }

    getQuestion(id) {

        return (
            this.questions.get(id) ??
            null
        );

    }

    getQuestions(filters = {}) {

        return this.questionSelector.filter(
            [
                ...this.questions.values()
            ],
            filters
        );

    }

    generateSessionId() {

        return `quiz_${this.nextSessionId++}`;

    }

    createSession(
        playerId,
        options = {}
    ) {

        const count =
            Math.max(
                1,
                Number(
                    options.questionCount ?? 10
                )
            );

        const questions =
            this.questionSelector.select(
                [
                    ...this.questions.values()
                ],
                count,
                {

                    category:
                        options.category,

                    difficulty:
                        options.difficulty,

                    type:
                        options.type,

                    tags:
                        options.tags

                }
            );

        const id =
            options.id ??
            this.generateSessionId();

        const session =
            new QuizSession({

                id,

                playerId,

                questions

            });

        this.sessions.set(
            id,
            session
        );

        return session;

    }

    getSession(id) {

        return (
            this.sessions.get(id) ??
            null
        );

    }

    startSession(id) {

        const session =
            this.getSession(id);

        if (!session) {

            return {

                success: false,

                reason:
                    "session_not_found"

            };

        }

        return {

            success:
                session.start(),

            session

        };

    }

    submitAnswer(
        sessionId,
        answer,
        options = {}
    ) {

        const session =
            this.getSession(
                sessionId
            );

        if (!session) {

            return {

                success: false,

                reason:
                    "session_not_found"

            };

        }

        const question =
            session.getCurrentQuestion();

        if (!question) {

            return {

                success: false,

                reason:
                    "no_current_question"

            };

        }

        let validation;

        if (
            options.index !== undefined ||
            question.type === "multiple_choice"
        ) {

            validation =
                this.answerValidator.validateIndex(
                    question,
                    options.index ??
                    answer
                );

        } else {

            validation =
                this.answerValidator.validate(
                    question,
                    answer
                );

        }

        const difficulty =
            this.difficultyManager.get(
                question.difficulty
            );

        const points =
            validation.correct
                ? Number(
                    question.points ??
                    1
                ) *
                  Number(
                    difficulty?.multiplier ?? 1
                  )
                : 0;

        const result =
            session.submitAnswer({

                answer,

                correct:
                    validation.correct,

                points

            });

        return {

            ...result,

            validation,

            questionId:
                question.id,

            difficulty:
                question.difficulty ?? null

        };

    }

    finishSession(
        sessionId,
        player = null,
        options = {}
    ) {

        const session =
            this.getSession(
                sessionId
            );

        if (!session) {

            return {

                success: false,

                reason:
                    "session_not_found"

            };

        }

        session.finish();

        let reward = null;

        if (player) {

            reward =
                this.rewardManager.grant(
                    player,
                    session,
                    options
                );

        }

        return {

            success: true,

            session:
                session.serialize(),

            reward

        };

    }

    deleteSession(id) {

        return this.sessions.delete(id);

    }

    getActiveSessions() {

        return [
            ...this.sessions.values()
        ].filter(
            session =>
                session.status === "active"
        );

    }

    getQuestionCount() {

        return this.questions.size;

    }

    clear() {

        this.questions.clear();

        this.sessions.clear();

    }

}

export class QuizRewardManager {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.progressionManager =
            options.progressionManager ?? null;

        this.rewardHistory = [];

        this.maxHistory =
            Number(
                options.maxHistory ?? 500
            );

    }

    initialize(services = {}) {

        if (
            services.progressionManager
        ) {

            this.progressionManager =
                services.progressionManager;

        }

        return this;

    }

    calculateReward(session, options = {}) {

        const baseXP =
            Number(
                options.baseXP ?? 10
            );

        const multiplier =
            Number(
                options.multiplier ?? 1
            );

        const score =
            Math.max(
                0,
                Number(session?.score ?? 0)
            );

        return {

            xp:
                Math.floor(
                    (
                        baseXP +
                        score
                    ) *
                    multiplier
                ),

            score,

            correctAnswers:
                Number(
                    session?.correctAnswers ?? 0
                ),

            wrongAnswers:
                Number(
                    session?.wrongAnswers ?? 0
                )

        };

    }

    grant(
        player,
        session,
        options = {}
    ) {

        if (!player) {

            return {

                success: false,

                reason:
                    "missing_player"

            };

        }

        const reward =
            this.calculateReward(
                session,
                options
            );

        let progressionResult =
            null;

        if (
            this.progressionManager &&
            player.id
        ) {

            progressionResult =
                this.progressionManager.addXP(
                    player.id,
                    reward.xp,
                    {
                        source: "quiz"
                    }
                );

        }

        const entry = {

            playerId:
                player.id ?? null,

            sessionId:
                session?.id ?? null,

            xp:
                reward.xp,

            score:
                reward.score,

            timestamp:
                Date.now()

        };

        this.rewardHistory.push(
            entry
        );

        while (
            this.rewardHistory.length >
            this.maxHistory
        ) {

            this.rewardHistory.shift();

        }

        return {

            success: true,

            reward,

            progression:
                progressionResult

        };

    }

    getHistory(playerId = null) {

        if (playerId === null) {

            return [
                ...this.rewardHistory
            ];

        }

        return this.rewardHistory.filter(
            entry =>
                entry.playerId === playerId
        );

    }

}

export class QuizSession {

    constructor(data = {}) {

        this.id =
            data.id ?? null;

        this.playerId =
            data.playerId ?? null;

        this.questions =
            Array.isArray(data.questions)
                ? [...data.questions]
                : [];

        this.currentIndex =
            Number(data.currentIndex ?? 0);

        this.score =
            Number(data.score ?? 0);

        this.correctAnswers =
            Number(
                data.correctAnswers ?? 0
            );

        this.wrongAnswers =
            Number(
                data.wrongAnswers ?? 0
            );

        this.startedAt =
            Number(
                data.startedAt ?? 0
            );

        this.finishedAt =
            Number(
                data.finishedAt ?? 0
            );

        this.status =
            data.status ?? "waiting";

        this.answers =
            Array.isArray(data.answers)
                ? [...data.answers]
                : [];

    }

    start(timestamp = Date.now()) {

        if (
            this.status !== "waiting"
        ) {

            return false;

        }

        this.status = "active";

        this.startedAt =
            timestamp;

        return true;

    }

    getCurrentQuestion() {

        if (
            this.currentIndex < 0 ||
            this.currentIndex >=
                this.questions.length
        ) {

            return null;

        }

        return this.questions[
            this.currentIndex
        ];

    }

    submitAnswer(result) {

        if (
            this.status !== "active"
        ) {

            return {
                accepted: false,
                reason: "session_not_active"
            };

        }

        const question =
            this.getCurrentQuestion();

        if (!question) {

            return {
                accepted: false,
                reason: "no_current_question"
            };

        }

        const answer = {

            questionId:
                question.id,

            answer:
                result.answer,

            correct:
                Boolean(result.correct),

            timestamp:
                Date.now()

        };

        this.answers.push(
            answer
        );

        if (answer.correct) {

            this.correctAnswers++;

            this.score +=
                Number(
                    result.points ?? 1
                );

        } else {

            this.wrongAnswers++;

        }

        this.currentIndex++;

        if (
            this.currentIndex >=
            this.questions.length
        ) {

            this.finish();

        }

        return {

            accepted: true,

            correct:
                answer.correct,

            score:
                this.score,

            finished:
                this.status === "finished"

        };

    }

    finish(timestamp = Date.now()) {

        if (
            this.status === "finished"
        ) {

            return false;

        }

        this.status = "finished";

        this.finishedAt =
            timestamp;

        return true;

    }

    getProgress() {

        const total =
            this.questions.length;

        return {

            current:
                Math.min(
                    this.currentIndex,
                    total
                ),

            total,

            progress:
                total > 0
                    ? Math.min(
                        1,
                        this.currentIndex / total
                    )
                    : 1

        };

    }

    serialize() {

        return {

            id: this.id,

            playerId:
                this.playerId,

            questions:
                this.questions,

            currentIndex:
                this.currentIndex,

            score:
                this.score,

            correctAnswers:
                this.correctAnswers,

            wrongAnswers:
                this.wrongAnswers,

            startedAt:
                this.startedAt,

            finishedAt:
                this.finishedAt,

            status:
                this.status,

            answers:
                [...this.answers],

            progress:
                this.getProgress()

        };

    }

}

export class LeaderboardManager {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.entries = new Map();

        this.maxEntries =
            Number(options.maxEntries ?? 100);

        this.sortDescending =
            options.sortDescending !== false;

    }

    setScore(playerId, score, metadata = {}) {

        if (!playerId) {
            return false;
        }

        const numericScore =
            Number(score);

        if (!Number.isFinite(numericScore)) {
            return false;
        }

        this.entries.set(playerId, {
            playerId,
            score: numericScore,
            metadata: { ...metadata },
            updatedAt: Date.now()
        });

        return true;
    }

    addScore(playerId, amount, metadata = {}) {

        const current =
            this.entries.get(playerId);

        const currentScore =
            current?.score ?? 0;

        return this.setScore(
            playerId,
            currentScore + Number(amount || 0),
            {
                ...(current?.metadata ?? {}),
                ...metadata
            }
        );
    }

    getSortedEntries() {

        const entries = [
            ...this.entries.values()
        ];

        entries.sort((a, b) =>
            this.sortDescending
                ? b.score - a.score
                : a.score - b.score
        );

        return entries;
    }

    getTop(limit = this.maxEntries) {

        return this.getSortedEntries()
            .slice(
                0,
                Math.max(0, Number(limit))
            )
            .map((entry, index) => ({
                rank: index + 1,
                ...entry
            }));

    }

    getPlayerRank(playerId) {

        const entries =
            this.getSortedEntries();

        const index =
            entries.findIndex(
                entry =>
                    entry.playerId === playerId
            );

        if (index === -1) {
            return null;
        }

        return index + 1;
    }

    getPlayer(playerId) {

        return (
            this.entries.get(playerId) ??
            null
        );

    }

    removePlayer(playerId) {

        return this.entries.delete(
            playerId
        );

    }

    clear() {

        this.entries.clear();

    }

    serialize() {

        return this.getSortedEntries()
            .slice(0, this.maxEntries)
            .map((entry, index) => ({
                rank: index + 1,
                ...entry
            }));

    }

}

export class ScoreManager {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.scores = new Map();

        this.multipliers = new Map();

        this.defaultMultiplier =
            Number(
                options.defaultMultiplier ?? 1
            );

    }

    getScore(playerId) {

        return (
            this.scores.get(playerId) ?? 0
        );

    }

    setScore(playerId, score) {

        const value =
            Math.max(
                0,
                Number(score) || 0
            );

        this.scores.set(
            playerId,
            value
        );

        return value;

    }

    addScore(playerId, amount, source = null) {

        const multiplier =
            this.getMultiplier(
                playerId,
                source
            );

        const value =
            Number(amount) *
            multiplier;

        const score =
            this.getScore(playerId) +
            Math.max(0, value);

        return this.setScore(
            playerId,
            score
        );

    }

    subtractScore(playerId, amount) {

        const score =
            Math.max(
                0,
                this.getScore(playerId) -
                Math.max(
                    0,
                    Number(amount) || 0
                )
            );

        return this.setScore(
            playerId,
            score
        );

    }

    setMultiplier(
        playerId,
        multiplier
    ) {

        const value =
            Math.max(
                0,
                Number(multiplier)
            );

        this.multipliers.set(
            playerId,
            value
        );

        return value;

    }

    getMultiplier(
        playerId,
        source = null
    ) {

        if (source) {

            const sourceMultiplier =
                this.multipliers.get(
                    `${playerId}:${source}`
                );

            if (
                sourceMultiplier !== undefined
            ) {

                return sourceMultiplier;

            }

        }

        return (
            this.multipliers.get(
                playerId
            ) ??
            this.defaultMultiplier
        );

    }

    reset(playerId) {

        this.scores.set(
            playerId,
            0
        );

        return 0;

    }

    remove(playerId) {

        this.multipliers.delete(
            playerId
        );

        return this.scores.delete(
            playerId
        );

    }

    getAll() {

        return [
            ...this.scores.entries()
        ].map(
            ([playerId, score]) => ({
                playerId,
                score
            })
        );

    }

    clear() {

        this.scores.clear();
        this.multipliers.clear();

    }

}

export class GameState {

    constructor(data = {}) {

        this.name = "game";

        this.status =
            data.status ?? "active";

        this.startedAt =
            Number(
                data.startedAt ?? Date.now()
            );

        this.updatedAt =
            Number(
                data.updatedAt ?? Date.now()
            );

        this.data = {
            ...data.data
        };

    }

    enter(context = {}) {

        this.status = "active";

        this.startedAt =
            Date.now();

        this.updatedAt =
            this.startedAt;

        Object.assign(
            this.data,
            context
        );

        return this;

    }

    update(context = {}) {

        Object.assign(
            this.data,
            context
        );

        this.updatedAt =
            Date.now();

        return this;

    }

    exit() {

        this.status = "inactive";

        this.updatedAt =
            Date.now();

        return this;

    }

    get(key, fallback = null) {

        return (
            this.data[key] ??
            fallback
        );

    }

    set(key, value) {

        this.data[key] = value;

        this.updatedAt =
            Date.now();

        return this;

    }

    serialize() {

        return {

            name: this.name,

            status: this.status,

            startedAt:
                this.startedAt,

            updatedAt:
                this.updatedAt,

            data: {
                ...this.data
            }

        };

    }

}

import { GameState } from "./GameState.js";

export class LobbyState extends GameState {

    constructor(data = {}) {

        super(data);

        this.name = "lobby";

        this.status = "waiting";

    }

    enter(context = {}) {

        super.enter(context);

        this.status = "waiting";

        this.data.readyPlayers =
            Array.isArray(
                context.readyPlayers
            )
                ? [...context.readyPlayers]
                : [];

        return this;

    }

    addReadyPlayer(playerId) {

        if (!playerId) {
            return false;
        }

        if (
            !Array.isArray(
                this.data.readyPlayers
            )
        ) {

            this.data.readyPlayers = [];

        }

        if (
            this.data.readyPlayers.includes(
                playerId
            )
        ) {

            return false;

        }

        this.data.readyPlayers.push(
            playerId
        );

        this.updatedAt =
            Date.now();

        return true;

    }

    removeReadyPlayer(playerId) {

        const players =
            this.data.readyPlayers ?? [];

        const index =
            players.indexOf(playerId);

        if (index === -1) {
            return false;
        }

        players.splice(
            index,
            1
        );

        this.updatedAt =
            Date.now();

        return true;

    }

    isReady(playerId) {

        return (
            this.data.readyPlayers ??
            []
        ).includes(playerId);

    }

}

import { GameState } from "./GameState.js";

export class RestartState extends GameState {

    constructor(data = {}) {

        super(data);

        this.name = "restart";

        this.status = "restarting";

        this.delay =
            Math.max(
                0,
                Number(data.delay ?? 5)
            );

        this.restartAt = null;

    }

    enter(context = {}) {

        super.enter(context);

        this.status = "restarting";

        this.restartAt =
            Date.now() +
            this.delay * 1000;

        this.data.reason =
            context.reason ??
            "game_restart";

        return this;

    }

    isReady(timestamp = Date.now()) {

        return (
            this.restartAt !== null &&
            timestamp >= this.restartAt
        );

    }

    getRemainingTime(
        timestamp = Date.now()
    ) {

        if (this.restartAt === null) {
            return this.delay;
        }

        return Math.max(
            0,
            (this.restartAt - timestamp) /
            1000
        );

    }

}

export class StateMachine {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.states = new Map();

        this.current = null;

        this.previous = null;

        this.context =
            options.context ?? {};

        this.history = [];

        this.maxHistory =
            Number(
                options.maxHistory ?? 50
            );

    }

    register(name, state) {

        if (!name || !state) {

            throw new TypeError(
                "A state name and state instance are required."
            );

        }

        this.states.set(
            name,
            state
        );

        return this;

    }

    unregister(name) {

        if (
            this.current?.name === name
        ) {

            return false;

        }

        return this.states.delete(name);

    }

    has(name) {

        return this.states.has(name);

    }

    get(name) {

        return (
            this.states.get(name) ??
            null
        );

    }

    getCurrent() {

        return this.current;

    }

    getPrevious() {

        return this.previous;

    }

    transition(name, context = {}) {

        const next =
            this.get(name);

        if (!next) {

            return {

                success: false,

                reason:
                    "state_not_found"

            };

        }

        if (
            this.current?.name === name
        ) {

            return {

                success: true,

                changed: false,

                state:
                    this.current

            };

        }

        const previous =
            this.current;

        if (
            previous &&
            typeof previous.exit ===
                "function"
        ) {

            previous.exit(
                this.context
            );

        }

        this.previous =
            previous;

        this.context = {
            ...this.context,
            ...context
        };

        this.current =
            next;

        if (
            typeof next.enter ===
                "function"
        ) {

            next.enter(
                this.context
            );

        }

        this.history.push({

            from:
                previous?.name ?? null,

            to:
                next.name,

            timestamp:
                Date.now()

        });

        while (
            this.history.length >
            this.maxHistory
        ) {

            this.history.shift();

        }

        return {

            success: true,

            changed: true,

            state:
                next

        };

    }

    update(context = {}) {

        this.context = {
            ...this.context,
            ...context
        };

        if (
            this.current &&
            typeof this.current.update ===
                "function"
        ) {

            this.current.update(
                this.context
            );

        }

        return this.current;

    }

    getHistory() {

        return [
            ...this.history
        ];

    }

    clearHistory() {

        this.history.length = 0;

    }

}

export class MatchEndManager {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.victoryManager =
            options.victoryManager ?? null;

        this.rewardManager =
            options.rewardManager ?? null;

        this.progressionManager =
            options.progressionManager ?? null;

        this.ended = false;

        this.result = null;

        this.callbacks = new Set();

    }

    initialize(services = {}) {

        if (services.victoryManager) {

            this.victoryManager =
                services.victoryManager;

        }

        if (services.rewardManager) {

            this.rewardManager =
                services.rewardManager;

        }

        if (services.progressionManager) {

            this.progressionManager =
                services.progressionManager;

        }

        return this;

    }

    onMatchEnd(callback) {

        if (
            typeof callback !==
            "function"
        ) {

            throw new TypeError(
                "Match end callback must be a function."
            );

        }

        this.callbacks.add(callback);

        return () =>
            this.callbacks.delete(callback);

    }

    endMatch(data = {}) {

        if (this.ended) {

            return {

                success: false,

                reason:
                    "match_already_ended",

                result:
                    this.getResult()

            };

        }

        const winnerId =
            data.winnerId ??
            this.victoryManager?.getWinnerId() ??
            null;

        const result = {

            winnerId,

            reason:
                data.reason ??
                "match_completed",

            conditionId:
                data.conditionId ??
                null,

            players:
                Array.isArray(data.players)
                    ? [...data.players]
                    : [],

            rewards:
                data.rewards
                    ? {
                        ...data.rewards
                    }
                    : {},

            metadata:
                data.metadata
                    ? {
                        ...data.metadata
                    }
                    : {},

            timestamp:
                Date.now()

        };

        this.result =
            result;

        this.ended = true;

        for (
            const callback
            of this.callbacks
        ) {

            try {

                callback(
                    this.getResult()
                );

            } catch {

                // A callback must not
                // interrupt match finalization.

            }

        }

        return {

            success: true,

            result:
                this.getResult()

        };

    }

    awardWinner(
        playerId,
        reward = {}
    ) {

        if (!playerId) {

            return {

                success: false,

                reason:
                    "missing_player"

            };

        }

        if (
            this.progressionManager
        ) {

            const xp =
                Number(
                    reward.xp ?? 0
                );

            if (xp > 0) {

                return {

                    success: true,

                    progression:
                        this.progressionManager.addXP(
                            playerId,
                            xp,
                            {
                                source:
                                    "match_victory"
                            }
                        )

                };

            }

        }

        return {

            success: true,

            reward: {
                ...reward
            }

        };

    }

    isEnded() {

        return this.ended;

    }

    getResult() {

        if (!this.result) {

            return null;

        }

        return {

            ...this.result,

            players: [
                ...this.result.players
            ],

            rewards: {
                ...this.result.rewards
            },

            metadata: {
                ...this.result.metadata
            }

        };

    }

    reset() {

        this.ended = false;

        this.result = null;

        return this;

    }

    serialize() {

        return {

            ended:
                this.ended,

            result:
                this.getResult()

        };

    }

}

export class VictoryCondition {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.id =
            options.id ?? null;

        this.name =
            options.name ??
            this.id ??
            "victory_condition";

        this.description =
            options.description ?? "";

        this.priority =
            Number(options.priority ?? 0);

        this.enabled =
            options.enabled !== false;

        this.check =
            typeof options.check === "function"
                ? options.check
                : () => false;

    }

    isEnabled() {

        return this.enabled;

    }

    enable() {

        this.enabled = true;

        return this;

    }

    disable() {

        this.enabled = false;

        return this;

    }

    evaluate(context = {}) {

        if (!this.enabled) {

            return {

                fulfilled: false,

                conditionId: this.id,

                reason: "disabled"

            };

        }

        try {

            const result =
                this.check(context);

            return {

                fulfilled:
                    Boolean(result),

                conditionId:
                    this.id,

                priority:
                    this.priority

            };

        } catch (error) {

            return {

                fulfilled: false,

                conditionId: this.id,

                priority:
                    this.priority,

                reason:
                    "condition_error",

                error

            };

        }

    }

    serialize() {

        return {

            id:
                this.id,

            name:
                this.name,

            description:
                this.description,

            priority:
                this.priority,

            enabled:
                this.enabled

        };

    }

}

import { VictoryCondition } from "./VictoryCondition.js";

export class VictoryManager {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.conditions = new Map();

        this.victory = null;

        this.ended = false;

        this.history = [];

        this.maxHistory =
            Number(options.maxHistory ?? 50);

        if (Array.isArray(options.conditions)) {

            for (
                const condition
                of options.conditions
            ) {

                this.registerCondition(
                    condition
                );

            }

        }

    }

    registerCondition(condition) {

        const instance =
            condition instanceof VictoryCondition
                ? condition
                : new VictoryCondition(
                    condition
                );

        if (!instance.id) {

            throw new TypeError(
                "Victory condition requires an id."
            );

        }

        this.conditions.set(
            instance.id,
            instance
        );

        return instance;

    }

    unregisterCondition(id) {

        return this.conditions.delete(id);

    }

    getCondition(id) {

        return (
            this.conditions.get(id) ??
            null
        );

    }

    getConditions() {

        return [
            ...this.conditions.values()
        ];

    }

    evaluate(context = {}) {

        if (this.ended) {

            return {

                won: true,

                victory:
                    this.getVictory(),

                reason:
                    "victory_already_declared"

            };

        }

        const results = [
            ...this.conditions.values()
        ]
            .map(
                condition =>
                    condition.evaluate(
                        context
                    )
            )
            .filter(
                result =>
                    result.fulfilled
            )
            .sort(
                (a, b) =>
                    b.priority - a.priority
            );

        if (results.length === 0) {

            return {

                won: false,

                victory: null,

                results: []

            };

        }

        const result =
            results[0];

        return {

            won: true,

            conditionId:
                result.conditionId,

            results

        };

    }

    declareVictory(
        winnerId,
        context = {}
    ) {

        if (this.ended) {

            return {

                success: false,

                reason:
                    "victory_already_declared",

                victory:
                    this.getVictory()

            };

        }

        if (!winnerId) {

            return {

                success: false,

                reason:
                    "missing_winner"

            };

        }

        const victory = {

            winnerId,

            conditionId:
                context.conditionId ?? null,

            reason:
                context.reason ??
                "victory",

            metadata:
                context.metadata
                    ? {
                        ...context.metadata
                    }
                    : {},

            timestamp:
                Date.now()

        };

        this.victory =
            victory;

        this.ended = true;

        this.history.push(
            victory
        );

        while (
            this.history.length >
            this.maxHistory
        ) {

            this.history.shift();

        }

        return {

            success: true,

            victory:
                this.getVictory()

        };

    }

    evaluateAndDeclare(
        winnerResolver,
        context = {}
    ) {

        if (
            typeof winnerResolver !==
            "function"
        ) {

            return {

                success: false,

                reason:
                    "invalid_winner_resolver"

            };

        }

        const evaluation =
            this.evaluate(
                context
            );

        if (!evaluation.won) {

            return {

                success: false,

                won: false,

                evaluation

            };

        }

        const winnerId =
            winnerResolver(
                evaluation,
                context
            );

        if (!winnerId) {

            return {

                success: false,

                won: true,

                reason:
                    "winner_not_resolved",

                evaluation

            };

        }

        return this.declareVictory(
            winnerId,
            {

                ...context,

                conditionId:
                    evaluation.conditionId

            }
        );

    }

    hasEnded() {

        return this.ended;

    }

    getWinnerId() {

        return (
            this.victory?.winnerId ??
            null
        );

    }

    getVictory() {

        if (!this.victory) {

            return null;

        }

        return {

            ...this.victory,

            metadata: {
                ...this.victory.metadata
            }

        };

    }

    reset() {

        this.victory = null;

        this.ended = false;

        return this;

    }

    getHistory() {

        return this.history.map(
            victory => ({
                ...victory,

                metadata: {
                    ...victory.metadata
                }

            })
        );

    }

    serialize() {

        return {

            ended:
                this.ended,

            victory:
                this.getVictory(),

            conditions:
                this.getConditions()
                    .map(
                        condition =>
                            condition.serialize()
                    ),

            history:
                this.getHistory()

        };

    }

}

export class Lobby {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.id =
            options.id ?? "main";

        this.name =
            options.name ?? "Main Lobby";

        this.maxRooms =
            Math.max(
                1,
                Number(options.maxRooms ?? 100)
            );

        this.rooms = new Map();

        this.players = new Map();

        this.status =
            options.status ?? "active";

        this.createdAt =
            Number(
                options.createdAt ?? Date.now()
            );

    }

    addRoom(room) {

        if (!room?.id) {

            return {
                success: false,
                reason: "invalid_room"
            };

        }

        if (this.rooms.has(room.id)) {

            return {
                success: false,
                reason: "room_already_exists"
            };

        }

        if (
            this.rooms.size >=
            this.maxRooms
        ) {

            return {
                success: false,
                reason: "lobby_full"
            };

        }

        this.rooms.set(
            room.id,
            room
        );

        return {

            success: true,

            roomId:
                room.id

        };

    }

    removeRoom(roomId) {

        const room =
            this.rooms.get(
                roomId
            );

        if (!room) {

            return {
                success: false,
                reason: "room_not_found"
            };

        }

        for (
            const playerId
            of room.getPlayerIds()
        ) {

            this.players.delete(
                playerId
            );

        }

        this.rooms.delete(
            roomId
        );

        return {

            success: true,

            roomId

        };

    }

    getRoom(roomId) {

        return (
            this.rooms.get(
                roomId
            ) ?? null
        );

    }

    getRooms() {

        return [
            ...this.rooms.values()
        ];

    }

    joinRoom(player, roomId) {

        const playerId =
            typeof player === "object"
                ? player?.id
                : player;

        if (!playerId) {

            return {
                success: false,
                reason: "invalid_player"
            };

        }

        const room =
            this.getRoom(roomId);

        if (!room) {

            return {
                success: false,
                reason: "room_not_found"
            };

        }

        const currentRoomId =
            this.players.get(
                playerId
            );

        if (
            currentRoomId &&
            currentRoomId !== roomId
        ) {

            return {
                success: false,
                reason: "player_already_in_room"
            };

        }

        const result =
            room.addPlayer(
                player
            );

        if (!result.success) {

            return result;

        }

        this.players.set(
            playerId,
            roomId
        );

        return {

            success: true,

            roomId,

            playerId

        };

    }

    leaveRoom(playerId) {

        const roomId =
            this.players.get(
                playerId
            );

        if (!roomId) {

            return {
                success: false,
                reason: "player_not_in_room"
            };

        }

        const room =
            this.getRoom(roomId);

        if (room) {

            room.removePlayer(
                playerId
            );

        }

        this.players.delete(
            playerId
        );

        return {

            success: true,

            playerId,

            roomId

        };

    }

    getPlayerRoom(playerId) {

        const roomId =
            this.players.get(
                playerId
            );

        if (!roomId) {

            return null;

        }

        return this.getRoom(
            roomId
        );

    }

    getAvailableRooms() {

        return this.getRooms()
            .filter(
                room =>
                    room.isJoinable()
            );

    }

    getPlayerCount() {

        return this.players.size;

    }

    getRoomCount() {

        return this.rooms.size;

    }

    clear() {

        for (
            const room
            of this.rooms.values()
        ) {

            for (
                const playerId
                of room.getPlayerIds()
            ) {

                this.players.delete(
                    playerId
                );

            }

        }

        this.rooms.clear();

    }

    serialize() {

        return {

            id:
                this.id,

            name:
                this.name,

            status:
                this.status,

            roomCount:
                this.getRoomCount(),

            playerCount:
                this.getPlayerCount(),

            rooms:
                this.getRooms().map(
                    room =>
                        room.serialize()
                ),

            createdAt:
                this.createdAt

        };

    }

}

import { Lobby } from "./Lobby.js";
import { RoomManager } from "./RoomManager.js";

export class LobbyManager {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.lobbies = new Map();

        this.roomManagers = new Map();

        this.defaultLobbyId =
            options.defaultLobbyId ??
            "main";

        this.defaultRoomOptions =
            {
                ...(options.defaultRoomOptions ?? {})
            };

        if (options.lobbies) {

            this.load(
                options.lobbies
            );

        }

    }

    createLobby(options = {}) {

        const lobby =
            options instanceof Lobby
                ? options
                : new Lobby(options);

        if (!lobby.id) {

            return {

                success: false,

                reason:
                    "invalid_lobby_id"

            };

        }

        if (
            this.lobbies.has(
                lobby.id
            )
        ) {

            return {

                success: false,

                reason:
                    "lobby_already_exists"

            };

        }

        this.lobbies.set(
            lobby.id,
            lobby
        );

        this.roomManagers.set(
            lobby.id,
            new RoomManager(
                this.defaultRoomOptions
            )
        );

        return {

            success: true,

            lobby

        };

    }

    getLobby(lobbyId = this.defaultLobbyId) {

        return (
            this.lobbies.get(
                lobbyId
            ) ?? null
        );

    }

    getRoomManager(
        lobbyId = this.defaultLobbyId
    ) {

        return (
            this.roomManagers.get(
                lobbyId
            ) ?? null
        );

    }

    ensureLobby(
        lobbyId = this.defaultLobbyId,
        options = {}
    ) {

        const existing =
            this.getLobby(
                lobbyId
            );

        if (existing) {

            return existing;

        }

        const result =
            this.createLobby({

                ...options,

                id:
                    lobbyId

            });

        return result.success
            ? result.lobby
            : null;

    }

    join(
        player,
        lobbyId = this.defaultLobbyId,
        roomId = null,
        roomOptions = {}
    ) {

        const lobby =
            this.ensureLobby(
                lobbyId
            );

        if (!lobby) {

            return {

                success: false,

                reason:
                    "lobby_not_found"

            };

        }

        const roomManager =
            this.getRoomManager(
                lobbyId
            );

        if (!roomManager) {

            return {

                success: false,

                reason:
                    "room_manager_not_found"

            };

        }

        let room =
            roomId
                ? roomManager.get(
                    roomId
                )
                : null;

        if (!room) {

            const result =
                roomManager.findOrCreate(
                    roomOptions
                );

            if (!result.success) {

                return result;

            }

            room =
                result.room;

            if (
                !lobby.getRoom(
                    room.id
                )
            ) {

                const addResult =
                    lobby.addRoom(
                        room
                    );

                if (!addResult.success) {

                    roomManager.remove(
                        room.id
                    );

                    return addResult;

                }

            }

        }

        return lobby.joinRoom(
            player,
            room.id
        );

    }

    leave(
        playerId,
        lobbyId = this.defaultLobbyId
    ) {

        const lobby =
            this.getLobby(
                lobbyId
            );

        if (!lobby) {

            return {

                success: false,

                reason:
                    "lobby_not_found"

            };

        }

        return lobby.leaveRoom(
            playerId
        );

    }

    removeLobby(lobbyId) {

        const lobby =
            this.lobbies.get(
                lobbyId
            );

        if (!lobby) {

            return false;

        }

        lobby.clear();

        this.lobbies.delete(
            lobbyId
        );

        this.roomManagers.delete(
            lobbyId
        );

        return true;

    }

    getAllLobbies() {

        return [
            ...this.lobbies.values()
        ];

    }

    getLobbyCount() {

        return this.lobbies.size;

    }

    load(definitions) {

        if (!Array.isArray(definitions)) {

            return this;

        }

        for (
            const definition
            of definitions
        ) {

            if (
                definition?.id
            ) {

                this.createLobby(
                    definition
                );

            }

        }

        return this;

    }

    clear() {

        for (
            const lobby
            of this.lobbies.values()
        ) {

            lobby.clear();

        }

        for (
            const manager
            of this.roomManagers.values()
        ) {

            manager.clear();

        }

        this.lobbies.clear();

        this.roomManagers.clear();

    }

    serialize() {

        return this.getAllLobbies()
            .map(
                lobby =>
                    lobby.serialize()
            );

    }

}

export class Room {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.id =
            options.id ?? null;

        this.name =
            options.name ??
            this.id ??
            "room";

        this.maxPlayers =
            Math.max(
                1,
                Number(options.maxPlayers ?? 16)
            );

        this.players = new Map();

        this.status =
            options.status ?? "waiting";

        this.createdAt =
            Number(
                options.createdAt ?? Date.now()
            );

        this.updatedAt =
            Number(
                options.updatedAt ?? this.createdAt
            );

        this.metadata = {
            ...(options.metadata ?? {})
        };

    }

    addPlayer(player, metadata = {}) {

        const playerId =
            typeof player === "object"
                ? player?.id
                : player;

        if (!playerId) {

            return {
                success: false,
                reason: "invalid_player"
            };

        }

        if (this.players.has(playerId)) {

            return {
                success: false,
                reason: "player_already_in_room"
            };

        }

        if (
            this.players.size >=
            this.maxPlayers
        ) {

            return {
                success: false,
                reason: "room_full"
            };

        }

        this.players.set(
            playerId,
            {

                player:
                    typeof player === "object"
                        ? player
                        : null,

                metadata: {
                    ...metadata
                },

                joinedAt:
                    Date.now()

            }
        );

        this.updatedAt =
            Date.now();

        return {

            success: true,

            playerId,

            roomId:
                this.id

        };

    }

    removePlayer(playerId) {

        if (!this.players.has(playerId)) {

            return {
                success: false,
                reason: "player_not_in_room"
            };

        }

        this.players.delete(
            playerId
        );

        this.updatedAt =
            Date.now();

        return {

            success: true,

            playerId,

            roomId:
                this.id

        };

    }

    hasPlayer(playerId) {

        return this.players.has(
            playerId
        );

    }

    getPlayer(playerId) {

        return (
            this.players.get(
                playerId
            ) ?? null
        );

    }

    getPlayers() {

        return [
            ...this.players.entries()
        ].map(
            ([playerId, data]) => ({

                playerId,

                ...data

            })
        );

    }

    getPlayerIds() {

        return [
            ...this.players.keys()
        ];

    }

    getPlayerCount() {

        return this.players.size;

    }

    isFull() {

        return (
            this.players.size >=
            this.maxPlayers
        );

    }

    isEmpty() {

        return this.players.size === 0;

    }

    setStatus(status) {

        this.status =
            String(status);

        this.updatedAt =
            Date.now();

        return this;

    }

    isJoinable() {

        return (
            this.status === "waiting" &&
            !this.isFull()
        );

    }

    updateMetadata(metadata = {}) {

        this.metadata = {

            ...this.metadata,

            ...metadata

        };

        this.updatedAt =
            Date.now();

        return this;

    }

    serialize() {

        return {

            id:
                this.id,

            name:
                this.name,

            maxPlayers:
                this.maxPlayers,

            playerCount:
                this.getPlayerCount(),

            players:
                this.getPlayerIds(),

            status:
                this.status,

            createdAt:
                this.createdAt,

            updatedAt:
                this.updatedAt,

            metadata: {
                ...this.metadata
            }

        };

    }

}

import { Room } from "./Room.js";

export class RoomManager {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.rooms = new Map();

        this.defaultMaxPlayers =
            Math.max(
                1,
                Number(
                    options.defaultMaxPlayers ?? 16
                )
            );

        this.nextRoomNumber = 1;

    }

    generateId() {

        let id;

        do {

            id =
                `room_${this.nextRoomNumber++}`;

        } while (
            this.rooms.has(id)
        );

        return id;

    }

    create(options = {}) {

        const room =
            options instanceof Room
                ? options
                : new Room({

                    ...options,

                    id:
                        options.id ??
                        this.generateId(),

                    maxPlayers:
                        options.maxPlayers ??
                        this.defaultMaxPlayers

                });

        if (
            !room.id ||
            this.rooms.has(room.id)
        ) {

            return {

                success: false,

                reason:
                    "room_already_exists"

            };

        }

        this.rooms.set(
            room.id,
            room
        );

        return {

            success: true,

            room

        };

    }

    get(roomId) {

        return (
            this.rooms.get(
                roomId
            ) ?? null
        );

    }

    has(roomId) {

        return this.rooms.has(
            roomId
        );

    }

    remove(roomId) {

        return this.rooms.delete(
            roomId
        );

    }

    findJoinable(filters = {}) {

        const rooms =
            [
                ...this.rooms.values()
            ].filter(
                room =>
                    room.isJoinable()
            );

        if (filters.name) {

            return rooms.find(
                room =>
                    room.name ===
                    filters.name
            ) ?? null;

        }

        if (filters.metadata) {

            return rooms.find(
                room => {

                    return Object.entries(
                        filters.metadata
                    ).every(
                        ([key, value]) =>
                            room.metadata[key] ===
                            value
                    );

                }
            ) ?? null;

        }

        return rooms[0] ?? null;

    }

    findOrCreate(options = {}) {

        const existing =
            this.findJoinable(
                options
            );

        if (existing) {

            return {

                success: true,

                created: false,

                room:
                    existing

            };

        }

        return {

            ...this.create(
                options
            ),

            created: true

        };

    }

    getAll() {

        return [
            ...this.rooms.values()
        ];

    }

    getJoinable() {

        return this.getAll()
            .filter(
                room =>
                    room.isJoinable()
            );

    }

    getCount() {

        return this.rooms.size;

    }

    clearEmpty() {

        let removed = 0;

        for (
            const [id, room]
            of this.rooms
        ) {

            if (room.isEmpty()) {

                this.rooms.delete(id);

                removed++;

            }

        }

        return removed;

    }

    clear() {

        this.rooms.clear();

    }

    serialize() {

        return this.getAll().map(
            room =>
                room.serialize()
        );

    }

}

export class ConnectionManager {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.connections = new Map();

        this.maxConnections =
            Number(options.maxConnections ?? 1000);

        this.createdAt =
            Date.now();

    }

    add(connection) {

        if (!connection?.id) {

            return {
                success: false,
                reason: "invalid_connection"
            };

        }

        if (this.connections.has(connection.id)) {

            return {
                success: false,
                reason: "connection_exists"
            };

        }

        if (
            this.connections.size >=
            this.maxConnections
        ) {

            return {
                success: false,
                reason: "connection_limit_reached"
            };

        }

        this.connections.set(
            connection.id,
            {
                connection,
                connectedAt: Date.now()
            }
        );

        return {
            success: true,
            connectionId: connection.id
        };

    }

    remove(connectionId) {

        return this.connections.delete(
            connectionId
        );

    }

    get(connectionId) {

        return (
            this.connections.get(
                connectionId
            )?.connection ?? null
        );

    }

    has(connectionId) {

        return this.connections.has(
            connectionId
        );

    }

    count() {

        return this.connections.size;

    }

    getAll() {

        return [
            ...this.connections.values()
        ].map(
            entry => entry.connection
        );

    }

    broadcast(event, payload) {

        let sent = 0;

        for (
            const entry
            of this.connections.values()
        ) {

            const connection =
                entry.connection;

            if (
                typeof connection.send ===
                "function"
            ) {

                try {

                    connection.send(
                        event,
                        payload
                    );

                    sent++;

                } catch {
                    // Individual connection failures
                    // must not stop the broadcast.
                }

            }

        }

        return sent;

    }

    clear() {

        for (
            const entry
            of this.connections.values()
        ) {

            try {

                entry.connection.close?.();

            } catch {
                // Ignore connection cleanup errors.
            }

        }

        this.connections.clear();

    }

}

export class EventRouter {

    constructor() {

        this.handlers = new Map();

    }

    on(event, handler) {

        if (
            !event ||
            typeof handler !== "function"
        ) {

            throw new TypeError(
                "Event and handler are required."
            );

        }

        if (!this.handlers.has(event)) {

            this.handlers.set(
                event,
                new Set()
            );

        }

        this.handlers
            .get(event)
            .add(handler);

        return () =>
            this.off(
                event,
                handler
            );

    }

    off(event, handler) {

        const handlers =
            this.handlers.get(event);

        if (!handlers) {

            return false;

        }

        const removed =
            handlers.delete(handler);

        if (handlers.size === 0) {

            this.handlers.delete(event);

        }

        return removed;

    }

    once(event, handler) {

        const unsubscribe =
            this.on(
                event,
                (...args) => {

                    unsubscribe();

                    return handler(
                        ...args
                    );

                }
            );

        return unsubscribe;

    }

    async emit(event, ...args) {

        const handlers =
            this.handlers.get(event);

        if (!handlers) {

            return [];

        }

        const results = [];

        for (
            const handler
            of [...handlers]
        ) {

            results.push(
                await handler(
                    ...args
                )
            );

        }

        return results;

    }

    has(event) {

        return this.handlers.has(event);

    }

    clear(event = null) {

        if (event === null) {

            this.handlers.clear();

            return;

        }

        this.handlers.delete(event);

    }

}

export class InputReceiver {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.router =
            options.router ?? null;

        this.validators =
            new Map();

        this.inputs =
            new Map();

    }

    setRouter(router) {

        this.router = router;

        return this;

    }

    registerValidator(
        inputType,
        validator
    ) {

        if (
            !inputType ||
            typeof validator !== "function"
        ) {

            throw new TypeError(
                "Input type and validator are required."
            );

        }

        this.validators.set(
            inputType,
            validator
        );

        return this;

    }

    receive(
        playerId,
        input
    ) {

        if (!playerId) {

            return {
                accepted: false,
                reason: "missing_player"
            };

        }

        if (!input || typeof input !== "object") {

            return {
                accepted: false,
                reason: "invalid_input"
            };

        }

        const type =
            input.type ?? "unknown";

        const validator =
            this.validators.get(type);

        if (validator) {

            let valid = false;

            try {

                valid =
                    Boolean(
                        validator(
                            input,
                            playerId
                        )
                    );

            } catch {

                valid = false;

            }

            if (!valid) {

                return {
                    accepted: false,
                    reason: "input_rejected"
                };

            }

        }

        const record = {

            ...input,

            playerId,

            receivedAt:
                Date.now()

        };

        if (!this.inputs.has(playerId)) {

            this.inputs.set(
                playerId,
                []
            );

        }

        const history =
            this.inputs.get(playerId);

        history.push(record);

        if (history.length > 100) {

            history.shift();

        }

        if (
            this.router &&
            typeof this.router.emit ===
                "function"
        ) {

            this.router.emit(
                "input",
                record
            );

        }

        return {

            accepted: true,

            input: record

        };

    }

    getLatest(playerId) {

        const history =
            this.inputs.get(playerId);

        return (
            history?.[history.length - 1] ??
            null
        );

    }

    getHistory(playerId) {

        return [
            ...(this.inputs.get(playerId) ?? [])
        ];

    }

    clear(playerId = null) {

        if (playerId === null) {

            this.inputs.clear();

            return;

        }

        this.inputs.delete(
            playerId
        );

    }

}

export class NetworkMetrics {

    constructor() {

        this.startedAt =
            Date.now();

        this.receivedPackets = 0;

        this.sentPackets = 0;

        this.rejectedPackets = 0;

        this.errors = 0;

        this.bytesReceived = 0;

        this.bytesSent = 0;

        this.latencies = [];

    }

    recordReceived(bytes = 0) {

        this.receivedPackets++;

        this.bytesReceived +=
            Math.max(
                0,
                Number(bytes) || 0
            );

    }

    recordSent(bytes = 0) {

        this.sentPackets++;

        this.bytesSent +=
            Math.max(
                0,
                Number(bytes) || 0
            );

    }

    recordRejected() {

        this.rejectedPackets++;

    }

    recordError() {

        this.errors++;

    }

    recordLatency(milliseconds) {

        const value =
            Number(milliseconds);

        if (!Number.isFinite(value)) {

            return;

        }

        this.latencies.push(
            Math.max(0, value)
        );

        if (
            this.latencies.length > 1000
        ) {

            this.latencies.shift();

        }

    }

    getAverageLatency() {

        if (this.latencies.length === 0) {

            return 0;

        }

        const total =
            this.latencies.reduce(
                (sum, value) =>
                    sum + value,
                0
            );

        return total /
            this.latencies.length;

    }

    getUptime() {

        return Date.now() -
            this.startedAt;

    }

    snapshot() {

        return {

            uptime:
                this.getUptime(),

            receivedPackets:
                this.receivedPackets,

            sentPackets:
                this.sentPackets,

            rejectedPackets:
                this.rejectedPackets,

            errors:
                this.errors,

            bytesReceived:
                this.bytesReceived,

            bytesSent:
                this.bytesSent,

            averageLatency:
                this.getAverageLatency()

        };

    }

    reset() {

        this.startedAt =
            Date.now();

        this.receivedPackets = 0;

        this.sentPackets = 0;

        this.rejectedPackets = 0;

        this.errors = 0;

        this.bytesReceived = 0;

        this.bytesSent = 0;

        this.latencies.length = 0;

    }

}

export class PacketValidator {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.maxPayloadSize =
            Number(options.maxPayloadSize ?? 64 * 1024);

        this.allowedTypes =
            new Set(
                options.allowedTypes ?? []
            );

        this.requireType =
            options.requireType !== false;

    }

    validate(packet) {

        if (
            packet === null ||
            packet === undefined
        ) {

            return {
                valid: false,
                reason: "empty_packet"
            };

        }

        if (
            typeof packet !== "object" &&
            typeof packet !== "string"
        ) {

            return {
                valid: false,
                reason: "invalid_packet_type"
            };

        }

        let payload;

        try {

            payload =
                typeof packet === "string"
                    ? JSON.parse(packet)
                    : packet;

        } catch {

            return {
                valid: false,
                reason: "invalid_json"
            };

        }

        if (
            payload === null ||
            typeof payload !== "object"
        ) {

            return {
                valid: false,
                reason: "invalid_payload"
            };

        }

        if (
            this.requireType &&
            typeof payload.type !== "string"
        ) {

            return {
                valid: false,
                reason: "missing_packet_type"
            };

        }

        if (
            this.allowedTypes.size > 0 &&
            !this.allowedTypes.has(payload.type)
        ) {

            return {
                valid: false,
                reason: "packet_type_not_allowed"
            };

        }

        const serialized =
            JSON.stringify(payload);

        if (
            serialized.length >
            this.maxPayloadSize
        ) {

            return {
                valid: false,
                reason: "payload_too_large"
            };

        }

        return {

            valid: true,

            packet: payload,

            size:
                serialized.length

        };

    }

    addAllowedType(type) {

        if (type) {

            this.allowedTypes.add(
                type
            );

        }

        return this;

    }

    removeAllowedType(type) {

        this.allowedTypes.delete(
            type
        );

        return this;

    }

    isAllowedType(type) {

        return (
            this.allowedTypes.size === 0 ||
            this.allowedTypes.has(type)
        );

    }

}

export class RateLimiter {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.maxRequests =
            Math.max(
                1,
                Number(options.maxRequests ?? 30)
            );

        this.windowMs =
            Math.max(
                1,
                Number(options.windowMs ?? 1000)
            );

        this.clients = new Map();

    }

    _getClient(id) {

        if (!this.clients.has(id)) {

            this.clients.set(
                id,
                {
                    count: 0,
                    startedAt: Date.now()
                }
            );

        }

        return this.clients.get(id);

    }

    check(id) {

        if (!id) {

            return {
                allowed: false,
                reason: "missing_client"
            };

        }

        const now =
            Date.now();

        const client =
            this._getClient(id);

        if (
            now - client.startedAt >=
            this.windowMs
        ) {

            client.count = 0;

            client.startedAt = now;

        }

        if (
            client.count >=
            this.maxRequests
        ) {

            return {

                allowed: false,

                reason:
                    "rate_limit_exceeded",

                remaining: 0,

                retryAfter:
                    Math.max(
                        0,
                        this.windowMs -
                        (now - client.startedAt)
                    )

            };

        }

        client.count++;

        return {

            allowed: true,

            remaining:
                Math.max(
                    0,
                    this.maxRequests -
                    client.count
                ),

            retryAfter: 0

        };

    }

    peek(id) {

        if (!id) {

            return {
                allowed: false,
                remaining: 0
            };

        }

        const client =
            this.clients.get(id);

        if (!client) {

            return {

                allowed: true,

                remaining:
                    this.maxRequests

            };

        }

        const elapsed =
            Date.now() -
            client.startedAt;

        if (
            elapsed >=
            this.windowMs
        ) {

            return {

                allowed: true,

                remaining:
                    this.maxRequests

            };

        }

        return {

            allowed:
                client.count <
                this.maxRequests,

            remaining:
                Math.max(
                    0,
                    this.maxRequests -
                    client.count
                ),

            retryAfter:
                Math.max(
                    0,
                    this.windowMs -
                    elapsed
                )

        };

    }

    reset(id) {

        return this.clients.delete(id);

    }

    resetAll() {

        this.clients.clear();

    }

    cleanup() {

        const now =
            Date.now();

        let removed = 0;

        for (
            const [id, client]
            of this.clients
        ) {

            if (
                now - client.startedAt >=
                this.windowMs
            ) {

                this.clients.delete(id);

                removed++;

            }

        }

        return removed;

    }

}

export class SnapshotBroadcaster {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.connectionManager =
            options.connectionManager ?? null;

        this.intervalMs =
            Math.max(
                0,
                Number(options.intervalMs ?? 50)
            );

        this.snapshotProvider =
            typeof options.snapshotProvider === "function"
                ? options.snapshotProvider
                : null;

        this.timer = null;

        this.sequence = 0;

        this.lastSnapshot = null;

        this.running = false;

    }

    initialize(connectionManager) {

        this.connectionManager =
            connectionManager;

        return this;

    }

    setSnapshotProvider(provider) {

        if (
            provider !== null &&
            typeof provider !== "function"
        ) {

            throw new TypeError(
                "Snapshot provider must be a function."
            );

        }

        this.snapshotProvider =
            provider;

        return this;

    }

    createSnapshot() {

        if (!this.snapshotProvider) {

            return {

                sequence:
                    ++this.sequence,

                timestamp:
                    Date.now(),

                data: {}

            };

        }

        const data =
            this.snapshotProvider();

        return {

            sequence:
                ++this.sequence,

            timestamp:
                Date.now(),

            data

        };

    }

    broadcast(snapshot = null) {

        if (
            !this.connectionManager
        ) {

            return {

                sent: 0,

                snapshot: null,

                reason:
                    "connection_manager_not_configured"

            };

        }

        const current =
            snapshot ??
            this.createSnapshot();

        this.lastSnapshot =
            current;

        const sent =
            this.connectionManager.broadcast(
                "snapshot",
                current
            );

        return {

            sent,

            snapshot:
                current

        };

    }

    tick() {

        return this.broadcast();

    }

    start() {

        if (this.running) {

            return false;

        }

        if (
            !this.connectionManager
        ) {

            return false;

        }

        this.running = true;

        if (this.intervalMs > 0) {

            this.timer =
                setInterval(
                    () => {

                        try {

                            this.tick();

                        } catch {
                            // Snapshot failures must not
                            // terminate the broadcaster.
                        }

                    },
                    this.intervalMs
                );

        }

        return true;

    }

    stop() {

        if (this.timer) {

            clearInterval(
                this.timer
            );

            this.timer = null;

        }

        this.running = false;

        return this;

    }

    getLastSnapshot() {

        return this.lastSnapshot;

    }

    isRunning() {

        return this.running;

    }

}
