CHEMIN : C:\Users\hp\Downloads\ISLAMCRAFT V2.0\server\src\network\SocketServer.js 
========================================== 
import { ConnectionManager } from "./ConnectionManager.js";
import { EventRouter } from "./EventRouter.js";
import { InputReceiver } from "./InputReceiver.js";
import { NetworkMetrics } from "./NetworkMetrics.js";
import { PacketValidator } from "./PacketValidator.js";
import { RateLimiter } from "./RateLimiter.js";
import { SnapshotBroadcaster } from "./SnapshotBroadcaster.js";

export class SocketServer {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.options = {
            ...options
        };

        this.connectionManager =
            options.connectionManager ??
            new ConnectionManager(
                options
            );

        this.eventRouter =
            options.eventRouter ??
            new EventRouter();

        this.inputReceiver =
            options.inputReceiver ??
            new InputReceiver({
                router:
                    this.eventRouter
            });

        this.metrics =
            options.metrics ??
            new NetworkMetrics();

        this.packetValidator =
            options.packetValidator ??
            new PacketValidator(
                options
            );

        this.rateLimiter =
            options.rateLimiter ??
            new RateLimiter(
                options
            );

        this.snapshotBroadcaster =
            options.snapshotBroadcaster ??
            new SnapshotBroadcaster({
                connectionManager:
                    this.connectionManager,

                intervalMs:
                    options.snapshotIntervalMs,

                snapshotProvider:
                    options.snapshotProvider

            });

        this.server = null;

        this.running = false;

        this.connections = new Map();

    }

    async start(server = null) {

        if (this.running) {

            return {

                success: true,

                alreadyRunning: true

            };

        }

        this.server =
            server;

        this.running = true;

        this.eventRouter.emit(
            "serverStarted",
            {
                timestamp:
                    Date.now()
            }
        );

        this.snapshotBroadcaster.start();

        return {

            success: true,

            timestamp:
                Date.now()

        };

    }

    async stop() {

        if (!this.running) {

            return {

                success: true,

                alreadyStopped: true

            };

        }

        this.snapshotBroadcaster.stop();

        for (
            const connection
            of this.connectionManager.getAll()
        ) {

            try {

                connection.close?.();

            } catch {
                // Ignore individual close failures.
            }

        }

        this.connections.clear();

        this.running = false;

        await this.eventRouter.emit(
            "serverStopped",
            {
                timestamp:
                    Date.now()
            }
        );

        return {

            success: true,

            timestamp:
                Date.now()

        };

    }

    registerConnection(connection) {

        const result =
            this.connectionManager.add(
                connection
            );

        if (!result.success) {

            this.metrics.recordRejected();

            return result;

        }

        this.connections.set(
            connection.id,
            connection
        );

        this.eventRouter.emit(
            "connection",
            {
                connection
            }
        );

        return result;

    }

    removeConnection(connectionId) {

        const connection =
            this.connections.get(
                connectionId
            );

        this.connections.delete(
            connectionId
        );

        const removed =
            this.connectionManager.remove(
                connectionId
            );

        if (removed) {

            this.eventRouter.emit(
                "disconnect",
                {
                    connectionId,
                    connection
                }
            );

        }

        return removed;

    }

    receive(
        connectionId,
        packet
    ) {

        const connection =
            this.connections.get(
                connectionId
            );

        if (!connection) {

            this.metrics.recordRejected();

            return {

                accepted: false,

                reason:
                    "connection_not_found"

            };

        }

        const rate =
            this.rateLimiter.check(
                connectionId
            );

        if (!rate.allowed) {

            this.metrics.recordRejected();

            return {

                accepted: false,

                reason:
                    rate.reason,

                retryAfter:
                    rate.retryAfter

            };

        }

        const rawSize =
            typeof packet === "string"
                ? packet.length
                : JSON.stringify(packet ?? {})
                    .length;

        this.metrics.recordReceived(
            rawSize
        );

        const validation =
            this.packetValidator.validate(
                packet
            );

        if (!validation.valid) {

            this.metrics.recordRejected();

            return {

                accepted: false,

                reason:
                    validation.reason

            };

        }

        const input =
            this.inputReceiver.receive(
                connection.playerId ??
                connection.id,
                validation.packet
            );

        if (!input.accepted) {

            this.metrics.recordRejected();

            return input;

        }

        this.eventRouter.emit(
            validation.packet.type,
            {

                connectionId,

                connection,

                packet:
                    validation.packet

            }
        );

        return {

            accepted: true,

            packet:
                validation.packet

        };

    }

    send(
        connectionId,
        event,
        payload
    ) {

        const connection =
            this.connections.get(
                connectionId
            );

        if (!connection) {

            return {

                success: false,

                reason:
                    "connection_not_found"

            };

        }

        try {

            if (
                typeof connection.send ===
                "function"
            ) {

                connection.send(
                    event,
                    payload
                );

            } else {

                return {

                    success: false,

                    reason:
                        "connection_send_unavailable"

                };

            }

            this.metrics.recordSent(
                JSON.stringify(
                    payload ?? {}
                ).length
            );

            return {

                success: true

            };

        } catch (error) {

            this.metrics.recordError();

            return {

                success: false,

                reason:
                    "send_failed",

                error

            };

        }

    }

    broadcast(event, payload) {

        const sent =
            this.connectionManager.broadcast(
                event,
                payload
            );

        this.metrics.sentPackets += sent;

        return {

            success: true,

            sent

        };

    }

    on(event, handler) {

        return this.eventRouter.on(
            event,
            handler
        );

    }

    once(event, handler) {

        return this.eventRouter.once(
            event,
            handler
        );

    }

    getMetrics() {

        return this.metrics.snapshot();

    }

    getConnectionCount() {

        return this.connectionManager.count();

    }

    isRunning() {

        return this.running;

    }

}

export class MatchDataStore {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.matches = new Map();

        this.maxEntries =
            Math.max(
                1,
                Number(options.maxEntries ?? 1000)
            );

    }

    create(matchId, data = {}) {

        if (!matchId) {

            return {
                success: false,
                reason: "missing_match_id"
            };

        }

        if (this.matches.has(matchId)) {

            return {
                success: false,
                reason: "match_already_exists"
            };

        }

        const record = {

            id: matchId,

            data: {
                ...data
            },

            createdAt:
                Date.now(),

            updatedAt:
                Date.now()

        };

        this.matches.set(
            matchId,
            record
        );

        this._enforceLimit();

        return {

            success: true,

            match:
                this._clone(record)

        };

    }

    save(matchId, data = {}) {

        const existing =
            this.matches.get(matchId);

        if (!existing) {

            return this.create(
                matchId,
                data
            );

        }

        existing.data = {
            ...existing.data,
            ...data
        };

        existing.updatedAt =
            Date.now();

        return {

            success: true,

            match:
                this._clone(existing)

        };

    }

    get(matchId) {

        const record =
            this.matches.get(matchId);

        return record
            ? this._clone(record)
            : null;

    }

    has(matchId) {

        return this.matches.has(
            matchId
        );

    }

    delete(matchId) {

        return this.matches.delete(
            matchId
        );

    }

    list(options = {}) {

        let records = [
            ...this.matches.values()
        ];

        if (options.limit !== undefined) {

            records =
                records.slice(
                    0,
                    Math.max(
                        0,
                        Number(options.limit)
                    )
                );

        }

        return records.map(
            record =>
                this._clone(record)
        );

    }

    clear() {

        this.matches.clear();

    }

    count() {

        return this.matches.size;

    }

    _enforceLimit() {

        while (
            this.matches.size >
            this.maxEntries
        ) {

            const oldestId =
                this.matches.keys().next().value;

            this.matches.delete(
                oldestId
            );

        }

    }

    _clone(value) {

        return JSON.parse(
            JSON.stringify(value)
        );

    }

}

export class PlayerDataStore {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.players = new Map();

        this.maxEntries =
            Math.max(
                1,
                Number(options.maxEntries ?? 10000)
            );

    }

    create(playerId, data = {}) {

        if (!playerId) {

            return {
                success: false,
                reason: "missing_player_id"
            };

        }

        if (this.players.has(playerId)) {

            return {
                success: false,
                reason: "player_already_exists"
            };

        }

        const record = {

            id: playerId,

            data: {
                ...data
            },

            createdAt:
                Date.now(),

            updatedAt:
                Date.now()

        };

        this.players.set(
            playerId,
            record
        );

        this._enforceLimit();

        return {

            success: true,

            player:
                this._clone(record)

        };

    }

    save(playerId, data = {}) {

        const existing =
            this.players.get(playerId);

        if (!existing) {

            return this.create(
                playerId,
                data
            );

        }

        existing.data = {

            ...existing.data,

            ...data

        };

        existing.updatedAt =
            Date.now();

        return {

            success: true,

            player:
                this._clone(existing)

        };

    }

    set(playerId, path, value) {

        const existing =
            this.players.get(playerId);

        if (!existing) {

            return {

                success: false,

                reason:
                    "player_not_found"

            };

        }

        const parts =
            String(path)
                .split(".")
                .filter(Boolean);

        if (parts.length === 0) {

            return {

                success: false,

                reason:
                    "invalid_path"

            };

        }

        let target =
            existing.data;

        for (
            let index = 0;
            index < parts.length - 1;
            index++
        ) {

            const key =
                parts[index];

            if (
                !target[key] ||
                typeof target[key] !== "object"
            ) {

                target[key] = {};

            }

            target =
                target[key];

        }

        target[
            parts[parts.length - 1]
        ] = value;

        existing.updatedAt =
            Date.now();

        return {

            success: true,

            player:
                this._clone(existing)

        };

    }

    get(playerId) {

        const record =
            this.players.get(playerId);

        return record
            ? this._clone(record)
            : null;

    }

    getData(playerId) {

        const record =
            this.players.get(playerId);

        return record
            ? this._clone(record.data)
            : null;

    }

    has(playerId) {

        return this.players.has(
            playerId
        );

    }

    delete(playerId) {

        return this.players.delete(
            playerId
        );

    }

    list() {

        return [
            ...this.players.values()
        ].map(
            player =>
                this._clone(player)
        );

    }

    count() {

        return this.players.size;

    }

    clear() {

        this.players.clear();

    }

    _enforceLimit() {

        while (
            this.players.size >
            this.maxEntries
        ) {

            const oldestId =
                this.players.keys().next().value;

            this.players.delete(
                oldestId
            );

        }

    }

    _clone(value) {

        return JSON.parse(
            JSON.stringify(value)
        );

    }

}

export class SaveManager {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.stores = new Map();

        this.autoSaveInterval =
            Math.max(
                0,
                Number(options.autoSaveInterval ?? 0)
            );

        this.saveCallback =
            typeof options.saveCallback === "function"
                ? options.saveCallback
                : null;

        this.timer = null;

        this.saving = false;

    }

    registerStore(name, store) {

        if (!name) {

            throw new TypeError(
                "Store name is required."
            );

        }

        if (
            !store ||
            typeof store !== "object"
        ) {

            throw new TypeError(
                "A valid store is required."
            );

        }

        this.stores.set(
            name,
            store
        );

        return this;

    }

    unregisterStore(name) {

        return this.stores.delete(
            name
        );

    }

    getStore(name) {

        return (
            this.stores.get(name) ??
            null
        );

    }

    createSnapshot() {

        const snapshot = {

            version: 1,

            timestamp:
                Date.now(),

            stores: {}

        };

        for (
            const [name, store]
            of this.stores
        ) {

            if (
                typeof store.list ===
                "function"
            ) {

                snapshot.stores[name] =
                    store.list();

                continue;

            }

            if (
                typeof store.serialize ===
                "function"
            ) {

                snapshot.stores[name] =
                    store.serialize();

                continue;

            }

            snapshot.stores[name] = null;

        }

        return snapshot;

    }

    async save() {

        if (this.saving) {

            return {

                success: false,

                reason:
                    "save_already_in_progress"

            };

        }

        this.saving = true;

        try {

            const snapshot =
                this.createSnapshot();

            if (this.saveCallback) {

                await this.saveCallback(
                    snapshot
                );

            }

            return {

                success: true,

                snapshot

            };

        } catch (error) {

            return {

                success: false,

                reason:
                    "save_failed",

                error

            };

        } finally {

            this.saving = false;

        }

    }

    startAutoSave() {

        if (
            this.autoSaveInterval <= 0 ||
            this.timer
        ) {

            return false;

        }

        this.timer =
            setInterval(
                async () => {

                    await this.save();

                },
                this.autoSaveInterval
            );

        return true;

    }

    stopAutoSave() {

        if (!this.timer) {

            return false;

        }

        clearInterval(
            this.timer
        );

        this.timer = null;

        return true;

    }

    isSaving() {

        return this.saving;

    }

    isAutoSaving() {

        return this.timer !== null;

    }

    clearStores() {

        for (
            const store
            of this.stores.values()
        ) {

            if (
                typeof store.clear ===
                "function"
            ) {

                store.clear();

            }

        }

    }

}

export class IdGenerator {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.prefix =
            options.prefix ?? "";

        this.counter =
            Number(options.start ?? 0);

        this.randomLength =
            Math.max(
                0,
                Number(options.randomLength ?? 8)
            );

    }

    next(prefix = this.prefix) {

        this.counter++;

        const timestamp =
            Date.now().toString(36);

        const counter =
            this.counter.toString(36);

        const random =
            this.random(
                this.randomLength
            );

        const parts = [
            prefix,
            timestamp,
            counter,
            random
        ].filter(Boolean);

        return parts.join("_");

    }

    random(length = this.randomLength) {

        if (length <= 0) {

            return "";

        }

        const characters =
            "abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz" +
            "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ" +
            "0123456789";

        let result = "";

        for (
            let index = 0;
            index < length;
            index++
        ) {

            result +=
                characters[
                    Math.floor(
                        Math.random() *
                        characters.length
                    )
                ];

        }

        return result;

    }

    uuid() {

        if (
            typeof crypto !== "undefined" &&
            typeof crypto.randomUUID === "function"
        ) {

            return crypto.randomUUID();

        }

        return "xxxxxxxx-xxxx-4xxx-yxxx-xxxxxxxxxxxx"
            .replace(
                /[xy]/g,
                character => {

                    const random =
                        Math.random() * 16 | 0;

                    const value =
                        character === "x"
                            ? random
                            : (
                                random & 0x3 |
                                0x8
                            );

                    return value.toString(16);

                }
            );

    }

    numeric() {

        this.counter++;

        return this.counter;

    }

    reset(value = 0) {

        this.counter =
            Number(value) || 0;

        return this;

    }

}

export class Logger {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.name =
            options.name ?? "IslamCraft";

        this.level =
            options.level ?? "info";

        this.enabled =
            options.enabled !== false;

        this.levels = {

            debug: 0,
            info: 1,
            warn: 2,
            error: 3,
            silent: 4

        };

    }

    shouldLog(level) {

        if (!this.enabled) {

            return false;

        }

        const current =
            this.levels[
                this.level
            ] ??
            this.levels.info;

        const target =
            this.levels[
                level
            ] ??
            this.levels.info;

        return target >= current;

    }

    format(level, message) {

        const timestamp =
            new Date().toISOString();

        const prefix =
            `[${timestamp}] ` +
            `[${this.name}] ` +
            `[${level.toUpperCase()}]`;

        return `${prefix} ${message}`;

    }

    write(level, message, ...args) {

        if (
            !this.shouldLog(level)
        ) {

            return;

        }

        const formatted =
            this.format(
                level,
                message
            );

        if (level === "error") {

            console.error(
                formatted,
                ...args
            );

            return;

        }

        if (level === "warn") {

            console.warn(
                formatted,
                ...args
            );

            return;

        }

        console.log(
            formatted,
            ...args
        );

    }

    debug(message, ...args) {

        this.write(
            "debug",
            message,
            ...args
        );

    }

    info(message, ...args) {

        this.write(
            "info",
            message,
            ...args
        );

    }

    warn(message, ...args) {

        this.write(
            "warn",
            message,
            ...args
        );

    }

    error(message, ...args) {

        this.write(
            "error",
            message,
            ...args
        );

    }

    child(name) {

        return new Logger({

            name:
                `${this.name}:${name}`,

            level:
                this.level,

            enabled:
                this.enabled

        });

    }

    setLevel(level) {

        if (
            this.levels[level] === undefined
        ) {

            throw new Error(
                `Unknown log level: ${level}`
            );

        }

        this.level =
            level;

        return this;

    }

    setEnabled(enabled) {

        this.enabled =
            Boolean(enabled);

        return this;

    }

}

export class PerformanceMonitor {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.samples = new Map();

        this.maxSamples =
            Math.max(
                1,
                Number(options.maxSamples ?? 1000)
            );

        this.startedAt =
            Date.now();

    }

    start(name) {

        if (!name) {

            throw new TypeError(
                "Performance sample name is required."
            );

        }

        return {

            name,

            startedAt:
                this.now()

        };

    }

    end(timer) {

        if (
            !timer ||
            !timer.name
        ) {

            return null;

        }

        const duration =
            Math.max(
                0,
                this.now() -
                timer.startedAt
            );

        this.record(
            timer.name,
            duration
        );

        return duration;

    }

    measure(name, callback) {

        const timer =
            this.start(name);

        try {

            const result =
                callback();

            if (
                result &&
                typeof result.then ===
                "function"
            ) {

                return result
                    .then(
                        value => {

                            this.end(timer);

                            return value;

                        }
                    )
                    .catch(
                        error => {

                            this.end(timer);

                            throw error;

                        }
                    );

            }

            this.end(timer);

            return result;

        } catch (error) {

            this.end(timer);

            throw error;

        }

    }

    record(name, duration) {

        const value =
            Number(duration);

        if (
            !Number.isFinite(value)
        ) {

            return;

        }

        if (!this.samples.has(name)) {

            this.samples.set(
                name,
                []
            );

        }

        const samples =
            this.samples.get(name);

        samples.push(
            value
        );

        if (
            samples.length >
            this.maxSamples
        ) {

            samples.shift();

        }

    }

    get(name) {

        const samples =
            this.samples.get(name) ??
            [];

        if (samples.length === 0) {

            return {

                name,

                count: 0,

                average: 0,

                minimum: 0,

                maximum: 0

            };

        }

        const total =
            samples.reduce(
                (sum, value) =>
                    sum + value,
                0
            );

        return {

            name,

            count:
                samples.length,

            average:
                total /
                samples.length,

            minimum:
                Math.min(...samples),

            maximum:
                Math.max(...samples)

        };

    }

    getAll() {

        const result = {};

        for (
            const name
            of this.samples.keys()
        ) {

            result[name] =
                this.get(name);

        }

        return result;

    }

    clear(name = null) {

        if (name === null) {

            this.samples.clear();

            return;

        }

        this.samples.delete(
            name
        );

    }

    now() {

        if (
            typeof performance !==
            "undefined" &&
            typeof performance.now ===
            "function"
        ) {

            return performance.now();

        }

        return Date.now();

    }

    uptime() {

        return Date.now() -
            this.startedAt;

    }

}

import { WorldSeed } from "./WorldSeed.js";

import { BiomeManager } from "./biomes/BiomeManager.js";

import { ChunkManager } from "./chunks/ChunkManager.js";

import { NoiseGenerator } from "./generation/NoiseGenerator.js";

import { HeightGenerator } from "./generation/HeightGenerator.js";

import { BiomeGenerator } from "./generation/BiomeGenerator.js";

import { TerrainGenerator } from "./generation/TerrainGenerator.js";

import { ObjectGenerator } from "./generation/ObjectGenerator.js";

import { StructureGenerator } from "./generation/StructureGenerator.js";

import { SpawnManager } from "./spawning/SpawnManager.js";

export class WorldManager {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.name =
            options.name ?? "islamcraft_world";

        this.seed =
            options.seed instanceof WorldSeed

                ? options.seed

                : new WorldSeed(options.seed);

        this.worldHeight =
            Number(options.worldHeight ?? 128);

        this.chunkSize =
            Number(options.chunkSize ?? 16);

        this.biomeManager =
            options.biomeManager ??
            new BiomeManager();

        this.noiseGenerator =
            options.noiseGenerator ??
            new NoiseGenerator({

                seed: this.seed.get(),

                scale:
                    options.noiseScale ?? 0.05

            });

        this.temperatureNoise =
            new NoiseGenerator({

                seed:
                    this.seed
                        .createChild("temperature")
                        .get(),

                scale: 0.01

            });

        this.humidityNoise =
            new NoiseGenerator({

                seed:
                    this.seed
                        .createChild("humidity")
                        .get(),

                scale: 0.01

            });

        this.heightGenerator =
            new HeightGenerator({

                noise:
                    this.noiseGenerator,

                baseHeight:
                    options.baseHeight ?? 32,

                amplitude:
                    options.heightAmplitude ?? 24,

                maxHeight:
                    this.worldHeight - 1

            });

        this.biomeGenerator =
            new BiomeGenerator({

                biomeManager:
                    this.biomeManager,

                temperatureNoise:
                    this.temperatureNoise,

                humidityNoise:
                    this.humidityNoise

            });

        this.terrainGenerator =
            new TerrainGenerator({

                heightGenerator:
                    this.heightGenerator,

                biomeGenerator:
                    this.biomeGenerator,

                chunkSize:
                    this.chunkSize,

                worldHeight:
                    this.worldHeight

            });

        this.objectGenerator =
            new ObjectGenerator({

                seed:
                    this.seed
                        .createChild("objects")
                        .get(),

                biomeManager:
                    this.biomeManager

            });

        this.structureGenerator =
            new StructureGenerator({

                seed:
                    this.seed
                        .createChild("structures")
                        .get()

            });

        this.chunkManager =
            new ChunkManager({

                chunkSize:
                    this.chunkSize,

                worldHeight:
                    this.worldHeight,

                seed:
                    this.seed.get(),

                biomeManager:
                    this.biomeManager

            });

        this.spawnManager =
            new SpawnManager();

        this.server = null;

        this.initialized = false;

        this.started = false;

    }

    initialize(server = null) {

        this.server = server;

        this.chunkManager.initialize(this);

        this.initialized = true;

        return this;

    }

    start() {

        if (!this.initialized) {

            this.initialize(this.server);

        }

        this.started = true;

        return this;

    }

    stop() {

        this.started = false;

        this.chunkManager.unloadAll();

    }

    getChunk(x, z) {

        return this.chunkManager.getChunk(
            Number(x),
            Number(z)
        );

    }

    loadChunk(x, z) {

        return this.chunkManager.loadChunk(
            Number(x),
            Number(z)
        );

    }

    unloadChunk(x, z) {

        return this.chunkManager.unloadChunk(
            Number(x),
            Number(z)
        );

    }

    getBiome(x, z) {

        return this.biomeGenerator.getBiome(
            Number(x),
            Number(z)
        );

    }

    getHeight(x, z) {

        return this.heightGenerator.getHeight(
            Number(x),
            Number(z)
        );

    }

    generateTerrain(x, z) {

        return this.terrainGenerator.generateColumn(
            Number(x),
            Number(z)
        );

    }

    generateObjects(x, z) {

        const biome =
            this.getBiome(x, z);

        return this.objectGenerator.generateForColumn(
            Number(x),
            Number(z),
            biome
        );

    }

    generateChunkContent(chunkX, chunkZ) {

        const terrain =
            this.terrainGenerator.generateChunk(
                Number(chunkX),
                Number(chunkZ)
            );

        const objects =
            this.objectGenerator.generateChunk(
                Number(chunkX),
                Number(chunkZ),
                this.chunkSize
            );

        const structures =
            this.structureGenerator.generateChunk(
                Number(chunkX),
                Number(chunkZ),
                this.chunkSize,
                this.heightGenerator.baseHeight
            );

        return {

            x: Number(chunkX),

            z: Number(chunkZ),

            terrain,

            objects,

            structures

        };

    }

    spawnPlayer(player, spawnId = null) {

        return this.spawnManager.spawnPlayer(
            player,
            spawnId
        );

    }

    update(deltaTime = 0, tick = 0) {

        if (!this.started) {

            return;

        }

        this.chunkManager.update(
            deltaTime,
            tick
        );

        this.spawnManager.update(
            deltaTime,
            tick
        );

    }

    getLoadedChunks() {

        return this.chunkManager.getLoadedChunks();

    }

    getSeed() {

        return this.seed.get();

    }

    getBiomeManager() {

        return this.biomeManager;

    }

    getChunkManager() {

        return this.chunkManager;

    }

    getSpawnManager() {

        return this.spawnManager;

    }

    serialize() {

        return {

            name:
                this.name,

            seed:
                this.seed.get(),

            worldHeight:
                this.worldHeight,

            chunkSize:
                this.chunkSize,

            initialized:
                this.initialized,

            started:
                this.started

        };

    }

    destroy() {

        this.stop();

        this.chunkManager.clear();

        this.spawnManager.clear();

        this.server = null;

        this.initialized = false;

    }

}

export class WorldSeed {

    constructor(seed = null) {

        this.value =
            seed === null ||
            seed === undefined ||
            seed === ""
                ? this.generate()
                : this.normalize(seed);

    }

    normalize(seed) {

        if (typeof seed === "number") {

            return Number.isFinite(seed)
                ? Math.trunc(seed)
                : 0;

        }

        const text =
            String(seed);

        let hash = 2166136261;

        for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i++) {

            hash ^= text.charCodeAt(i);

            hash +=
                (hash << 1) +
                (hash << 4) +
                (hash << 7) +
                (hash << 8) +
                (hash << 24);

        }

        return hash | 0;

    }

    generate() {

        return Math.floor(

            Math.random() *
            0xFFFFFFFF

        );

    }

    get() {

        return this.value;

    }

    toString() {

        return String(this.value);

    }

    equals(other) {

        if (other instanceof WorldSeed) {

            return this.value === other.value;

        }

        return this.value ===
            this.normalize(other);

    }

    createChild(label) {

        return new WorldSeed(

            this.normalize(
                `${this.value}:${label}`
            )

        );

    }

    serialize() {

        return {

            value: this.value

        };

    }

}

import { DesertBiome } from "./DesertBiome.js";
import { ForestBiome } from "./ForestBiome.js";
import { PlainsBiome } from "./PlainsBiome.js";

export class BiomeManager {

    constructor() {

        this.biomes = new Map();

        this.registerDefaults();

    }

    register(biome) {

        if (!biome || !biome.id) {

            throw new TypeError(

                "Un biome valide est requis."

            );

        }

        if (this.biomes.has(biome.id)) {

            throw new Error(

                `Biome déjà enregistré: ${biome.id}`

            );

        }

        this.biomes.set(

            biome.id,

            biome

        );

        return biome;

    }

    registerDefaults() {

        this.register(new DesertBiome());

        this.register(new ForestBiome());

        this.register(new PlainsBiome());

    }

    get(id) {

        return this.biomes.get(id) ?? null;

    }

    has(id) {

        return this.biomes.has(id);

    }

    getAll() {

        return [...this.biomes.values()];

    }

    getIds() {

        return [...this.biomes.keys()];

    }

    selectFromTemperatureAndHumidity(

        temperature,

        humidity

    ) {

        let selected = this.get("plains");

        let bestDistance = Infinity;

        for (const biome of this.biomes.values()) {

            const temperatureDistance =

                temperature - biome.temperature;

            const humidityDistance =

                humidity - biome.humidity;

            const distance =

                temperatureDistance ** 2 +

                humidityDistance ** 2;

            if (distance < bestDistance) {

                bestDistance = distance;

                selected = biome;

            }

        }

        return selected;

    }

    serialize() {

        return this.getAll().map(

            biome => biome.serialize()

        );

    }

    clear() {

        this.biomes.clear();

    }

}

export class Chunk {

    constructor(data = {}) {

        this.x = Number(data.x ?? 0);
        this.z = Number(data.z ?? 0);

        this.size = Number(data.size ?? 16);
        this.height = Number(data.height ?? 128);

        this.blocks = data.blocks ?? new Map();

        this.generated = Boolean(data.generated);
        this.loaded = Boolean(data.loaded);
        this.dirty = Boolean(data.dirty);

        this.biomeId = data.biomeId ?? "plains";

        this.lastAccess = Date.now();

    }

    getKey() {

        return `${this.x}:${this.z}`;

    }

    getBlockKey(x, y, z) {

        return `${x}:${y}:${z}`;

    }

    setBlock(x, y, z, block) {

        if (!this.isInside(x, y, z)) {

            return false;

        }

        const key = this.getBlockKey(x, y, z);

        this.blocks.set(key, block);

        this.dirty = true;

        this.lastAccess = Date.now();

        return true;

    }

    getBlock(x, y, z) {

        if (!this.isInside(x, y, z)) {

            return null;

        }

        this.lastAccess = Date.now();

        return this.blocks.get(

            this.getBlockKey(x, y, z)

        ) ?? null;

    }

    removeBlock(x, y, z) {

        if (!this.isInside(x, y, z)) {

            return false;

        }

        const removed = this.blocks.delete(

            this.getBlockKey(x, y, z)

        );

        if (removed) {

            this.dirty = true;

        }

        return removed;

    }

    isInside(x, y, z) {

        return (

            Number.isInteger(x) &&

            Number.isInteger(y) &&

            Number.isInteger(z) &&

            x >= 0 &&

            x < this.size &&

            y >= 0 &&

            y < this.height &&

            z >= 0 &&

            z < this.size

        );

    }

    markGenerated() {

        this.generated = true;

        this.loaded = true;

        this.dirty = false;

        this.lastAccess = Date.now();

    }

    markLoaded() {

        this.loaded = true;

        this.lastAccess = Date.now();

    }

    markUnloaded() {

        this.loaded = false;

    }

    markDirty() {

        this.dirty = true;

    }

    clean() {

        this.dirty = false;

    }

    serialize() {

        return {

            x: this.x,

            z: this.z,

            size: this.size,

            height: this.height,

            biomeId: this.biomeId,

            generated: this.generated,

            blocks: Object.fromEntries(this.blocks)

        };

    }

}

export class ChunkCache {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.maxSize = Number(

            options.maxSize ?? 256

        );

        this.chunks = new Map();

    }

    makeKey(x, z) {

        return `${x}:${z}`;

    }

    set(chunk) {

        if (!chunk) {

            return false;

        }

        const key = this.makeKey(

            chunk.x,

            chunk.z

        );

        if (this.chunks.has(key)) {

            this.chunks.delete(key);

        }

        this.chunks.set(key, chunk);

        this.enforceLimit();

        return true;

    }

    get(x, z) {

        const key = this.makeKey(x, z);

        const chunk = this.chunks.get(key);

        if (!chunk) {

            return null;

        }

        this.chunks.delete(key);

        this.chunks.set(key, chunk);

        chunk.lastAccess = Date.now();

        return chunk;

    }

    has(x, z) {

        return this.chunks.has(

            this.makeKey(x, z)

        );

    }

    delete(x, z) {

        return this.chunks.delete(

            this.makeKey(x, z)

        );

    }

    enforceLimit() {

        while (

            this.chunks.size >

            this.maxSize

        ) {

            const oldestKey =

                this.chunks.keys().next().value;

            this.chunks.delete(oldestKey);

        }

    }

    values() {

        return [...this.chunks.values()];

    }

    clear() {

        this.chunks.clear();

    }

    get size() {

        return this.chunks.size;

    }

} 
.

CHEMIN : C:\Users\hp\Downloads\ISLAMCRAFT V2.0\server\src\world\chunks\ChunkCleanup.js 
========================================== 
export class ChunkCleanup {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.maxInactiveTime = Number(

            options.maxInactiveTime ?? 300000

        );

    }

    cleanup(manager, timestamp = Date.now()) {

        if (!manager) {

            return 0;

        }

        const removed = [];

        for (const chunk of manager.getLoadedChunks()) {

            const inactiveTime =

                timestamp -

                chunk.lastAccess;

            if (

                !chunk.dirty &&

                inactiveTime >=

                    this.maxInactiveTime

            ) {

                removed.push(

                    manager.unloadChunk(

                        chunk.x,

                        chunk.z

                    )

                );

            }

        }

        return removed.filter(Boolean).length;

    }

}

========================================== 
import { Chunk } from "./Chunk.js";

export class ChunkGenerator {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.chunkSize = Number(

            options.chunkSize ?? 16

        );

        this.worldHeight = Number(

            options.worldHeight ?? 128

        );

        this.biomeManager =

            options.biomeManager ?? null;

        this.seed =

            Number(options.seed ?? 0);

    }

    initialize(world) {

        this.world = world;

        if (!this.biomeManager) {

            this.biomeManager =

                world?.biomeManager ?? null;

        }

        return this;

    }

    generate(x, z) {

        const chunk = new Chunk({

            x,

            z,

            size: this.chunkSize,

            height: this.worldHeight

        });

        const biome =

            this.selectBiome(x, z);

        chunk.biomeId = biome?.id ?? "plains";

        const baseHeight =

            biome?.terrain?.baseHeight ?? 4;

        const variation =

            biome?.terrain?.heightVariation ?? 3;

        for (

            let localX = 0;

            localX < this.chunkSize;

            localX++

        ) {

            for (

                let localZ = 0;

                localZ < this.chunkSize;

                localZ++

            ) {

                const worldX =

                    x * this.chunkSize +

                    localX;

                const worldZ =

                    z * this.chunkSize +

                    localZ;

                const noise =

                    this.noise(worldX, worldZ);

                const terrainHeight = Math.max(

                    1,

                    Math.min(

                        this.worldHeight - 1,

                        Math.floor(

                            baseHeight +

                            noise * variation

                        )

                    )

                );

                for (

                    let y = 0;

                    y <= terrainHeight;

                    y++

                ) {

                    let block;

                    if (y === terrainHeight) {

                        block =

                            biome?.terrain

                                ?.surfaceBlock ??

                            "grass";

                    } else if (

                        y >= terrainHeight - 3

                    ) {

                        block =

                            biome?.terrain

                                ?.undergroundBlock ??

                            "dirt";

                    } else {

                        block = "stone";

                    }

                    chunk.setBlock(

                        localX,

                        y,

                        localZ,

                        block

                    );

                }

            }

        }

        chunk.markGenerated();

        return chunk;

    }

    selectBiome(x, z) {

        if (!this.biomeManager) {

            return null;

        }

        const temperature =

            this.noise(x + 10000, z + 10000);

        const humidity =

            this.noise(x - 10000, z - 10000);

        return this.biomeManager

            .selectFromTemperatureAndHumidity(

                (temperature + 1) / 2,

                (humidity + 1) / 2

            );

    }

    noise(x, z) {

        let value =

            Math.sin(

                x * 12.9898 +

                z * 78.233 +

                this.seed

            ) *

            43758.5453;

        value -= Math.floor(value);

        return value * 2 - 1;

    }

}

import { ChunkCache } from "./ChunkCache.js";
import { ChunkGenerator } from "./ChunkGenerator.js";
import { ChunkCleanup } from "./ChunkCleanup.js";

export class ChunkManager {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.cache =

            options.cache ??

            new ChunkCache({

                maxSize:

                    options.maxCacheSize ?? 256

            });

        this.generator =

            options.generator ??

            new ChunkGenerator(options);

        this.cleanup =

            options.cleanup ??

            new ChunkCleanup(options);

        this.loaded = new Map();

        this.options = options;

    }

    initialize(world) {

        this.world = world;

        this.generator.initialize(world);

        return this;

    }

    makeKey(x, z) {

        return `${x}:${z}`;

    }

    loadChunk(x, z) {

        x = Number(x);
        z = Number(z);

        const key = this.makeKey(x, z);

        const existing =

            this.loaded.get(key);

        if (existing) {

            existing.markLoaded();

            return existing;

        }

        let chunk =

            this.cache.get(x, z);

        if (!chunk) {

            chunk =

                this.generator.generate(x, z);

        }

        chunk.markLoaded();

        this.loaded.set(key, chunk);

        return chunk;

    }

    unloadChunk(x, z) {

        const key = this.makeKey(x, z);

        const chunk =

            this.loaded.get(key);

        if (!chunk) {

            return false;

        }

        if (chunk.dirty) {

            return false;

        }

        chunk.markUnloaded();

        this.loaded.delete(key);

        this.cache.set(chunk);

        return true;

    }

    getChunk(x, z) {

        return (

            this.loaded.get(

                this.makeKey(x, z)

            ) ??

            this.cache.get(x, z)

        );

    }

    getOrLoadChunk(x, z) {

        return (

            this.getChunk(x, z) ??

            this.loadChunk(x, z)

        );

    }

    hasChunk(x, z) {

        return Boolean(

            this.getChunk(x, z)

        );

    }

    getLoadedChunks() {

        return [...this.loaded.values()];

    }

    update(deltaTime, tick) {

        this.cleanup.cleanup(this);

    }

    cleanupInactive(timestamp = Date.now()) {

        return this.cleanup.cleanup(

            this,

            timestamp

        );

    }

    unloadAll() {

        let count = 0;

        for (const chunk of this.loaded.values()) {

            if (chunk.dirty) {

                continue;

            }

            chunk.markUnloaded();

            this.cache.set(chunk);

            count++;

        }

        this.loaded.clear();

        return count;

    }

    clear() {

        this.loaded.clear();

        this.cache.clear();

    }

    get loadedCount() {

        return this.loaded.size;

    }

}

export class BiomeGenerator {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.biomeManager =
            options.biomeManager ?? null;

        this.temperatureNoise =
            options.temperatureNoise ?? null;

        this.humidityNoise =
            options.humidityNoise ?? null;

    }

    initialize({

        biomeManager,

        temperatureNoise,

        humidityNoise

    } = {}) {

        if (biomeManager) {

            this.biomeManager =
                biomeManager;

        }

        if (temperatureNoise) {

            this.temperatureNoise =
                temperatureNoise;

        }

        if (humidityNoise) {

            this.humidityNoise =
                humidityNoise;

        }

        return this;

    }

    getTemperature(x, z) {

        const value =
            this.temperatureNoise
                ? this.temperatureNoise.get(x, z)
                : 0;

        return (
            value + 1
        ) / 2;

    }

    getHumidity(x, z) {

        const value =
            this.humidityNoise
                ? this.humidityNoise.get(x, z)
                : 0;

        return (
            value + 1
        ) / 2;

    }

    getBiome(x, z) {

        if (!this.biomeManager) {

            return null;

        }

        return this.biomeManager
            .selectFromTemperatureAndHumidity(
                this.getTemperature(x, z),
                this.getHumidity(x, z)
            );

    }

    getBiomeId(x, z) {

        return this.getBiome(x, z)?.id ?? "plains";

    }

}

export class HeightGenerator {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.noise = options.noise ?? null;

        this.baseHeight = Number(
            options.baseHeight ?? 32
        );

        this.amplitude = Number(
            options.amplitude ?? 24
        );

        this.minHeight = Number(
            options.minHeight ?? 1
        );

        this.maxHeight = Number(
            options.maxHeight ?? 127
        );

    }

    initialize(noiseGenerator) {

        this.noise = noiseGenerator;

        return this;

    }

    clamp(value) {

        return Math.max(
            this.minHeight,
            Math.min(
                this.maxHeight,
                value
            )
        );

    }

    getHeight(x, z) {

        const noiseValue = this.noise
            ? this.noise.get(x, z)
            : 0;

        return Math.floor(

            this.clamp(
                this.baseHeight +
                noiseValue * this.amplitude
            )

        );

    }

    getNormalizedHeight(x, z) {

        const height = this.getHeight(x, z);

        return (
            height - this.minHeight
        ) / (
            this.maxHeight -
            this.minHeight
        );

    }

    setRange(minHeight, maxHeight) {

        this.minHeight = Number(minHeight);

        this.maxHeight = Number(maxHeight);

    }

}

export class NoiseGenerator {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.seed = Number(options.seed ?? 0);

        this.scale = Number(options.scale ?? 0.05);

        this.octaves = Math.max(
            1,
            Number(options.octaves ?? 4)
        );

        this.persistence = Number(
            options.persistence ?? 0.5
        );

        this.lacunarity = Number(
            options.lacunarity ?? 2
        );

    }

    hash(x, z) {

        const value =
            Math.sin(
                x * 127.1 +
                z * 311.7 +
                this.seed * 74.3
            ) * 43758.5453123;

        return value - Math.floor(value);

    }

    smooth(t) {

        return t * t * (3 - 2 * t);

    }

    interpolate(a, b, t) {

        return a + (b - a) * t;

    }

    valueNoise(x, z) {

        const x0 = Math.floor(x);
        const z0 = Math.floor(z);

        const x1 = x0 + 1;
        const z1 = z0 + 1;

        const tx = this.smooth(x - x0);
        const tz = this.smooth(z - z0);

        const n00 = this.hash(x0, z0);
        const n10 = this.hash(x1, z0);
        const n01 = this.hash(x0, z1);
        const n11 = this.hash(x1, z1);

        const nx0 =
            this.interpolate(n00, n10, tx);

        const nx1 =
            this.interpolate(n01, n11, tx);

        return this.interpolate(nx0, nx1, tz) * 2 - 1;

    }

    get(x, z) {

        let frequency = this.scale;

        let amplitude = 1;

        let value = 0;

        let amplitudeSum = 0;

        for (let i = 0; i < this.octaves; i++) {

            value +=
                this.valueNoise(
                    x * frequency,
                    z * frequency
                ) * amplitude;

            amplitudeSum += amplitude;

            amplitude *= this.persistence;

            frequency *= this.lacunarity;

        }

        if (amplitudeSum === 0) {

            return 0;

        }

        return value / amplitudeSum;

    }

    setSeed(seed) {

        this.seed = Number(seed) || 0;

    }

}

export class ObjectGenerator {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.seed = Number(options.seed ?? 0);

        this.biomeManager =
            options.biomeManager ?? null;

        this.defaultDensity =
            Number(options.defaultDensity ?? 0.02);

    }

    initialize(biomeManager) {

        this.biomeManager = biomeManager ?? this.biomeManager;

        return this;

    }

    hash(x, z, salt = 0) {

        const value =
            Math.sin(
                x * 127.1 +
                z * 311.7 +
                salt * 74.3 +
                this.seed
            ) * 43758.5453123;

        return value - Math.floor(value);

    }

    generateForColumn(x, z, biome = null) {

        const selectedBiome =
            biome ??
            this.biomeManager?.get(
                "plains"
            );

        if (!selectedBiome) {

            return [];

        }

        const objects = [];

        const definitions =
            selectedBiome.getObjects?.() ??
            selectedBiome.objects ??
            [];

        for (let index = 0; index < definitions.length; index++) {

            const definition = definitions[index];

            const rarity =
                Number(
                    definition.rarity ??
                    this.defaultDensity
                );

            const roll =
                this.hash(
                    x,
                    z,
                    index + 100
                );

            if (roll > rarity) {

                continue;

            }

            objects.push({

                type: definition.type,

                x,

                z,

                rotation:
                    this.hash(
                        x,
                        z,
                        index + 500
                    ) * Math.PI * 2

            });

        }

        return objects;

    }

    generateChunk(chunkX, chunkZ, chunkSize = 16) {

        const objects = [];

        const biomeManager =
            this.biomeManager;

        for (
            let localX = 0;
            localX < chunkSize;
            localX++
        ) {

            for (
                let localZ = 0;
                localZ < chunkSize;
                localZ++
            ) {

                const worldX =
                    chunkX * chunkSize +
                    localX;

                const worldZ =
                    chunkZ * chunkSize +
                    localZ;

                let biome = null;

                if (biomeManager) {

                    biome =
                        biomeManager
                            .selectFromTemperatureAndHumidity(
                                (
                                    this.hash(
                                        worldX,
                                        worldZ,
                                        1000
                                    ) + 1
                                ) / 2,
                                (
                                    this.hash(
                                        worldX,
                                        worldZ,
                                        2000
                                    ) + 1
                                ) / 2
                            );

                }

                objects.push(
                    ...this.generateForColumn(
                        worldX,
                        worldZ,
                        biome
                    )
                );

            }

        }

        return objects;

    }

}

export class TerrainGenerator {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.heightGenerator =
            options.heightGenerator ?? null;

        this.biomeGenerator =
            options.biomeGenerator ?? null;

        this.chunkSize = Number(
            options.chunkSize ?? 16
        );

        this.worldHeight = Number(
            options.worldHeight ?? 128
        );

    }

    initialize({

        heightGenerator,

        biomeGenerator

    } = {}) {

        if (heightGenerator) {

            this.heightGenerator =
                heightGenerator;

        }

        if (biomeGenerator) {

            this.biomeGenerator =
                biomeGenerator;

        }

        return this;

    }

    generateColumn(worldX, worldZ) {

        const biome =
            this.biomeGenerator?.getBiome(
                worldX,
                worldZ
            );

        const height =
            this.heightGenerator
                ? this.heightGenerator.getHeight(
                    worldX,
                    worldZ
                )
                : 1;

        const surfaceBlock =
            biome?.terrain?.surfaceBlock ??
            "grass";

        const undergroundBlock =
            biome?.terrain?.undergroundBlock ??
            "dirt";

        const blocks = [];

        for (
            let y = 0;
            y <= height && y < this.worldHeight;
            y++
        ) {

            let block;

            if (y === height) {

                block = surfaceBlock;

            } else if (
                y >= height - 3
            ) {

                block = undergroundBlock;

            } else {

                block = "stone";

            }

            blocks.push({

                x: worldX,

                y,

                z: worldZ,

                block

            });

        }

        return {

            height,

            biomeId:
                biome?.id ?? "plains",

            blocks

        };

    }

    generateChunk(chunkX, chunkZ) {

        const columns = [];

        for (
            let localX = 0;
            localX < this.chunkSize;
            localX++
        ) {

            for (
                let localZ = 0;
                localZ < this.chunkSize;
                localZ++
            ) {

                const worldX =
                    chunkX *
                    this.chunkSize +
                    localX;

                const worldZ =
                    chunkZ *
                    this.chunkSize +
                    localZ;

                columns.push(
                    this.generateColumn(
                        worldX,
                        worldZ
                    )
                );

            }

        }

        return {

            x: chunkX,

            z: chunkZ,

            size: this.chunkSize,

            columns

        };

    }

}

export class ChestSpawn {

    constructor(data = {}) {

        this.id =
            data.id ?? null;

        this.position = {

            x: Number(data.position?.x ?? data.x ?? 0),

            y: Number(data.position?.y ?? data.y ?? 0),

            z: Number(data.position?.z ?? data.z ?? 0)

        };

        this.lootTable =
            data.lootTable ??
            "common_chest";

        this.respawnDelay =
            Number(
                data.respawnDelay ?? 300000
            );

        this.enabled =
            data.enabled !== false;

    }

    createChestData() {

        return {

            id: this.id,

            position: {
                ...this.position
            },

            lootTable:
                this.lootTable,

            respawnDelay:
                this.respawnDelay

        };

    }

    isAvailable() {

        return this.enabled;

    }

    serialize() {

        return {

            id: this.id,

            position: {
                ...this.position
            },

            lootTable:
                this.lootTable,

            respawnDelay:
                this.respawnDelay,

            enabled:
                this.enabled

        };

    }

export class DynamicSpawnManager {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.spawns = new Map();

        this.maxSpawns =
            Number(options.maxSpawns ?? 100);

        this.nextId = 1;

    }

    generateId(prefix = "spawn") {

        return `${prefix}_${this.nextId++}`;

    }

    add(position, options = {}) {

        if (!position) {

            return null;

        }

        if (
            this.spawns.size >=
            this.maxSpawns
        ) {

            return null;

        }

        const id =
            options.id ??
            this.generateId();

        const spawn = {

            id,

            position: {

                x: Number(position.x ?? 0),

                y: Number(position.y ?? 0),

                z: Number(position.z ?? 0)

            },

            type:
                options.type ?? "dynamic",

            createdAt:
                Date.now(),

            expiresAt:
                options.duration
                    ? Date.now() +
                      Number(options.duration)
                    : 0

        };

        this.spawns.set(
            id,
            spawn
        );

        return spawn;

    }

    get(id) {

        return this.spawns.get(id) ?? null;

    }

    remove(id) {

        return this.spawns.delete(id);

    }

    update(timestamp = Date.now()) {

        let removed = 0;

        for (const [
            id,
            spawn
        ] of this.spawns) {

            if (
                spawn.expiresAt > 0 &&
                timestamp >= spawn.expiresAt
            ) {

                this.spawns.delete(id);

                removed++;

            }

        }

        return removed;

    }

    getAll() {

        return [
            ...this.spawns.values()
        ];

    }

    clear() {

        this.spawns.clear();

    }

}

export class PlayerSpawn {

        };

            x: Number(data.rotation?.x ?? 0),

            y: Number(data.rotation?.y ?? 0),

            z: Number(data.rotation?.z ?? 0)

        };

        this.priority =
            Number(data.priority ?? 0);

        this.enabled =
            data.enabled !== false;

    }

        return this.enabled;

    }

        return {
            ...this.position
        };

    }

    getRotation() {

        return {
            ...this.rotation
        };

    }

    serialize() {

        return {

            id: this.id,

            position: this.getPosition(),

import { PlayerSpawn } from "./PlayerSpawn.js";
import { ChestSpawn } from "./ChestSpawn.js";
import { DynamicSpawnManager } from "./DynamicSpawnManager.js";

export class SpawnManager {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.playerSpawns = new Map();

        this.chestSpawns = new Map();

        this.dynamic =
            options.dynamicSpawnManager ??
            new DynamicSpawnManager(options);

        this.defaultPlayerSpawn =
            new PlayerSpawn({

                id: "default",

                x: options.x ?? 0,

                y: options.y ?? 5,

                z: options.z ?? 0

            });

        this.registerPlayerSpawn(
            this.defaultPlayerSpawn
        );

    }

    initialize(server) {

        this.server = server;

        return this;

    }

    registerPlayerSpawn(spawn) {

        const playerSpawn =
            spawn instanceof PlayerSpawn
                ? spawn
                : new PlayerSpawn(spawn);

        this.playerSpawns.set(
            playerSpawn.id,
            playerSpawn
        );

        return playerSpawn;

    }

    registerChestSpawn(spawn) {

        const chestSpawn =
            spawn instanceof ChestSpawn
                ? spawn
                : new ChestSpawn(spawn);

        if (!chestSpawn.id) {

            throw new Error(
                "Un identifiant de coffre est requis."
            );

        }

        this.chestSpawns.set(
            chestSpawn.id,
            chestSpawn
        );

        return chestSpawn;

    }

    getPlayerSpawn(id = null) {

        if (
            id &&
            this.playerSpawns.has(id)
        ) {

            return this.playerSpawns.get(id);

        }

        const available =
            [
                ...this.playerSpawns.values()
            ]
            .filter(
                spawn =>
                    spawn.isAvailable()
            )
            .sort(
                (a, b) =>
                    b.priority -
                    a.priority
            );

        return (
            available[0] ??
            this.defaultPlayerSpawn
        );

    }

    getChestSpawn(id) {

        return (
            this.chestSpawns.get(id) ??
            null
        );

    }

    spawnPlayer(player, spawnId = null) {

        if (!player) {

            return null;

        }

        const spawn =
            this.getPlayerSpawn(spawnId);

        player.setPosition(

            spawn.position.x,

            spawn.position.y,

            spawn.position.z

        );

        player.setRotation(

            spawn.rotation.x,

            spawn.rotation.y,

            spawn.rotation.z

        );

        return spawn;

    }

    createDynamicSpawn(
        position,
        options = {}
    ) {

        return this.dynamic.add(
            position,
            options
        );

    }

    update(deltaTime, tick) {

        return this.dynamic.update();

    }

    getPlayerSpawns() {

        return [
            ...this.playerSpawns.values()
        ];

    }

    getChestSpawns() {

        return [
            ...this.chestSpawns.values()
        ];

    }

    clear() {

        this.playerSpawns.clear();

        this.chestSpawns.clear();

        this.dynamic.clear();

        this.registerPlayerSpawn(
            this.defaultPlayerSpawn
        );

    }

}
